# Changelog

All notable changes to this project will be documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.0.0/),
adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html),
and is generated by [Changie](https://github.com/miniscruff/changie).


## [v0.35.0] - 2026-10-23
### Changed
* Replace the bundled card scans with 900px copies, cutting the card art from 828 MB to 552 MB.
### Fixed
* A treachery that makes the villain scheme or attack during the reveal step (Dark Designs) now shows that activation's boost card and breakdown on the villain phase panel, as the villain's own activation does, instead of only the treachery (#370, reported by @zackburnley).
* The villain phase's interrupt and response windows say what is about to happen, such as how much threat is about to go on which scheme when Great Responsibility can step in (#371, reported by @zackburnley).
### Docs
* Drop a retired card-scan download command and its source notes; the scans themselves are unchanged.

## [v0.34.0] - 2026-10-23
### Added
* Send to another device: Settings → Save data can send your saves to another phone, tablet or computer with a 12-character code or a QR code, encrypted on your device and gone after one use or 15 minutes (#276).
* Sync across devices: keep saved games, decks, campaigns and unlocks in step on your phone, tablet and computer with no login, set up once with a recovery code and linked by QR or a short code (#276).
* The Shadowland campaign: three scenarios in order, Dark Protégé villains checked off, Creeping Corruption and OVERPOWERING records, two recorded support or upgrade slots, and the expert persistent-damage rules.
* Creeping Corruption begins in play, one per player, in a standalone game with Black Flower as a modular set.
* Shadowland's setup screens: a random Dark Protégé villain (or a labelled house pick), Shadow Labyrinth's hidden set-aside draw, the trap deck and its discard, and the campaign's briefing and dossier.
* Shadowland (MC65): its three scenarios, seven encounter sets and campaign are playable, unlocked with Fear No Evil's Agents of S.H.I.E.L.D. gate.
* Extras has a Side Stories tab. Its first comic, Thor and She-Hulk's date from Avengers #11, opens with the "Worthy of a Second Date?" achievement and reads panel by panel in the comic reader, each page ending on the whole page.
* Shadowland art goes live: a campaign cover, panel openers, Marvel's 2010 Shadowland comic pages between issues (credited on screen), scenario tile pictures, and earned Extras artwork.
* Shadowland: every card shows its own picture; full-size card images fill the faces the Hall of Heroes previews lacked.
### Changed
* Several games can be in progress at once: the title screen lists them with the device each was last played on, you can resume any of them or remove one, and starting a new game no longer retires the others (#276).
* On a phone held upright, the Inspect sheet shows the card scan at the full width of the screen, with its text and play history below (#358, thanks @zackburnley)
* With two or three villains (Tower Defense, the Sinister Six's opening pair), each villain is drawn as a full card panel with its stats and hit points, and an environment such as Avengers Tower gets a card-sized tile. An environment's damage shows as a large "1/9 DAMAGE" chip on every screen size (#356)
* The table-setup screen jumps (`screen=table-setup` and the screens after it) take `&seed=<n>` for a fixed deal, and the Shadow King setup spec uses it, so it no longer fails now and then when the hero loses before round 2.
* Shadowland expert campaign: each player's recorded hit points are set after the scenario's own setup (RRG Appendix II step 13), so setup damage no longer carries over.
* Swap 500 more card scans for sharper copies, most going from about 840px to 1030px tall
### Fixed
* An effect that puts a "Hero form only" or "Alter-Ego form only" card into play no longer does so for a player in the other form; the card stays where it was (RRG p. 32)
* An effect that removes an acceleration token can no longer take one from the main scheme; it removes only a token placed on another card (RRG p. 5)
* An "after [enemy] attacks and damages" or "attacks and defeats" ability now resolves once for each character the attack damages or defeats, so overkill damage spilled onto an identity or villain triggers it again (Jigsaw, Toad, Cypher, Captain Marvel; ruling January 11, 2026 (2), RRG p. 31)
* Any player can now use the Hero Action of Obedience Potion and The Poison (RRG 1.8 p. 66 errata)
* A card whose text box is blank (Hope Summers treated as a minion) still loses the game when it leaves play: alternate win or loss conditions cannot be blanked (RRG p. 48; ruling Nov 29, 2023)
* "Attacks and damages you" (Green Goblin, Tombstone, the Sinister Six, Beetle, Gamora, Space Pirates, Power Gauntlets, Ionic Boots) now need your identity damaged, not a defending ally, "You'll Pay for That!" counts only your identity's damage and is not offered when your identity took none, and Defiance cannot be played while your ally defends (RRG p. 43, p. 49; FFG rulings Jul 18, 2024 and Mar 15, 2024)
* A sustained-damage cap such as Boarding Party lowers damage already above it as soon as it is in effect again, e.g. after a blank ends (FFG ruling Oct 10, 2025)
* Revealing another player's obligation while that player is in a different game area (Kang stage 2B) now removes it from the game and reveals a replacement card (FFG ruling Sep 21, 2023; RRG 1.8 p. 30)
* Bulldozer's Helmet resolves once per attack and discards 1 card per point of damage the whole attack dealt, overkill onto your hero included; Major Domo and Head of Steam count overkill damage too, and all three count damage a tough status card stopped, since it was dealt (RRG 1.8 p. 14, p. 31, p. 49; rulings Jan 11, 2026 (2) and Jan 17, 2026 (3))
* Battery Pack moves its charge counter rather than removing and adding one, so it arrives as the type the Tech card defines (a reflection counter on Energy Barrier), per RRG p. 6 and the Battery Pack FAQ on p. 62
* Tower Defense: threat moved to "the main scheme" (Manipulated M.U.S.I.C.) goes only to the main scheme with Focused Defense (ruling Oct 10, 2025)
* Beat Cop moving threat off Show of Empathy puts the token on the non-Elite minion instead of also on Beat Cop (ruling Nov 21, 2025; RRG p. 30)
* A card that declares a character the defender no longer replaces a character already defending that attack (RRG 1.8 p. 15; FFG ruling Jul 19, 2023)
* An alter-ego is no longer "the character with the highest ATK" (Restrained), whether the card attaches to it or targets it: an alter-ego has no ATK, THW or DEF and a hero no REC (ruling April 17, 2025). Allies, minions and villains are still ranked by every stat
* An enemy attack moved onto a minion engaged with a player (Sinister Ends onto Hope Summers) now attacks that minion, and a player can still defend (ruling July 25, 2025)
* "Discard the lowest-cost upgrade you control" and other highest/lowest-cost removals skip a permanent card and take the next card that fits (Beetle, Speed Demon, Oversized Hands; RRG p. 32)
* Vapors of Valtorr can no longer target a Stalwart enemy to replace its tough status card, since it cannot be stunned or confused (FFG ruling Jun 20, 2024, Ruling 215; RRG p. 43)
* Replacing a status card with Vapors of Valtorr no longer counts as placing one, so Mister Sinister's Forced Response does not place threat (FFG ruling May 10, 2024, #181)
* With several main schemes in play (Venom Goblin), Cable, Montage and the Venom ally count the main scheme best for their controller each time, as the player may choose it (RRG 1.8 p. 63; FFG rulings of Dec 22, 2023 and May 16, 2024). In Tower Defense the Venom ally now reads the scheme with Focused Defense, as a player card's constant does (MC21 p. 10)
* Determination (22016) now asks which main scheme to remove threat from when several are in play (Venom Goblin, Tower Defense), as "the main scheme" on a player card lets its controller choose (MC27 insert, Venom Goblin FAQ; MC21 insert p. 10 for Tower Defense).
* A screen redrawn while a scrolled list is still gliding (the campaign market, once its card pictures load) no longer throws on the old list's scrollbar
* Mutant Protectors still exhausts its ally when another character is already defending the attack; the ally just cannot defend (RRG "And", p. 7; FFG ruling July 19, 2023)
* A card that discards your highest- or lowest-cost card and surges or offers another choice when you cannot (Inconspicuous Box, Oversized Hands, Breakthrough, Greycrow, Inconspicuous, Burn Notice) now treats a permanent card as nothing to discard (RRG p. 32)
* Arm Cannon's cost (discard the highest-cost upgrade you control) skips a permanent upgrade it cannot remove and takes the next-highest one (RRG p. 32)
* Exporting save data in the desktop app opens a Save dialog in Downloads instead of the share sheet.
* With reduced motion on (or battery saver, which turns it on), the villain phase stops on each facedown encounter card for Flip Encounter Card and on each revealed card for Next card again, instead of jumping straight to the recap (#342, reported by @zackburnley).
* Cards in a decision sheet open the card hover popover as soon as the sheet opens, not only after something redraws it.
* Optic Blast can be paid by Ruby Quartz Visor alone with an empty hand (#349, reported by @Lemantia).
* Defiance is offered while the boost card is still facedown, before it is turned up, as its "would be turned faceup" text asks (#348, reported by @Lemantia).
* Webbed Up replaces any attack your hero would make, an attack event or ability included, not only a basic attack (#346, reported by @Lemantia).
* Black Widow (54003), Jack Flag, the Flying and Psionic Inhuman allies and Passion for Justice are offered in alter-ego form too: their text says Response or Interrupt, not Hero Response or Hero Interrupt.
* Turn the Tide is offered when your hero thwarts the main scheme down to 0 threat, not only a side scheme.
* Pressing HIDE on the villain phase before the replay reaches your decision no longer leaves that decision hidden: the rail comes back for it, so an interrupt or a defense is not missed.
* Scarlet Spider and Spider-Tingle ("when you would reveal an encounter card") are offered while the card is still facedown, and Spider-Tingle only on your own reveals.
* A card in hand whose response only works once it is in play (Indomitable and other upgrades, supports and allies) is tagged Your turn during the villain phase, not When hit.
* The game log says when a card ability readies a character (Indomitable readying your hero), not only the cost it paid.
* Black Swan, Defensive Protocols, Retrieve Odin's Armor, Jormungand, Avengers Tower's damaged side, Odin's King side and the Norn Stone's flip side show their card art in The Mad Titan's Shadow campaign (#356)
* The attack target picker says which hero each minion is engaged with and its hit points, and lists targets in the same order as the villain area, so facedown Ultron Drones can be told apart (#357)
* Replace 1143 old card scans that still carried a faint FFG preview watermark, mostly Core Set, Red Skull, Wrecking Crew, Green Goblin and the early hero packs, with clean, sharper copies
* Green Goblin II (Risky Business) deals 4 indirect damage to each player when revealed, as printed, not 3 (#361, reported by @Perezh21).
* On a phone, a swipe across the table that starts on a card no longer opens Inspect on that card when the finger lifts
* Thwarting The Red House asks whether to use THW or ATK, so a character can thwart it with ATK as the card allows (#365, reported by @AlecHolland1582).
* On a phone, the Pause menu's ✕ closes it again after the settings rows are scrolled down; the same hidden-row leak is fixed on How to play, What's new in a box, aspect lessons, the guide panel, Inspect, compact Table setup and the saved-games list.
* Team-Up rings on a phone show the pair's picture instead of a black circle.
* A button half scrolled under a screen's header or footer (an aspect lesson's Try it, a Pause menu row) answers a tap on the part that shows again.
### Engine
* Scenario setup can choose its villain among the villain cards of a set-aside set (seeded random or picked), shuffling in that villain's linked cards and recording it as the chosen villain.
* A choice's options can carry a tag: a card can interrupt when a player chooses a tagged option, a constant can stop a player choosing one, and naming an encounter card type offers only encounter types.
* Card abilities can check which side of the villain is faceup.
* A main scheme's next stage counts as entering play when the scheme advances, so it no longer answers a thwart (or anything else) that happened against the stage before it.
* A set-aside villain card can be put into play as an Elite minion by the card attached to it, keeping its own text; when that card leaves it stays in play as a villain, a two-sided villain card discarded as a minion is removed from the game, and finding a villain searches every deck and discard pile or reveals one already in play.
* A villain flipping to its other side no longer gets a tough status card from Toughness; a new villain stage still does, and Typhoid Mary's flip (MC60 FAQ) and Shadow Labyrinth's expert side B reveal keep it.
### Docs
* Rule comments cite the FFG rulings issued after March 2025 by date, as Hall of Heroes lists them, instead of a made-up "69.NN" number
* The v0.33.0 release notes' Five reporters, six fixes images live in docs/screenshots/releases/v0.33.0/.
* The README roadmap and current status now say every released product through Shadowland is playable end to end, with online multiplayer next.
* The v0.34.0 release notes' Into Shadowland images live in docs/screenshots/releases/v0.34.0/.

## [v0.33.0] - 2026-10-21
### Added
* Every pull request now builds an Android preview APK next to its Netlify preview, signed with the release key so testers can install it over the app, and links it in a PR comment (#328)
### Fixed
* Organizational Support's payment strip shows one tile per S.H.I.E.L.D. card it can exhaust, which you tap to build the set, instead of one tile for every combination (#326).
* Scrolling menus and the hand no longer lag behind the finger on high-resolution phones at Native or 720p: rectangular clips now use the GPU's scissor test instead of three full-screen passes a frame (#328)
* Peek at table's banner is now one slim band with a Move button that puts it at the top or the bottom of the table, so it no longer sits on the main scheme or the first enemy on a phone (#329)
* Thwart events like Multitasking can no longer be played while you cannot thwart (Baron Zemo engaged with you), and a move whose target choice found nothing can be undone again (#330)
* On a phone, a swipe of the play area that lifts over its ‹ › arrows no longer pages the row, and a drag that starts anywhere in the play area (or the phone's hand strip) scrolls it, not only one that starts on a card.
* The game no longer repaints a frame that looks the same as the last one, and draws without 4x multisampling at 2x density and up, so a high-resolution phone's GPU isn't kept busy while you read or scroll (#328)
* Make the Call now defeats an Ant-Man or Wasp ally it puts into play at 0 hit points, and resources overpaid for their cost through Make the Call now give them pym counters (#335).

## [v0.32.1] - 2026-10-21
### Fixed
* Wasp in Giant form can divide her basic attack among enemies (and her basic thwart among schemes): Attack opens a picker where each tap places one point, then Confirm (#322, reported by @AlecHolland1582).
### Docs
* The v0.32.0 release notes' Sharper on every screen images live in docs/screenshots/releases/v0.32.0/.

## [v0.32.0] - 2026-10-21
### Added
* Android Settings > Display > Screen: Resolution (Native down to 1x) and Frame rate (match the screen's refresh rate, 60 or 30 fps).
* Android picks Resolution and Frame rate defaults from the phone's hardware (memory, GPU, Android's performance class), and Settings says what it recommends.
### Fixed
* Gjallerbru and Hall of Nastrond (Hela) show their own card pictures instead of each other's (#314)
* The villain phase no longer stops on an encounter card after Flip Encounter Card on a phone in power saving mode, where the card stayed marked facedown with no Next card button (#315).
* The Android app draws at the phone's own resolution instead of stretching a 1x picture, so a 3x phone is sharp.
* The web and the desktop app draw at the display's own scale (Windows scaling, 4K, Retina) instead of a stretched 1x picture, with the same Render resolution and Frame rate settings.
### Docs
* Added Markdown copies of the post-RRG 1.6 FFG rulings (July 2024 to December 2025, split by month under docs/rulings/post-rrg-1-6/) and of the v1.6 Rules Reference.

## [v0.31.0] - 2026-10-21
### Added
* Daredevil and Echo, the Fear No Evil heroes, are fully scripted with their starter decks
* Fear No Evil campaign is playable in the client: choose each scenario from the progress draw, set the villain order, and Daredevil and Echo's starter decks unlock with the box after the Agents of S.H.I.E.L.D. campaign.
* Earned item: Hall of Heroes' Ultron Drone art, earned with the Drone On achievement (win a game against Ultron). Facedown Drones then show a Drone card with their ATK; turn it on or off in Settings > More > Earned items (#291).
* A hidden-hand facedown pick shows each option as a player card back under a "Choose cards from X's hand" title, selectable but never hovered or inspected.
* Fear No Evil's six scenarios (with a villain picker at setup), Jessica Jones and Luke Cage's starter decks, and their art join the pool.
* Achievements model: 232 achievements across wins, villains, campaigns, heroes, Expert, feats, team-ups and funny losses, derived from the save history, with lifetime champion points and nine ranks (screens to follow).
* Trophy Room: every achievement with its progress under a rank header, reached from the Title and Extras; achievement points add to your champion points.
* Title rank badge (rank, lifetime points, bar to the next rank) that opens the Trophy Room, with a Rank up banner and sound on a new rank; Extras can unlock on an achievement.
* A finished game's save now records a small summary (loss reason, heroes defeated, winners' hit points, allies played), so the Respawn Pending, Read the Room, Cutting It Close, I Work Alone and Gamma-Ray Overdose achievements count from new games on.
* Art & Credits screen (from Extras): who made the card scans, art, comic pages, music and tools, under the fan-project notice.
* Scenario select shows villain art for the five Fear No Evil scheme scenarios and the She-Hulk and Vision leaders, from FFG's announcement art (via Hall of Heroes), instead of a card scan.
* An "Achievement unlocked" popup with its own sound when you earn an achievement; Settings > More > Achievements sets how long it stays up (or turns it off) and where it shows.
* Settings > More > Achievements has a Popup size (Small, Medium, Large), and the popup's trophy badge now fits the MC logo inside the cup.
* New achievement "Studying Like Doctor Strange" for finishing all of How to Play (every basics lesson and every aspect's Try it).
* Achievements for playing a game as each hero, each named for the hero's line ("Another!", "I Am Iron Man", "Wakanda Forever!"), and "Sometimes You Gotta Run Before You Can Walk" for turning on Unlock everything.
* Achievement "I Love You 3000" for dealing the final blow to Thanos as Iron Man; a finished game's save now records whose card landed the last blow.
* Game Over shows the Fear No Evil villain you faced winning or losing (Hammerhead, Bullseye, Purple Man, Typhoid Mary) and Kingpin defeated; Kingpin's transfer is in Extras.
* Winning the Kingpin finale with Daredevil at the table shows Kingpin's transfer with Matt Murdock on Game Over.
* Achievements for deck building (Some Assembly Required, Taking It for a Spin, Mark II), for finishing a game with one to four seats filled, and Better Together for your first Team-Up card.
### Changed
* Under 'Pool-ized, Lady Deadpool's and Dogpool's own When Defeated no longer resolve: the card's printed text gives the attached ally a blank text box.
* Fear No Evil: on a first playthrough the group chooses before each scenario whether to take the next recommended villain or a random one (MC60 p. 9 step 5b).
* An encounter card's option can be chosen unless it needs a target and has none (RRG 1.8 'Choose (Option)', p. 12; owner revision of wave 7 Q8 and wave 9 Q33): spends are offered even when the player can't pay; targets a status can't affect don't count.
* Rewind after a Fear No Evil defeat shows the log marks it keeps and sends you back to Campaign Setup, instead of claiming the log is restored.
* The seat picker's TEAM-UP tag reads in white on its dark plate.
* The Fear No Evil briefing explains its first-playthrough and villain questions in plain words and says why Open issue is greyed (Answer the question on this page to open Issue #1).
* On a phone the Title's rank badge is a full-width strip above the footer (cards live, release notes) instead of beside the logo.
* Fear No Evil briefing: the Handled for you rows read as plain sentences (Hammerhead is the villain for Art Museum Heist), and the scenario pick says which scenarios are open.
* Several achievements at once now stack into one popup you can tap open to see them all, with Collapse and Dismiss; newly opened Extras show in the same popup right after.
* The Extras popup wears a note for new songs and a star for other Extras instead of the achievement trophy; the Art & Credits screen now credits the soundtrack (Google Gemini), the sound effects and the Shadowland #5 panels.
* The achievement popup now defaults to the title bar at Small size, and its opened list grows out of the popup and shrinks back into it.
### Fixed
* "After you play X from your hand" responses no longer answer a card an effect plays from the deck (Fetch Quest, Shuri's Inventor); plays "as if it were in your hand" still answer them (RRG 1.8 Magik FAQ, p. 64)
* Maggia Enforcers (60092) prints a star in its SCH box, not a dash: the card data now emits SCH 0 so its text can set the value, like Purple Man.
* The card ingest numbers an infinite-hit-point back face of a single-stage villain as stage 2 (FFG ruling Sep 5, 2024: the printed numeral), so Hela's Wounded faces keep their fix and The Collector's and the Loki Avatars' back faces read stage 2.
* Effects where each player picks from a shared pile (Zola, Hydra Patrol, Kingpin's Underlings, The Raft's Prisoners, set-aside allies and upgrades, and others) now let the first player choose the order players resolve in (RRG 1.8 'Each Player', p. 17).
* Sanctuary (Thanos) deals its damage as one instance after every player has spent, so Thanos's Armor reduces it once.
* Zeal for the Cause checks whether you are engaged with an Acolyte minion, as printed.
* Thanos's Armor reduces damage as a Forced Interrupt, so a tough status card is used first (RRG 1.8 Appendix III).
* When an encounter card picks one of several eligible cards without printing 'choose' (including 'a card you control' and ties on highest/lowest), the first player now picks (RRG 1.8 'First Player', p. 19); obligations and Peril stay with their player.
* A flipped double-sided encounter attachment applies its showing face's printed stat modifiers (Vision's Dense and Intangible, Hope's Captor).
* Ties on 'the villain/scheme with the most or least threat' (Wrecking Crew, Crossfire) are broken by the first player, and a tied side-scheme pick no longer puts threat on every tied scheme.
* The Civil War builder lays out three leaders per side and five stage options, so it opens with Synthezoid Smackdown's leaders; Random can draw them.
* Metal Bracer reduces damage as it would be dealt, before tough (FFG ruling Aug 26, 2026); Kingpin keeps his protections as an ally under Karma (Sep 10); King's Gambit shuffles even with an empty discard pile (Sep 10).
* 'When a card is revealed from the encounter deck' (Black Widow, Enhanced Spider-Sense and 12 more) no longer hears reveals from under a card, the set-aside area or a search (FFG ruling Sep 14, 2026); Return the Favor's treachery still counts as from the encounter deck.
* Encounter options that stun or confuse a fixed identity are no longer offered when that identity can't take the status (RRG p. 42), and Deadly Contraptions' Disarm options are offered whenever their discard can resolve.
* An Ultron Drone that attacks and is then defeated in the same villain phase is still called a Drone in the log and the round recap, instead of naming the card underneath (#291).
* Regression test: a deck left under 40 cards when Fear No Evil removes a recorded title still blocks the next game, and the removed title cannot be added back.
* Fear No Evil's issues shelf shows the five open scenarios with their villain art (Kingpin sealed until they are done), and its cover offers Read issue #1 instead of a disabled Campaign over.
* Hero art can name where the hero is in the picture (focus.json), so seat tiles, hero cards and avatars crop around the hero instead of the middle; Daredevil and nine other off-center pictures are set.
* Card scans whose white text boxes had turned see-through (123 scans, mostly Fear No Evil, Jessica Jones, Luke Cage and Shadowland) are solid again; Photographic Reflexes uses fresh scans.
* Side decks beside the play area (Daredevil's Sense deck, Doctor Strange's Invocation deck) are portrait cards at the play area's card height, and the yellow team-up tag no longer clips or hides behind the Act button.
* fetch_card_art.py's trim no longer turns white text boxes see-through (with a --self-test and an interior-transparency check in audit), and the 25 more scans it flagged are solid again.
* The defend picker's health line wraps instead of being cut off when four defenders are offered.
* A player side scheme in your hand is drawn sideways, showing the whole card, instead of cropped into a portrait slot.
* Fear No Evil now plays an issue's comic after you pick that issue on the Briefing (it was skipped because the issue is chosen there), and numbers issues in the order you play them.
* Coming back to a Fear No Evil issue left on the table (after a refresh or Back to title) now reads its comic and continues that game, instead of landing on the Dossier or dealing a new game.
* An encounter card dealt facedown stays a card back on the table until the villain phase reveals it, and the table's log waits for the phase too (issue #311).
* A Fear No Evil issue is played against the villain the Briefing recorded instead of always Hammerhead, and Protection Racket opens with one main scheme per seat.
### Engine
* Daredevil's Sense deck keeps its order: a fixed-order separate deck is shuffled only at setup, and only its owner may look through it
* A card can be played from a separate deck (its top card, or any card as a search), and a play that ignores the resource cost can put an upgrade on a host the player chooses
* Scripting registries for Jessica Jones and Luke Cage, with five reprints playable through their originals' scripts
* A card can go to the bottom of its owner's separate deck instead of leaving play, alone or as an attachment leaving with its host
* Unpreventable damage (Luke Cage): it ignores tough status cards and damage reductions and preventions; increases, redirects, caps and "cannot take damage" still apply
* An interrupt can answer the removal of a scheme's last threat, before the threat is removed and before the scheme is defeated
* A scheme's threat can be limited to removal by one identity and its events and upgrades (Focus the Senses), and an event is the playing player's, not its owner's
* The Starting keyword: before drawing the starting hand a player may take their Starting card, which counts toward hand size, and a deck holds at most one Starting card
* Exactly defeating a side scheme: a thwart that removes no more threat than the scheme holds
* A card tucked under another can be played as if it were in hand, on every play-from-hand route
* A card can let you play a tucked event as if from hand by discarding itself from your hand (Photographic Reflexes), and a card with a star cost is never played
* A character can use one stat in place of others (The Best Offense: DEF for THW and ATK, ignoring their modifiers), and an upgrade can say it attaches to minions or side schemes
* After a defense card redirects an enemy attack, the defending player declares the defender; and a card can remove any acceleration token in play
* A card can skip the next villain phase (the round still ends), and round-end abilities now trigger on the round's end even when the villain phase is skipped
* Lasting effects that end with the player phase, and a stat equal to the highest among the other characters (Purple Man)
* "Attach to a card" upgrades, end-of-thwart effects that read whether the thwart defeated the scheme, a hit points value, a check that a card in hand can be played, and Take a Stand's bonus only inside a player phase
* A cost can choose a card in play, and a counter cost's amount can be read from the board after that choice (4K Digital Camcorder); a deck play "as if in hand" counts as from hand (Calling in Favors)
* A basic thwart made on a card's instruction can be the hero's own, made without exhausting and ignoring patrol and crisis (Grapnel Launcher); a damage reduction can be read live, such as your hero's DEF (Echo)
* Attacks against a character can gain a keyword and deal extra damage (Cruisin' for a Bruisin'), and Prerequisite is enforced when a card is played (Defend Our City)
* A query for cards that print a Boost ability, and a search for allies named on a Team-Up card (Sidewinder, Dynamic Duo)
* An ability cost can change your form (Entrapment), paid together with any additional cost to change form, and player side schemes are matched by card type
* Change-form costs no longer depend on remembered objects; `changeFormCost` is refused on resource abilities, and the client labels it "change to hero form".
* `reduceCostToPlay(query, n, player?)`: a play-cost rule that can last until the end of the phase (Defend Our City).
* Attachment hosts honour 'without a [trait] attachment' (withoutAttachmentTrait), so a Vehicle skips an enemy that already has one.
* 'Cannot be dealt damage' (cannotTakeDamage.stage: "dealt"): the damage is never dealt, so no 'would be dealt' interrupt, tough or piercing, and no 'after ... dealt' response; logged and shown as damageNotDealt.
* A main scheme per player in their own play area (Protection Racket): setup picks or draws one per seat, 'the main scheme' is read by play area, enemies scheme onto the activated player's scheme, acceleration tokens go near the villain and add to each, and an anyPlayerEliminated predicate.
* attacksLoseKeywords can be scoped to the attacker ("Bullseye's attacks lose ranged"), so the loss beats any grant of the keyword; the defend preview reads ranged the way the attack does.
* Main scheme swap (Change of Venue): swapCards exchanges a main scheme in play with a set-aside one in the same play area at 1B (attachments discarded, the old one set aside), optional placePreviousThreat, a 'your main scheme' target; logged mainSchemeSwapped.
* A villain can start on a random side (setVillainSide, drawn from the seed; Disturbed Psyche), encounter cards' Setup abilities resolve in setup step 12, and a villain face revealed by a flip gets its toughness (MC60 p. 26 FAQ).
* gainsAbility can grant a Boost ability to boost cards (TargetQuery.boostCard, DSL boostCardsFromSet), resolved after the card's printed Boost abilities when it is turned faceup (Purple Man's set).
* Add an eventKindIs predicate, so one ability that answers several kinds of event can do something different for each; replacement threat placements and removals keep the original's source and the thwart or activation they belong to; EventPattern.removalWillApply ignores removals that will be stopped.
* A minion can hold threat and be treated as an ally by its own constant while a condition holds (treatSelfAsAlly, takeControl; Black Cat 60169); a bare 'when threat is placed' interrupt now hears only threat placed on schemes.
* Black Cat's ally treatment holds at exactly 4 threat (owner Q205 = B): a 5th threat turns her back into a minion.
* Each ally a query matches can be treated as a minion while it matches (treatAsMinion: Baron Zemo's 'each ally with threat on it'), with SCH equal to its base THW; it is an ally again under its owner's control, keeping its threat, once the query stops matching or the card leaves play or is blanked.
* Encounter minions can be tucked under a side scheme and revealed from there: a response can hear a card revealed from the encounter deck only, and a card's own When Defeated can tuck it under another card in place of its discard.
* Fear No Evil scenario builder wave11Scenario: the main scheme is the scenario and the villain a setup option (villainSetId), offered to setup screens as PlayableScenarioOffer.villains; per-player main scheme picks pass through as mainSchemeChoice.
* Campaign runner: a choice campaign's finale is reached only through its finale.when (MC60 p. 9 step 4); nodeProgress values; choices over Completed scenarios; removal from the campaign by title and subtitle (MC60 p. 9 step 8); cards removed from the campaign are never set aside.
* Villainous works for allies and heroes (RRG 1.8 p. 47; MC60 p. 26 FAQ, Black Cat 60169): a basic power is given a facedown boost card, turned faceup as the power resolves and heard by unqualified 'boost card turned faceup' cards; its Boost resolves for its controller, its icons go to the stat used (following any swap), and it is discarded once the power has resolved. An ally's defense gets no card.
* putIntoPlay can resolve the Setup abilities of the cards it puts into play (resolveSetup; the first player orders two or more, RRG p. 40) and put a double-sided card into play on its other face without flipping it (otherFace, logged cardEnteredOnOtherFace), for Fear No Evil's campaign environments.
* Civil War leaders choose main scheme stages from every stage of their side in either box (owner question SS1 open, built A); a gated box's stages are not offered.
* An encounter attachment attached by its attach instruction enters play once, so its Uses counters are placed once (Bear Hug starts with 3 grip counters, not 6).
* A 'would be given a facedown boost card' interrupt window (boostCardBeingGiven, on.boostCardWouldBeGiven) lets an ability replace the give of a boost card; the setAside card selector takes random, picked from the seeded RNG.
* A When Defeated or When Revealed ability a card gains now resolves: game-triggered abilities read the card's live abilities, printed and gained, so a blank text box keeps a gained one and an ignore-When-Defeated effect stops it (RRG 1.8 'Gains', p. 21).
* An attack's characterAttacked event records who the target was engaged with as the attack was made, so 'after … is attacked' patterns still see a target the attack defeated (Battle in the Bodega, owner Q208 open, built A); a main scheme swap compares faceup 1B titles (RRG 1.8 pp. 23, 42).
* A flipped double-sided ally uses its other face's printed THW, ATK and hit points (RRG 1.8 'Flip', p. 20): Bloody Mary, and Odin's King side.
* forEachPlayer can let the first player choose the order players resolve it in (order: firstPlayerChooses, DSL forEachPlayerInChosenOrder; RRG 1.8 'Each Player', p. 17); inEncounterSet also matches a support or ally printed as belonging to an encounter set (specificTo).
* forEachPlayer can bind its passes' results (bind): each player's vars and chosen cards are summed or joined back to the ability once every player has resolved.
* Campaign loss policy keepLog: a lost scenario keeps the campaign log and returns to the campaign's scenario choice instead of restoring the log and replaying the same scenario.
* resolveSetups effect: resolve the Setup abilities of cards already in play together, the first player choosing the order (RRG 1.8 'Simultaneous Resolution', p. 40). Campaign progress marks stop at the fail threshold.
* An ability counts as resolved only if one of its effects changed the game state (RRG 1.8 'Resolve', p. 37): Teenage Superheroes, Mighty Avengers, The Initiative, Freedom Fighters, Citywide Crisis, Elaborate Trap and Black Widow's Preparations now see a no-op as unresolved.
* A card cancelled as it is played (Spellcasting, Counterspell) is discarded before it enters play, so its own enters-play and after-played abilities never fire; it still counts as played for other cards (RRG 1.8 'Initiating Abilities', 'Cancel', p. 11).
* An ability whose effects change nothing no longer counts as resolved for 'resolved this way' or 'after you resolve' (RRG 1.8 'Resolve', p. 37).
* A scheme threat cannot be removed from is no target for a basic thwart while the rule is active; a confused hero may still try (RRG 1.8 'Target', p. 43).
* An 'if X, gets +N ATK/SCH for this activation' bonus is read after the activation's interrupts (Solar Beam, Swinging Stone, Unrelenting Savage; FFG ruling Feb 15, 2024).
* Ties on 'the [superlative] card in your hand' (Temporal Trickery, Thoughtcasting, View the Cosmos, Smoke Bombs) are broken by the first player, who picks among another player's tied cards facedown, in a shuffled order (owner 211B; RRG 1.8 pp. 19, 42).
* Scenario decks can be gathered by trait at setup (the Shadowland trap deck): discards route by printed trait, and a deck reshuffles its discard the moment it runs out, once per batch.
* A status card that cannot be placed (no room, or replaced by an 'instead' interrupt) means the effect did not fully resolve, so a following 'then' is skipped (RRG 1.8 'Then', p. 44).
* Reductions to 'the cost to play' an event also reduce its ability cost (FFG ruling Aug 26, 2026), so Photographic Reflexes lowers Make the Call's ally cost.
* Surge and incite count as When Revealed abilities when a card resolves another card's When Revealed, and the first player orders incite, printed When Revealed and surge on a reveal (FFG ruling Sep 22, 2026; Change of Venue and Countless Numbers' boosts excepted pending erratum).
* A card first dealt facedown to a player counts as revealed from the encounter deck (FFG rulings Jun 2, 2022 and Sep 14, 2026), for Enhanced Spider-Sense, Black Widow and SHOW's surge alike.
* Damage from an event or upgrade counts as coming from the identity that used it for 'damage from a character' checks (Krahllak's Blessing; RRG 1.8 'You, Your').
* Rogue's copied traits (Skin Contact, Energy Transfer, the Rogue ally) follow the chosen character and end when it leaves play, not when Touched moves (FFG ruling Oct 9, 2026).
### Content
* Fear No Evil's villains, scenarios, modular sets and campaign cards, transcribed from Hall of Heroes scans because MarvelCDB lacks them
* Fear No Evil (fne) card data: 199 cards with Daredevil and Echo's starter decks, Daredevil's Sense deck, and card scans for the whole box
* Jessica Jones (jj) card data: 42 cards and a stand-in precon from a public decklist until the printed list is found
* Luke Cage (luke_cage) card data: 37 cards and a stand-in precon from a public decklist until the printed list is found
* Jessica Jones' identity and events and Luke Cage's events are scripted
* Daredevil's identity is scripted: Superhuman Senses plays the top card of his Sense deck, and a Sense upgrade that would leave play goes to the bottom of the deck instead
* Daredevil's and Echo's nemesis sets and obligations, and Echo's supports, upgrades and allies are scripted
* Luke Cage's identity is scripted: any number of tough cards, piercing lost against him, and 1 unpreventable damage when a tough card is discarded from him
* Daredevil's events are scripted: Cross-Examination, Deposition, Living Lie Detector and Raising Hell
* Daredevil's Sense deck, supports, upgrades and allies are scripted, Focus the Senses included
* Fear No Evil's Justice cards are scripted, De-escalation aside
* Echo's identity and events are scripted: Watch and Learn, Practice Makes Perfect, Photographic Reflexes and Study the Tape
* Fear No Evil's Protection cards are scripted, The Best Offense and Stand Alone included
* Fear No Evil's basic cards are scripted: Stick, Contingency Planning and the two Team-Ups
* Jessica Jones' obligation and nemesis set (Purple Man and the Suggestions) are scripted
* Luke Cage's Leadership cards are scripted
* Jessica Jones' other aspect cards are scripted (Shakedown, Lay Low, Mitigated Threat and the Innate cards); her Echo ally waits on an engine change
* Jessica Jones' supports, upgrades and allies are scripted, Stakeout and the 4K Digital Camcorder included
* Jessica Jones' Justice cards are scripted (Run Them to Ground, Grapnel Launcher, Calling in Favors, Squirrel Girl); Entrapment waits on a change-form cost
* Jessica Jones' basic cards and Luke Cage's supports, upgrades and allies are scripted; Defend Our City waits on a lasting cost discount
* Luke Cage's nemesis set and his other aspect and basic cards are scripted (Cottonmouth, Sidewinder, Dynamic Duo, Toughen Up)
* Script Jessica Jones' Entrapment (61024) and Defend Our City (61029); Stakeout and Strategy Session use the shared side-scheme queries.
* Jessica Jones and Luke Cage are playable: their starter decks play full games against Rhino and replay deterministically.
* A two-player Jessica Jones and Luke Cage game against Rhino, with Unbreakable Bond (61027, 62024) checked for its Team-Up requirement and its effect.
* Daredevil and Echo starter decks played together against Rhino: See No Evil, Hear No Evil, a Sense upgrade only its controller triggers, and Echo replaying Daredevil's event.
* Scaffold the Fear No Evil encounter-half ability modules (villains, scenarios, modular sets, campaign cards) with coverage guards.
* Script the Hammerhead villain set (60086-60096): stun-or-damage forced response, Energy Gatling Gun, Hardheaded, Chameleon, Maggia minions, Consolidate Power, Power Struggle, Head First.
* Script the Bullseye villain set (60065-60075): boost icons and ranged on activation, Adamantium-Laced Spine, Raise the Stakes, Deadly Sai, Incessant Pursuit, Lady Bullseye, Deranged Bloodlust, Everything is a Weapon, Unerring Desire.
* Script the Electro villain set (60076-60085): Electro, Electric Charge, Drained of Power, Whiplash, Charging Up, Chain Lightning, Energy Overload, Shocking Revelation.
* Script the Disasters modular set (60177-60181); the ability validator reads cards picked inside an either/or cost branch.
* Script the Cops modular set (60182-60185): Cop attacks the villain instead of scheming, Crooked Cop, Wanted, Police Cordon.
* Script the Drive modular set: Sports Car, Motorcycle, Utility Van and Carjacking (Traffic Jam waits on an engine task, E20).
* Script the Tombstone modular set (60195-60199): Cold as Ice, Hard as Marble, Beetle, Tombstone and Hit List.
* Script The Owl modular set (60191-60194): Flight Serum, Mister Fish, The Owl, Swoop Down.
* Script the Typhoid Mary villain set (60110-60120): Disturbed Psyche's random side and phase-end flip, Establish Trust and Mary Walker, Telekinesis, Assembled Armor and the treacheries.
* Script Traffic Jam (60189): threat a character with a Vehicle attachment would add to or remove from a scheme is added to or removed from Traffic Jam instead.
* Scenario records can offer a choice of villain (Scenario.villainOptions, curated as villainOptionSetCodes / villainOptionDetails), with schema and card validation.
* Script the Tracksuit Mafia modular set (60200-60204): Ivan Banionis, The Clown, Tracksuit Mafioso and Bro tucked under the Tracksuit Mafia side scheme, revealed when a Tracksuit minion comes off the encounter deck; actingCharacter ref (RRG You, Your p. 49).
* Script the Purple Man villain set (60097-60109): Influenced minion keywords, the Boost his stages grant his set's boost cards, Converted, the civilians' When Defeated, the command obligations, Power of Suggestion and Sway the Masses.
* Fear No Evil: the six scenario records (five underling scenarios with villain options, Kingpin) and the campaign record; Protection Racket's five main schemes are five cards.
* Script the Art Museum Heist scenario set (60121-60127) and play it against Hammerhead to a win and a loss.
* Script The Getaway scenario set (60128-60133): Out Front and Alongside, speed counters, Escort Vehicle, Jackknifed Tanker Truck, Burning Rubber and Pedestrians, with scenario and full-game tests.
* Synthezoid Smackdown (MC57) card data: She-Hulk and Vision leaders, ten encounter sets, four main scheme stages and two scenarios, in cycle 10 between Civil War and Wonder Man; held back as unscripted until its scripts land.
* Moon Knight and Phased Out errata (RRG 1.8 p. 70) recorded with the printed text kept; Choosing Sides starts at 4 threat per player.
* Script the Kingpin scenario (60159-60176): Kingpin's two faces in both modes, The King's Gambit and Endgame, Public Support, the attachments, Black Cat, the minions and treacheries; Public Support's High side and Bag of Tricks wait on engine tasks E23 and E24.
* Kingpin: Public Support's High side (owner Q207 open, built B) and both of Bag of Tricks's abilities are scripted; nothing in the Kingpin set is left unscripted.
* Script the Stop the Presses! scenario (60151-60158): Daily Bugle setup with random supports, stamina, Ben Urich, Betty Brant, J. Jonah Jameson, Robbie Robertson, Exclusive Interview and Breaking News; its four supports are set aside and its stage loses when completed.
* Script The Raft Breakout scenario (60142-60150): each minion's gained When Defeated, Master Key and Mysterio dealing boost minions, Baron Zemo, Absorbing Man, Drang, Proxima Midnight, Rhino and Imprisoned (owner Q209 open, built A).
* Script the Protection Racket scenario (60134-60141): five per-player main schemes, Shop Proprietor, Man-Bull and Change of Venue.
* Double-sided allies record the stats their flip side prints (Bloody Mary 60210b THW 1 / ATK 2, Odin's King side 21139b THW 3 / ATK 4); the Typhoid Mary campaign ally is scripted.
* Fear No Evil's five campaign environments are scripted on their Completed and Failed faces, including The Getaway's expert round-one attachment rule.
* Fear No Evil campaign: progress draws, villain record, environments, the Typhoid Mary ally, expert hit points and the Kingpin finale. A lost scenario goes back to Campaign Setup with its marks kept (MC60 p. 8).
* Fear No Evil Campaign Setup step 12 puts every environment into play, then the first player orders their Setups.
* Synthezoid Smackdown: the registration and resistance main schemes are scripted (the pack stays gated until its leaders and modular sets are).
* Synthezoid Smackdown: the SHIELD Ops modular set is scripted.
* Synthezoid Smackdown: the Deadly Duo modular set is scripted.
* Synthezoid Smackdown: the She-Hulk leader and her encounter set are scripted.
* Synthezoid Smackdown: the Royal Guard modular set is scripted.
* Synthezoid Smackdown: the Young Avengers and Scarlet Twins modular sets are scripted.
* Synthezoid Smackdown: the Taskmaster modular set is scripted.
* Synthezoid Smackdown: the Moon Knight modular set is scripted (Moon Knight's errata limit once per phase).
* Synthezoid Smackdown: the Thunderbolts modular set is scripted.
* Synthezoid Smackdown: the reprinted Standard PvP sets reuse Civil War's scripts; the leader player cards wait for competitive mode.
* Synthezoid Smackdown: the Vision leader and his set are scripted, including the Dense and Intangible mass form attachment.
* Next Evolution re-emits cleanly from its curation: the Marauders random villain name is curated, stage cards keep their printed card ids, and Luck Be a Lady's stale split refs are gone.
* Synthezoid Smackdown is playable: She-Hulk and Vision join Civil War's shelf after its four leaders, their modular sets join each side's pool, and Civil War leaders can pick the pack's main scheme stages.
* Shadowland (MC65): the 40 cards with published previews are transcribed from Hall of Heroes scans and second-checked; the remaining 30 wait for scans. Not playable yet.
* Shadowland (MC65) joins the card pool as data, gated so none of its sets is playable until it is scripted.
* Shadowland: every transcribed card is scripted (positions, the trap deck, corruption, the Dark Protégé villains' A sides, Ninja Swarm); the pack stays gated until the rest are transcribed.
* Shadowland campaign record (three scenarios in order, Black Flower prohibited) and its three scenario records authored; the scenarios are emitted once their main scheme faces are transcribed.
* Fear No Evil's Art Museum Heist opens on its own five-page lettered comic.
* Fear No Evil's The Getaway opens on its own five-page lettered comic.
* Fear No Evil's Protection Racket opens on its own five-page comic, and the comic title boxes no longer print an issue number (the underling issues play in any order).
* Fear No Evil's The Raft Breakout opens on its own five-page comic, from New Avengers (2004) #1-3.
* Fear No Evil's Stop the Presses! opens on its own five-page comic, from Devil's Reign (2021-2022).
* Fear No Evil builds each issue's comic from its scenario and the villain you face: the full story when it fits that villain, otherwise neutral pages, a reveal of that villain and Suit up. Kingpin's issue opens on the Final Showdown.
### Docs
* A read-only code-reviewer agent that applies the rules authority order, and CLAUDE.md guidance on when to call it
* Add a rules-expert agent that answers rules questions from the sources in authority order before any go to the owner; CLAUDE.md says when to use it.
* Older FFG rulings (RRG 1.0-1.4, and after RRG 1.5) transcribed by script from Hall of Heroes into docs/rulings/, one small file per section with an index; the rules expert searches them.
* MC57 Synthezoid Smackdown rulebook PDF and markdown added to docs/campaign-modes.
* The rules authority page links each source's local Markdown copy (RRG v1.8, the rulings transcripts and split older rulings, every campaign box rulebook) for searching and for agents.
* Shadowland (MC65) sources and data survey, with its rules insert transcribed to Markdown.
* The post-RRG 1.7 rulings transcript is regenerated verbatim from Hall of Heroes by the rulings scraper, now through October 9, 2026 (14 new rulings).

## [v0.30.0] - 2026-10-21
### Added
* A resource that pays only for one kind of card, like Finesse, shows in the payment row as unable to pay for other cards. Inspect says why, and the pause menu's table rules explain aspect cards.
* The card sheet for a main scheme has a Show A side / Show B side button, so you can read the side you missed
### Changed
* A copied or downloaded bug report log puts its replay and debug snapshot in json code blocks, so they read as code when pasted into an issue.
### Fixed
* An effect that puts a "Hero form only" or "Alter-Ego form only" card into play no longer does so for a player in the other form; the card stays where it was (RRG p. 32)
* An effect that removes an acceleration token can no longer take one from the main scheme; it removes only a token placed on another card (RRG p. 5)
* An "after [enemy] attacks and damages" or "attacks and defeats" ability now resolves once for each character the attack damages or defeats, so overkill damage spilled onto an identity or villain triggers it again (Jigsaw, Toad, Cypher, Captain Marvel; ruling January 11, 2026 (2), RRG p. 31)
* Any player can now use the Hero Action of Obedience Potion and The Poison (RRG 1.8 p. 66 errata)
* A card whose text box is blank (Hope Summers treated as a minion) still loses the game when it leaves play: alternate win or loss conditions cannot be blanked (RRG p. 48; ruling Nov 29, 2023)
* "Attacks and damages you" (Green Goblin, Tombstone, the Sinister Six, Beetle, Gamora, Space Pirates, Power Gauntlets, Ionic Boots) now need your identity damaged, not a defending ally, "You'll Pay for That!" counts only your identity's damage and is not offered when your identity took none, and Defiance cannot be played while your ally defends (RRG p. 43, p. 49; FFG rulings Jul 18, 2024 and Mar 15, 2024)
* A sustained-damage cap such as Boarding Party lowers damage already above it as soon as it is in effect again, e.g. after a blank ends (FFG ruling Oct 10, 2025)
* Revealing another player's obligation while that player is in a different game area (Kang stage 2B) now removes it from the game and reveals a replacement card (FFG ruling Sep 21, 2023; RRG 1.8 p. 30)
* Bulldozer's Helmet resolves once per attack and discards 1 card per point of damage the whole attack dealt, overkill onto your hero included; Major Domo and Head of Steam count overkill damage too, and all three count damage a tough status card stopped, since it was dealt (RRG 1.8 p. 14, p. 31, p. 49; rulings Jan 11, 2026 (2) and Jan 17, 2026 (3))
* Battery Pack moves its charge counter rather than removing and adding one, so it arrives as the type the Tech card defines (a reflection counter on Energy Barrier), per RRG p. 6 and the Battery Pack FAQ on p. 62
* Finesse's resource can only be used while Spider-Woman is in hero form, as its Hero Resource text says.
* The Mad Titan's Shadow campaign Cosmo reward shows its own card art instead of the basic Cosmo's (#296).
* Main schemes show their B side in play and their real A side as the other side: Mad Titan's Shadow schemes stayed on their A side, and many later packs showed the B side on both
* The guide's payment warning suggests the cheapest card to spend instead (a free resource ability, then a resource card, then a card with no play this round), says why it picked it, checks resource types, and no longer calls a chosen spend an overpay (#299).
* The defend picker shows each defender's health now beside what the attack leaves it ("Now 2 of 4 HP → 0 left"), so a hurt ally reads as hurt, and its card pictures open the hover popover (#298).
* Hovering a defender's picture in the defend picker shows its health bar with the attack's damage hatched ("2→0"), as the villain's own preview does (#298).
* On a narrow desktop window the defend picker writes each defender's health as "3/3 HP → 0–1 LEFT" instead of cutting the line off (#298).
* Spectrum's energy forms show faceup, in their own column beside the play area, and the form picker shows each form's face instead of a card back (#308).
* A card hover popover on a screen with a search field (Seats, Decks, the deck builder) no longer slides under the field.
### Engine
* The pay step's query lists resource abilities that can't pay for the card only because they generate for another kind of card (Finesse for a hero card).
### Docs
* Rule comments cite the FFG rulings issued after March 2025 by date, as Hall of Heroes lists them, instead of a made-up "69.NN" number

## [v0.29.0] - 2026-10-20
### Added
* Villain phase: each encounter card dealt facedown stops on its back with a Flip Encounter Card button and turns over before it is revealed, in deal order; settings turn the stop off and set the flip speed (#287).
* Facedown encounter cards dealt to a player show as a small pile of card backs (×N) on their identity card or team row, with a hover caption saying why they were dealt, and each dealt card flies from the encounter deck to it; the rail's FACEDOWN total is gone (#287).
### Fixed
* On a phone, the villain's boost card no longer covers the attack's damage breakdown, and the damage total is never cut off (#285).
### Engine
* Log an encounterCardDealt event, with what dealt it, for every facedown encounter card dealt to a player.

## [v0.28.1] - 2026-10-20
### Fixed
* Red Skull: The Sleeper no longer comes back from the encounter deck after it is defeated and removed from the game (#277).
* Picking a second way to spend Organizational Support (or Energy Siphon) in the payment strip now replaces the first, instead of leaving Pay refused with no reason (#279).
* The README's Special thanks lists stay filled in: the release job can read issues again, and the lists are left as they are if GitHub returns none.
* The release workflow no longer misreads an existing release tag as missing and fails trying to tag it again.

## [v0.28.0] - 2026-10-19
### Added
* Every release's notes thank the GitHub users who reported or wrote its changes, and the README has Special thanks lists with everyone's avatar (#268).
### Changed
* The campaign roster's seat picker lists the top five recommended partners best first (seat 1 included), then every available hero A to Z, then locked or already-seated heroes A to Z under their own heading.
### Fixed
* A hero's name, deck note and reason no longer go blank in the campaign roster's seat picker after a finger touches their row (#272).
* A sideways swipe across a row of a scrolling list no longer picks that row.
* The in-game bug report link now fills only a Game details box, so edits on GitHub's Bug report form stay put and the screenshot box takes images again (#273).
### Docs
* A Rules authority page gives the order rules sources win in when they disagree, how conflicts are resolved, and links every Rules Reference version and FFG rulings page.

## [v0.27.0] - 2026-10-19
### Added
* Ironheart's Level Up! gets its own band across the table, in her armor's colors, naming the version she becomes.
### Fixed
* An attack dealt two boost cards (Klaw) no longer lets a second press on Let it resolve pass the next boost card's interrupt window unseen: each window names its boost card and ignores presses for a moment after it opens, and a card on top of Magik's deck reads as played from the deck.
* Paying for a card no longer freezes the game for seconds on a slower device: a rules check that ran on every move listed every card ability in the game each time (#256).
* Dragging through the campaign roster's deck picker on a phone no longer closes it when the finger lifts; only a tap on the backdrop does (#257).
* Paying for a card no longer freezes the game: each check of your picks runs in the engine worker, and pressing Pay no longer stalls for over a second while the guide checks for a wasted card (#258).
* The deck builder's hero list shows Ironheart once instead of once per version card (#260).
* Ending a swipe over the hero-down banner on a phone no longer dismisses it; only a tap does.
* Starting Galaxy's Most Wanted mission 3 no longer deals each player a second opening hand (#263).
* Leaving Game over by Back to title after a campaign game no longer loses the result: continuing the campaign opens that game's Aftermath instead of the same mission (#265).

## [v0.26.0] - 2026-10-18
### Added
* The Android app notices power saving mode: while it is on, the game turns on reduced motion, draws text at 1x and caps the frame rate at 30 fps (unless you set those yourself), and says so in a notice, on the title screen and in Settings. Settings > Display > Lighter play in power saving turns it off (#249).
* On Android the music pauses while the game is in the background and plays again when you come back to it (#249).

## [v0.25.0] - 2026-10-18
### Added
* The music player has Pause, and on desktop a volume slider; Settings > Display > Sound has Music and Effects volumes, and in the Windows app the slider and a Game volume row follow the game's own entry in the Windows volume mixer (#243).
### Changed
* Opening the app and the first visit to Decks & Collection, Campaign and Extras freeze less on slower devices: screens load on demand, labels are measured once per font and painted off the main thread, and deck checks run in a worker
* Settings has a Sound tab: Music and Effects volumes on every platform, Game volume in the Windows app; muting the computer pauses the music (#243).
### Fixed
* Dauntless and Fighting Fit compare remaining hit points with the printed starting hit points, so a damaged hero with a hit point upgrade keeps the benefit (RRG 1.8 p. 22).
* Superhuman Strength no longer discards itself when the attacked enemy cannot be stunned (already stunned, stalwart) or was defeated by the attack (RRG 1.8 FAQ p. 57; FFG rulings May 25 and 30, 2023)
* Hela's second villain face (A2) now counts as stage 2, so effects that read her stage number see 2 (FFG ruling, Sep 5, 2024)
* Warning no longer has the Defense trait (RRG 1.8 p. 65 errata)
* Events with a cost before the arrow (Defender of the Nine Realms, Return the Favor, Karmic Blast, Cosmic Awareness) now pay that cost when the hero is stunned or confused, and Cosmic Awareness needs 3 threat on a scheme (FFG ruling April 30, 2026 (2); RRG 1.8 p. 14).
* We Are One can no longer be discarded by spending wild resources as energy, mental and physical: a "printed resources" cost does not let a wild stand in for another type (RRG p. 35).
* Spectrum tucking a card with two matching resource icons (such as Mental and Wild) now gets both bonuses, e.g. +4 THW (FFG ruling, August 22, 2025).
* Mutant Education can no longer be played with no identity-specific card in your discard pile and no X-Mansion in play (FFG ruling May 22, 2023; RRG 1.8 p. 42).
* Worried Father and Lost Visor: the Alter-Ego Action adds George Stacy or Ruby Quartz Visor to hand from wherever the card is when the search found nothing (FFG ruling May 10, 2024, Ruling 180).
* Seduced (Enchantress set 21179 and Valkyrie nemesis 25032) now bars only basic attacks, not attacks made by card abilities (RRG p. 10, p. 49).
* X-Men Instruction can no longer be played with no X-MEN ally in your discard pile and no X-Mansion in play (FFG ruling May 22, 2023; RRG 1.8 p. 42).
* Deadpool (ally) healing 3 damage when consequential damage would defeat him no longer skips the defeat check: if he still has no hit points left he is defeated (FFG ruling, March 15, 2024; RRG 1.8 p. 14).
* Cosmo: naming a card type now offers player side scheme for a player deck and environment for the encounter deck, plus encounter types for a player deck holding an encounter card (RRG 1.8 p. 12, errata p. 66).
* Chill Out! played by another hero can attach Iceman's set-aside Frostbite (FFG ruling of June 20, 2024, Q216 answer 2).
* Deckbuilding: Spider-Woman's identity-specific cards that print an aspect (Pheromones) now count toward that aspect in her equal split, so 10 Leadership + 12 'Pool is legal (FFG ruling Mar 22, 2024)
* "I Got This" checks the icons in play when it is played and lets you resolve its bullets in the order you choose (FFG ruling, post-RRG 1.5).
* Every ability labeled "(attack)" is now an attack by your identity from when it begins resolving, even when it deals no enemy damage (Multitalented paid without [physical] still counts as attacking), RRG p. 26 and FFG ruling 69.67
* Hex Bolt: you choose the order in which the discarded cards resolve, each counting its own boost icons as it resolves (FFG ruling, Oct 26, 2023).
* Hulk (ally) discarding a card with several printed resources, such as Salvage, now lets you choose the order the results resolve in (RRG 1.8 p. 61).
* Nebula's Combat Protocols discards only the Technique upgrades whose Special actually resolved; one replaced by a stunned or confused status card stays in play (RRG 1.8 p. 44; FFG ruling Oct 18, 2024).
* Side Holster makes room for Laser Swords, so Captain America's Shield, Side Holster and Laser Swords stay in play together (FFG ruling 152, March 22, 2024)
* Laser Swords (and Kurt's Cutlasses) count as one of your restricted cards weighing 2: over the restricted limit they can be chosen for the discard, freeing 2 (RRG p. 38; contributor's rules call (bluefireF5ran) 2026-10-16)
* A character that cannot be healed is no longer offered as the target of a heal, and an ability whose only effect is healing such a character cannot be used (RRG p. 43; ruling Dec 8, 2023)
* A piercing attack (Piercing Strike, or a basic attack that gains piercing) can target a Tough enemy that cannot take damage and discards its Tough card (RRG p. 43; ruling Jul 11, 2025)
* A character with a printed dash THW (such as the Hulk ally) can no longer thwart The Red House with its ATK (ruling June 17, 2025; RRG p. 15 "Dash")
* Energy Transfer (and any cost that attaches a card) can no longer name the character the card is already attached to (FFG ruling May 10, 2024, post-RRG 1.5 #182)
* Venom Goblin: the glider counter can no longer stay on its own scheme when another main scheme ties it for least (or most) threat; it must move to the other scheme (RRG p. 30 "Move", ruling of July 2, 2023)
* Hela's expert B2 side counts as stage 2, so Muster Courage and other stage-reading effects see 2 there too.
* Cosmo offers the same card types for every player deck, so naming a type no longer reveals whether that deck holds an encounter card; the Nebula pack reprint now matches the Star-Lord pack's Cosmo.
* A cancelled enemy attack still ends, so its "at the end of this attack" effects resolve: Rhino's Charge is discarded when Grasping Tendrils cancels his attack (FFG Ruling 148)
* "At the end of this attack" effects resolve before the attack's responses, so Riposte's delayed damage lands before Electrostatic Armor's 1 (RRG 1.8 "Delayed Effect"; FFG ruling Sep 26, 2024)
* A permanent player attachment whose host leaves play resolves its "attach to" text or is removed from the game (RRG 1.8 "Permanent"), except Frostbite, which is set aside, and Wrist Navigator when its host is defeated
* Wonder Man's attack asks which card to discard instead of discarding the first card in hand (#242).
* Ant-Man's Giant form shows the Giant card on the table and in the hover popover, and its type line reads GIANT (#244).
* CI parses every changelog fragment on pull requests, so a malformed one no longer reaches main and breaks the release PR
* Music keeps playing when the desktop app is in the background, and the game shows in the Windows volume mixer as Marvel Champions (#243).
### Docs
* Changelog fragments are linted by pnpm check, the pre-commit hook and their own CI check, so a malformed one shows up on its pull request
* Every YAML file is linted with yamllint by pnpm check, the pre-commit hook and its own YAML lint CI check

## [v0.24.0] - 2026-10-18
### Added
* The deck builder shows the card pool as card pictures (List view keeps the text rows), searches card text, warns before leaving unsaved changes, can delete a saved deck, and New deck picks a hero from searchable tiles and starts from an empty deck or a precon copy (#217).
* Decks & Collection gets a card search, an Edit a copy button for precons and a confirm step on Delete (#217).
* Take your seats shows one tile per hero with a count of its decks and a Pick a deck submenu; search matches deck names, authors and notes, ranked by relevance and run in a background worker (#219).
* Every decision sheet can step aside to show the table: Peek at table on the full-screen sheets (your turn's targets and discards, the villain phase's defend and choices), Hide or Peek on the docked defend, and Collapse to dock a decision again after Expand (#231).
### Changed
* Every search filter (Take your seats, Scenario select, Decks & Collection, the deck builder and the campaign roster and collection) ranks its matches in a background worker, fuzzy and by relevance, and the worker shuts down when idle.
* A revealed card's decision during the villain phase (Hydra Bomber's choose one) now docks in the villain phase panel beside the table, with Hide on desktop and tablet and Peek at table on phone, so you can check the battlefield before you choose (#231).
* CI: one "All checks" check covers lint, format, typecheck, the unit tests and the client build, so the branch ruleset can require it alone; lint moved from checks.yml into the CI workflow
### Fixed
* The defend prompt's damage range now counts prevention already on the attack, such as Magic Barrier's "prevent 3" (#212).
* A decision sheet no longer keeps a card picked on a sheet that was undone: after an undo, the end-of-turn discard could hold an invisible pick that stopped the hand's last card from being chosen (#211).
* Clicking a shelf's scroll arrow (Take your seats, Choose a scenario, Extras, campaign shelves) no longer also picks the card that slides in under it (#218)
* Editing a precon now edits your own copy: saving it adds a new deck instead of silently keeping the precon, and decks saved the old way are repaired into their own decks (#217).
* Domino: a card discarded from the top of her deck still counts for the card that discarded it after its response moves it, so Jackpot! shuffled back gives Luck Be a Lady, A Good Workout and the rest its three icons (Digging Deep, White Fox and The Painted Lady likewise; Age of Apocalypse mission attempts keep FFG's ruling and warn). The response sheet names what discarded the card (#222).
* Opening the app, the Campaign saga and Decks & Collection no longer freezes for seconds on older devices: release builds skip the card-script check, art decodes off the main thread and arrives in one redraw, and deck checks are cached (#223)
* Phones: a sideways swipe of the play area that lifts on the villain phase review handle no longer opens the recap, and the play area no longer runs under the handle (#227)
* Battle Fury, Jarnbjorn, Tigra and every other "after you attack (and defeat) a minion" response now trigger when the attack defeats an Ultron Drone or another card that is a minion only while in play (#228)
* Phone: during a payment, a tap on a pinned table card (Peter Parker's Scientist) no longer picks the hand card scrolled under it.
* Phone: the pause menu sets Card size in place as a quick setting, and a Show all settings row opens the full Settings screen; before, a phone could reach only a few Table toggles mid-game.
* Phone: on the full-screen defend sheet, a drag that lifts on an option no longer picks it.

## [v0.23.0] - 2026-10-17
### Added
* Desktop app: Settings > Display > Window picks Windowed, Borderless or Fullscreen and, from a dropdown with Apply, a resolution (Native by default). On Windows, Fullscreen really switches the monitor to one of its own display modes and puts yours back when you leave Fullscreen, switch away, quit or crash; elsewhere the game draws at that resolution and scales to fit with black bars. SteamOS and Bazzite start in Fullscreen at native, with a Steam Deck row to return to it.
* Desktop app: a Quit to desktop button on the Title screen.
* Music player (#191): a music button in the table's and Title's top bar announces each new song with a Now playing pill (a notification on phones) and opens a player with your earned songs, Shuffle and Next; Extras' Music tab gains an expanded player with a progress bar; Settings ▸ Sound adds Shuffle earned music.
* New in Extras (#191): a tink and a notice naming what a game just opened (New song earned, New villain file, 3 new extras unlocked) slide in as it happens.
* A sound plays when hands refill at the end of the player phase.
* A punch sound plays on every basic attack.
* Sounds for a basic thwart, a card played or entering play, and a Spell played; a card's sound plays before its attack or thwart.
* Doctor Strange's Cloak of Levitation and Captain America's Shield Toss have their own sounds.
### Changed
* Arrow keys and the D-pad move focus to the nearest control in that direction on screen; Tab and Shift+Tab keep the route order.
* Pull requests from forks now run the typecheck and unit tests on CI; only the Netlify preview deploy still skips them.
* Netlify builds deploy previews only for pull requests from forks; everything else still deploys from GitHub Actions.
### Fixed
* Android: the update notice and other outside links open in your browser instead of the GitHub app's in-app tab, where the APK download stalled and its Open button did nothing
* Android: the update banner's Download and the game's other outside links open again (the browser hand-off never finished)
* Conceding from the pause menu while the villain phase waits for Start now ends the game and goes to Game Over, instead of leaving the round stuck behind a menu reading Defeat.
* The pause menu's status line reads Conceded after a concession, not Defeat.
* The keyboard and controller focus ring now frames the scenario and hero cards it selects (it sat one gutter to the left on both setup screens).
* Sound effects start on the action: the silence at the front of the punch, card, Spell and Cloak sounds is trimmed.
* Sound effects load when the game starts, so the first deal or punch of a session is not late.
### Docs
* A CODEOWNERS file asks the maintainer to review every pull request.

## [v0.22.0] - 2026-10-16
### Added
* Desktop: the villain phase docks on the right with the table and your hand still in view, and the interrupt window answers there (#129, #138)
* Tablet: in landscape the villain phase takes the villain row with your hero, play area and hand below; in portrait it sits above your hand with its buttons at the thumb
* Phone: the villain phase keeps your hand in view with USE and RESOLVE at the thumb, and PEEK AT TABLE lets you look at the board while it waits
* While the villain phase is up, each card in your hand is tagged with when it can answer it (Play now, When hit, On a treachery, When threat lands, Resource, Your turn), and the one you can play now is outlined in yellow (#129, #138)
* Desktop: rest the mouse on a card on the table to see it larger beside the pointer, with its state; new Card size on hover setting (off, 1.5x to 4x, default 2.5x), every card blown up to the same size
* The docked villain phase slides in as the phase begins and back out after its recap; through the next player phase a handle at the table's edge reopens that round's recap for review
* A global Card size setting (Fit to screen, Same size everywhere, Large, Extra large) draws cards larger on the table, which then scrolls under the top bar, your hand and the action buttons, in the villain phase, and in Decks, the deck builder, Deck check, Rules and the Civil War builder, with fewer columns and scrolling instead of cut-off cards
* Desktop: the card hover popover also works on the villain phase's cards and on the table beside the docked villain phase
* The villain phase opens by saying who drew what as the player phase ended, and the cards you drew are tagged New in your hand
* Tap your discard pile (or the encounter discard) to fan out every card in it at once, most recent first, on phone, tablet and desktop; hold or right-click still opens Inspect, and the pile's top card shows the hover preview
* Cards drawn into your hand now fly in from your deck one after another, and each hand slot stays empty until its card arrives (reduced motion skips it)
* Settings: Hero card popover and Villain card popover turn the hover preview on or off for those cards
* The villain phase answers a defend and shows its recap inside the docked panel, widening the dock (about half a desktop screen) or growing the tablet band with a compact hero row, animated; Expand opens the full-screen sheet or recap
* Optional interrupt and response windows say what is about to happen (or just happened), why now, and what confirming does; a decision during table setup docks beside the setup card; Setting up the table honors Card size and has the card hover popover
* Resting the mouse on a card in Decks & Collection, the deck builder, Deck check, the Rules card list, the campaign Aftermath picker or the Civil War builder opens the same larger card popover as on the table
* Kang's per-player areas label their villains and main schemes ("VS SPIDER-MAN"), and Sinister Six villains show an ORDER n chip
* A scheme activation names the scheme that gets the threat, and the recap header shows each side or extra main scheme that gained threat
* A change of active villain (Wrecking Crew, Four Horsemen) gets its own beat in the villain phase and its own recap row
* Ending a turn on a hand size under 4 asks "Are you sure?" and explains the hand size: the printed value, what changes it (Iron Man's +1 per Tech upgrade) and the other face's value; the hand caption shows the hand size beside the count (#150)
* Set the table: each modular set shows a picture of one of its cards and a Cards button that lists every card in the set (tap one to read it), and a Random button draws legal modular sets for you.
* The game's seed is now in the pause menu's status line, opens the game log, and is in the Ctrl+Shift+D diagnostic snapshot, so a game can be named exactly in a bug report or replayed.
* Set the table: a Cards button on the Standard set (and Expert set) row opens every version in one sheet, each saying where it comes from, and each seat shows its hero's picture.
* The first player token: it sits on the first player on Set the table and on the board, and is tossed (or slid, bounced, spun or placed, a new Settings option) to each new first player.
* The name-conflict sheet now offers "Replace with Team-Up" on a hero's own card that matches another seated hero, listing only the Team-Up cards that make the swap legal (RRG v1.8 p.50).
* A bug icon in the board's top bar (and on Title) opens Report an issue: a screenshot tip for your device, Copy log, Download log, and links to report a bug, suggest a feature or request a deck on GitHub (#176).
### Changed
* Villain-phase walkthrough now tracks the encounter cards dealt and revealed and tags each beat with its card; adds the pure held-open recap model.
* With Auto-advance off (the default) the docked villain phase waits for you to press Start villain phase, and a decision someone has to make comes up as a notice with Answer instead of a sheet jumping over your cards
* The hover preview of a scheme shows its threat as the board's red meter with the count, and every card hovers at the same size, the hero panel included
* The main scheme card grows with Card size: its rows scale, and above Fit to screen the whole sideways card is shown larger
* Set the table: unchosen difficulty cards, modular sets and options are drawn at full strength instead of faded (they looked unavailable), and the sidebar shows the chosen villain's picture.
* Settings is split into tabs (Game, Cards, Display, Guide, More) with labeled sections inside them, instead of one long scrolling list.
* The slowest card test files (the modular set soak, now four files, and the deck soaks) run in CI jobs of their own instead of whichever shard their path hashed into, so the cards tests no longer wait on one 20-to-30-minute shard
* The README download buttons show the latest version and link straight to its installers; each release PR updates them, and an All releases button opens every version
### Fixed
* The villain phase recap stays open until you start the player phase, with auto-advance and beat speed in Settings (#117)
* The revealed encounter card is shown large with which card of how many this round, and Hold encounter cards keeps each one up until Next card (#128)
* A decision sheet hidden under the villain phase no longer swallows presses on the table beside it
* The villain phase shows each hero's face instead of a name that did not fit, and Peek at table no longer covers your hand
* Undo no longer rewinds a resumed game to round 1: a resumed game rebuilds its command history, so Undo and Back out stop at the start of the turn as in an uninterrupted game
