* A choice sheet on a phone held sideways now scrolls its options instead of cutting them off with "resize to see them", and shows the source card in a side rail
* While the villain phase is open beside the table, the table takes no actions until you start the player phase (a hand card could be played while the phase was still showing its encounter cards); Inspect and hover still work
* The villain phase recap on a tablet puts a long hero name above the card name instead of crushing it, with more padding
* No player card ability can be used during game setup unless it is a Setup ability (I Object! is no longer offered there), and a side scheme's starting threat is no longer treated as placed threat (#146)
* A guided lesson no longer holds its villain-phase decisions behind ANSWER when Auto-advance is off
* Larger Card sizes make play-area cards, Doctor Strange's Invocation piles and a phone's minions bigger instead of smaller, and an engaged minion's alter-ego chip reads "vs S.-Man" instead of "S.-M. (. PARKER)"
* Main scheme names in the log, villain phase rail and recap show the stage the scheme is on, not its stage 1 title
* Log lines read "against you" mid-sentence instead of "against You", and status lines say the status ("Pestilence is stunned.") instead of ending at "is"
* A villain phase breakdown with several boost cards reads "BOOST 0 (2 cards)" instead of "BOOST ×2 0"
* Several villains in a row (Wrecking Crew, Sinister Six, Four Horsemen) show their card art at every Card size, docked or on a phone, with whole-word names and stats and rows that follow Card size
* A side scheme's owner ("SIDE SCHEME · WRECKER") is no longer covered by its threat bar
* On very large screens the hand and the main scheme card grow instead of staying thumbnails, and the Invocation piles' DECK and DISC. captions no longer cover the card title
* While the villain phase replays, the table keeps the phase's round and step and shows that it waits, instead of End turn and Undo
* A villain phase decision appears only when the replay reaches it, headed with the deciding hero and the activation ("SPIDER-MAN — DOCTOR OCTOPUS ATTACKS YOU")
* Game over waits until the villain phase has shown the beats that ended the game
* A phone with three or more players docks the defend decision instead of opening the full-screen sheet
* Villain phase headlines wrap or shrink instead of losing their target, and heroes read by their short names in headlines, target tags and the encounter strip
* Villain phase decision notices show the prompt's own question and options, Breakout's pick reads "Choose the new active villain" and answers itself with one candidate, and an interrupt window names the hero when several are playing
* The villain phase recap keeps its figures readable, drops the Auto-advance paused rows, gives encounter-card activations their own rows and fits the token pass on one line
* Hydra Flame-Soldier now discards a support after its undefended attack, and Laser Strike and Home by Dawn no longer let you skip their discard to take surge instead.
* Resting the mouse on a card in a decision sheet (the source card, the option cards, the defend matchup) opens the card hover popover, as it does on the table (#149).
* Settings: the ✕ closes the panel again after a Card size change (rows scrolled under the title bar no longer swallow the press) (#151)
* A device whose WebGL cannot draw shows why in plain text (the error, the WebGL renderer and limits, the screen and browser) instead of a black page (#157)
* Test links for a device whose WebGL shows black: ?renderer=canvas, ?gl=alpha|preserve|noaa|lowpower, and ?diag=1's readout of what WebGL drew (#157)
* Set the table no longer goes blank when you deselect a chosen modular set: it shows how many more to pick and holds Deal it out until you do (#161).
* Edge on Android no longer shows a black screen: on a device whose WebGL runs on ANGLE's Vulkan backend the game asks for a single-sampled canvas, which Edge displays; every other browser keeps the usual one (#157)
* Text fields (the search bars and the seed box) no longer show as a bright bar over the black between New game screens: they fade with the screen.
* Set the table: Longshot's extra set shows its card picture and its one card (it read as 0 cards).
* Set the table: with a long modular list scrolled, the difficulty cards and the seats take clicks again (scrolled-away modular tiles no longer catch the mouse).
* Marked for Death now tucks only Clint Barton's own Mockingbird (04004), never Core's basic Mockingbird or another player's (ruling Feb 28, 2026 R7).
* Drax's "after the villain attacks Drax" no longer places a vengeance counter when one of his allies defends the attack (ruling Dec 17, 2025 R3; RRG v1.8 p.15).
* "Give the villain an additional boost card" boost abilities (Pirate Lackey, Intimidation, Titania's Fury, Masterful Mirage, The Trickster and seven others) now give the extra card only when the villain is the one activating, not when a minion is (ruling Feb 28, 2026 R6).
* Bombshell's attack is now divided among each enemy as evenly as possible and can no longer be put on a single target; a plain attack splits itself, with the remainder going to the enemy you attacked first (ruling Jan 11, 2026 R4).
* Every card in the villain-phase recap opens the hover popover: the villain's second attack row, and cards scrolled up from under the footer bar.
* Prince of Power now heals for the excess damage the enemy took, the amount overkill spills, rather than the damage dealt: RRG v1.8 p.31 supersedes the February 8, 2026 ruling (6 damage to Thumbelina heals 2, not 3).
* The main scheme shows The Collection's count against its lose-at-5-per-hero limit, and the Collection pile tile reads N/limit (Galaxy's Most Wanted, #173).
* The campaign Market is one scrolling list of price tiers on every screen size: jump-to-price chips, wallets pinned above the list, a cart bar with a Receipt sheet on phones, "Only what I can afford" on an upright tablet, and the card hover popover on desktop.
* Nebula's Ship "Shoot the Thrusters!" and Machine Man's "spend up to" costs open the pay step again and need at least one resource; the pay bar shows the 1–2 range and no longer offers the Milano to pay its own cost (#174)
* While Chaos Control or Scarlet Witch's Crest is on offer, the villain phase shows the boost card being counted as a card, with its name and icon count; boost cards flipped during an activation show as cards you can tap or hover, and a phone fits two of them (#172).
* Campaign roster seat picker: a search field, a top 5 recommended group, the rest A to Z, reason lines that stay inside their rows, card hover on desktop, and no more freeze while hero art loads (#177).
* A villainous minion that attacks because of Headpool is now dealt a boost card, resolved by Headpool's controller, and its icons add to the damage (ruling Jan 26, 2026).
* Lifting a finger after scrolling Settings or Rules reference on a touch screen no longer presses whatever is behind the panel.
* Batroc's Alert Level shows its threat against the line it waits for ("2/4 THREAT", solo), with "Flips at 4" on the Low side and "Lose at 4" with a red stripe on the High side, instead of "no counters" printed over the card (#182).
* When a "choose one" has only one option left (Security Cameras on an already exhausted character), the log now says so: "2 threat placed on Alert Level (the only option left for Rescued Captive)." (#182)
* Moondragon's "that minion attacks another enemy" now gives a villainous minion its boost card, matching Headpool (ruling Jan 26, 2026).
* Photographic Reflexes and Temporal Shield now trigger when Taskmaster or Kang is attacked, so a tough status card no longer stops them, and Temporal Shield's 1 damage goes to the attacker.
* Robert Kelly now takes an undefended attack's damage even when your identity has a tough status card: his interrupt resolves at the attack's damage step, before any damage is dealt, so the tough card stays.
### Engine
* The end-of-phase draw reports each player's refill (handRefilled), a full hand too, and the log says so, so a small hand size no longer reads as a missed draw (#150)
* Media Coverage now repeats a revealed card's Incite and Surge as well as its printed When Revealed abilities, since both keywords are When Revealed abilities (RRG v1.8 p.24, p.42; ruling Aug 3, 2026 R3).
* A "deal N damage to a character" cost (Thwip Thwip!, Quick Quip, Spider-Tingle, Clarity of Purpose) now counts as paid even when Toughness, a tough status card or a prevention or reduction effect stops the damage (RRG v1.8 p.14; rulings June 25 and July 9, 2026).
* Deck legality now allows Appendix I's Team-Up replacement: a hero's own card that matches another seated hero can be swapped for a Team-Up card naming both (RRG v1.8 p.50).
* A stunned hero or ally can attempt a basic attack even when no enemy is a valid target; the attempt exhausts it and removes the stunned card without dealing damage (RRG v1.8 p.41).
* An attack's forced "after attacks / is attacked / defends / takes or deals damage" abilities, retaliate included, now all resolve before any non-forced response to the same attack (RRG v1.8 p.8-9).
* Peril now stops other players from triggering optional interrupts and responses, including (defense) events, while a player resolves a peril card, and from triggering abilities on a peril card in another player's area; forced abilities still resolve (RRG v1.8 p.32; ruling July 9, 2026 R3).
* While a player resolves a peril card, only that player's characters can defend (RRG v1.8 p.32; ruling July 9, 2026 R3).
* An ally whose basic attack or thwart target leaves play before it resolves no longer takes consequential damage, and its "after it attacks/thwarts" abilities no longer trigger (RRG v1.8 p.13).
* A stunned character that cannot attack at all (for example Vision in Intangible form) can no longer use a basic attack attempt to remove its stun, and "you cannot make basic attacks" is now always found when other attack prohibitions are in play (FFG ruling Jan 18, 2023; RRG v1.8 p.11).
* A stunned character that cannot attack (for example Vision in Intangible form) can no longer use an attack ability to remove its stun (FFG ruling Jan 18, 2023; RRG v1.8 p.11).
* A boost card turned faceup now resolves its interrupts, then its Boost ability, then its responses (RRG v1.8 p.5), so Target Acquired is no longer offered once the Boost ability has resolved.
* Return the Favor: when the treachery revealed while paying its cost stuns your hero, the stun is discarded instead of dealing the 5 damage, and the cost stays paid (FFG ruling April 30, 2026).
* Damage now resolves in RRG v1.8 p.14 order: "would be dealt" abilities such as Armored Rhino Suit resolve before tough status cards, so Rhino keeps his tough card while the suit takes the hit, and "would take" abilities still lose to tough.
* Every response triggered while an encounter card is being revealed (incite or When Revealed threat, damage, cards entering play, discards, status cards) now waits until the reveal has finished, and a revealed character's toughness arrives after its When Revealed (RRG v1.8 p.38, p.45).
* Surge now deals its facedown encounter card while the surging card's When Revealed abilities resolve, so responses to that card's reveal can no longer change which card surge dealt (RRG v1.8 p.42).
### Docs
* Documented why responses to a boost card being turned faceup still resolve before its Boost ability: FFG's June 16, 2022 Target Acquired ruling, kept over the RRG v1.8 p.5 chart.
* CLAUDE.md now sets the order of rules authority: the latest RRG, then errata, then rulings after v1.8, then older rulings where v1.8 doesn't contradict them, then engine interpretations.
* Marked as unresolved, pending FFG, what happens to an enemy attack's after-attack responses when the attacker leaves play during initiation (RRG v1.8 p.6 vs p.9).
* README: current status, roadmap, version and CI badges, and links to the issue chooser; contributor material moved to CONTRIBUTING.md
* README: clickable buttons to play in the browser, download for each platform, report a bug, request a feature or deck, Discord and contributing

## [v0.21.1] - 2026-10-14
### Fixed
* The game log says when an encounter card exhausts a hero or ally (Sonic Boom, Exhaustion, Melter), so a card still exhausted after the villain phase no longer looks like it failed to ready (#136)
* Kang: a stage 3 scheme in your own game area (The Present Future War and the others) now shows as a main scheme with its own name, art and threat limit, instead of a nameless "Kang's Arrival" side scheme (#139)
* Scenario select's win/loss record now follows the selected scenario instead of staying on the one selected when the screen opened.

## [v0.21.0] - 2026-10-14
### Added
* The Civil War box: Tigra, Hulkling, four leaders and a custom scenario builder
* Civil War games: a readable save and pause label for ready-made, custom, random and blind scenarios, and Game Over's Play again and New random scenario.
* Wonder Man is playable: his cards, scripts and nemesis set join the pool, and the deck builder offers him (his starter deck is not printed yet, so he is seated with a deck you build)
* Art for Civil War: Tigra's and Hulkling's hero pictures, the four leaders' pictures and the box cover on the pack shelf
* Hercules joins the playable game: his pack, nemesis set and All Versus All, his Leadership starter deck and Team-Up art; Wonder Man and Hercules unlock by beating Registration Iron Man and Resistance Captain America
* Guided mode: Try-it lessons for Wonder Man (overpay, tuck under Ionic Physiology, attack with the bonus) and Hercules (reveal a Labor, finish it, earn a Gift, play a card it discounts), on the Civil War page
* Community decks: four more MarvelCDB decklists (Tigra, Hulkling, Wonder Man, Hercules, each by Web-Warrior Fanatic), tested; a deck refusal now reads "an attachment card" for vowel-sound types
* Coordinated Effort on the board: a second player can help pay an ability's cost. The player using it is asked who takes each shareable part (damage, a deck discard), the helper's hand cards can be picked for a discard cost, each helper agrees in turn before their cards or hit points are used, the engine's reason is shown when a player cannot take a part, and the log says who helped.
* Game Over pictures for Iron Man and Captain Marvel as leaders, for when they win and when they lose
* Game Over pictures for Captain America and Spider-Woman as leaders, for when they win and when they lose
* In the Pause menu's game log, the moments Undo could get back to this turn are marked ↶, and tapping one rewinds there as pressing Undo that many times would.
### Changed
* Civil War heroes on the board: Ionic Physiology shows its tucked events against its maximum, Hunted's minion pick is a list with each candidate's area, Teddy Altman's refusal reads "can't enter play", the payment bar says how much is overpaid and who helped, Energy Siphon's tiles say their damage, Hercules's Labor and Gift decks show no discard pile and list Labor cards out of the deck, and the log words the wave 10 events it was missing
* The Civil War builder shows the cards: each side's leaders as pictures, leaders with their stage cards, every main scheme stage as its printed card, each modular set as a fan of its cards that opens a sheet of the whole set, and a summary with the leader, the two stages and the chosen sets (hidden ones as card backs); a Civil War! entry on the Title, under Campaign, once the box is unlocked
* Hunted's minion pick is a sheet of card faces, each with its area tag (encounter deck or discard) and an Inspect button
* The first player's choice of which keyword resolves first shows each keyword's name and what it does, with the minion's face shown once
* Hercules: the Gift count shows on his identity and on the cards that read it (with why it reads 0 under Appeal to Athena), and a refused New Labors says which part of its condition failed
* Release downloads spell out their OS, architecture and installer kind (MarvelChampions-<version>-Windows-x64-Setup.exe / -Windows-x64-AllUsers.msi, -macOS-AppleSilicon-arm64.dmg / -macOS-Intel-x64.dmg, -Linux-x64.deb / .rpm / .AppImage, -Android.apk), and release notes end with a "Which download do I need?" table
### Fixed
* Armored Defense gives Hulkling his tough status card even when its damage defeats the attacking minion: an "at the end of this attack" effect now resolves when the attacker left play before the attack resolved
* An attack cancelled outright no longer resolves its 'at the end of this attack' effects (Charge stays on Rhino when Grasping Tendrils cancels his attack); an attack whose attacker left play still does
* The Civil War Maria Hill modular set shows its printed name, Maria Hill, in the scenario builder
* A main scheme stage is completed the moment its target threat falls to the threat on it (a card placed facedown under it, a modifier entering or leaving play), not at the next threat placement.
* Content: Super Skrull (Hulkling) and Grim Reaper (Wonder Man) now carry nemesisMinion, set through a curated correction since neither prints a reminder line
* Secret Avengers, Targeted Strike and Choosing Sides shuffle the encounter deck after searching its top 5 cards (RRG 1.8 Search, p. 39)
* Bring Them In deals a copy of Unregistered Super when every Unregistered identity is already exhausted (RRG 1.8 Otherwise, p. 31; Exhaust, p. 19)
* A search the enemy team must make that finds exactly one card no longer asks the first player (Stark Tower, The Initiative, Neighborhood Protectors)
* Spider-Man (Civil War minion) finds a copy of Tangled Up when no attack was made, including when his attack is replaced by a stun card; a for-each loop can now report what its passes did
* Cosmic Flight: with two copies only one answers a big hit and is discarded; an interrupt to damage is checked again, and reads the new amount, after an earlier interrupt changes it
* Air Cover's search must take a Tactic upgrade it finds; Fantastic Powers shuffles the encounter deck whenever its find looks through it
* A scheme with no threat is not a target for a thwart or a removal of threat (RRG 1.8 "Target", p. 42): a card that only removes threat (For Justice!, Feline Senses, Precinct HQ) cannot be played or used while no scheme holds any, and a scheme with none is not offered
* Ionic Physiology tucks its event only from the discard pile: an event that has left the pile is not tucked and nothing heals (`cardsFrom("discard", …)`)
* A minion being revealed now resolves its When Revealed first, then Quickstrike and Teamwork (the first player picks the order when it has both); responses to it entering play, such as Widow's Bite, wait until after those.
* Prince of Power heals for the excess of the damage Hercules dealt, before a reduction of the damage the enemy takes (6 dealt to a 3-hit-point minion that takes 1 less heals 3, not 2); overkill still spills the excess of the damage taken.
* Protect Humanity (Hercules) with no Amadeus Cho to find is discarded to the bottom of the Labor deck instead of staying in play
* Evaluate Threat asks each player whether to search; only a player who searches has their deck shuffled
* Wonder Man: Everywhere All at Once is not playable with no threat on any scheme, Swordsman's Hero Response is offered for every player's undefended attack, and Disarming Defense reads damage taken rather than dealt
* Not Today! and Desperate Defense (and their Silk and Groot reprints) read 'if you take no damage from that attack' as damage taken, so a tough status card on the defender no longer blocks the reward
* Every revealed encounter card that enters play (side scheme, environment, attachment), not only a minion, holds the responses to its entering play until its reveal has finished (Rules Reference p. 38; owner answer 35B)
* Teamwork resolving after a When Revealed that moved the minion to another player activates against the player it is engaged with as it resolves; Quickstrike still answers only the player the reveal engaged (Rules Reference p. 43; owner answer 36B)
* Follow Through also adds 1 when only the damage dealt has excess: the excess of the damage dealt (Prince of Power's number) goes up, and nothing more spills by overkill (owner answer 42B)
* A card that was not in play when a triggering condition occurred does not answer it: All Versus All, revealed by Hecate's When Defeated, no longer responds to Hecate's defeat (4 threat, not 6; Rules Reference p. 45; owner answer 43B)
* Overpaying: a cost-0 card that reads an overpaid value (Active Altruism) now offers the pay step with Play without overpaying as the default, and Mr. Hollywood shows as an overpay-only source, held until the cost is covered and counted in Overpaid by
* A Find no longer searches or shuffles a special deck that cannot hold the card it names: Protect Humanity looking for Amadeus Cho leaves Hercules's Labor and Gift decks in order
* A resource generated only to overpay (Mr. Hollywood) is no longer refused by a card that can only be paid for with one resource type
* Everywhere All at Once cannot be played with X = 0; a card with a printed X cost can state its least X, and the pay step prices and names that X
* Alliance: another player can pay a whole non-resource part of the costs of an alliance card being played (take damage, discard from their deck), not only its resources
* Playing a card printed with an X cost (Everywhere All at Once, Speed Cyclone, Lethal Intent) from the board now offers an X stepper in the pay step: X starts at the engine's least and goes up to the most you can pay for, the price follows it, and the played card gets that X instead of always the least
* The Title menu fits a small phone or a 1024x768 window with every row showing (Continue, Civil War!, the update banner): gaps and the logo tighten instead of the footer running off the screen, and the Release notes link no longer sits over Settings
* Game Over keeps its rematch buttons and Back to title on screen on a short phone when a long result pushes into them: the art window gives the room back
* The log says "You were flipped" for a flip an effect caused
* Scenario select: picking a product chip or a search that hides the selected scenario now selects the first scenario still shown, so the side panel and Choose heroes never act on a scenario the shelf is not showing
* Scenario select: the Build your own tile shows the Civil War box cover (Captain America against Iron Man) instead of a blank picture area
* Civil War builder: once four modular sets are chosen the other sets are dimmed with a 4 of 4 chosen note instead of the padlock that marks sets that cannot be picked at all
* Title and Pause: a built or rolled Civil War game says so (Custom Civil War: leader, both stages, set count on the Continue row, and the stages and sets listed on the wide Pause menu); the Continue row wraps instead of cutting the label
* Resumed games open with the recent lines in the Board's log panel instead of an empty one
* Game Over: the How it went panel is as tall as its lines instead of a large mostly empty black box
* Civil War builder: Inspect on a main scheme stage prints only that stage's collector number, drops the empty Rules and state panel, and stage tile summaries end at a sentence or a word with an ellipsis
* Table setup: a blind Civil War build shows hidden in place of the deck's card-type counts
* Rules reference: searching the glossary lists the entry named by the search first (Stalwart, Villainous, Permanent, Restricted) before entries that only mention it
* Red Skull: The Sleeper Awakened is removed from the game when The Sleeper is defeated, and cannot leave play before then (#130)
* The villain-phase recap's Continue always closes it and shows a response that opened with the player phase, instead of sitting dead while the prompt waited behind it
* The discard-to-hand-size sheet lays seven or more cards out whole and tappable on a phone instead of fanning them into slivers
* The discard-to-hand-size sheet says how many you must discard at least ("Discard at least 2, hand size 5") and that you may discard more
* The Hunted minion sheet's title wraps to two lines on a phone instead of running under Cancel
* A Labor card's chip on its host reads "Labor (Hercules)" and the Labor pile's lines say where the card is ("In play: ...")
* The threat bar's count is drawn in a face where 7 no longer looks like 1
* Max 1 per scheme is now enforced: a second Under Surveillance, Chance Encounter, Followed, Overwatch or Wrapped in Metal can no longer be attached to the same host (their data carries the per-host limit)
* Zola's Algorithm (Expert Campaign) shows its printed acceleration icon
* The Sleeper Awakened no longer stays in play forever at 0 threat once The Sleeper leaves play without being defeated
* Friends and Family (Down to Earth obligation) now does something: it is scripted, and is a member of the Down to Earth set so it can be revealed
* A locked Civil War hero now reads "Beat Iron Man to unlock Wonder Man" instead of doubling the leader's name, so the "or N pts" price is no longer cut off.
* Two-seat tables: a minion tag too long for its tile shortens to "vs W. Man" instead of ending in an ellipsis, a privacy cover names the seat the way the sheet's header does, and a card pick out of a deck is titled by what the player is doing with the card that asks as its subtitle.
* The Civil War builder's summary reads "4 modular sets (3 or 4 allowed)".
* The update banner has a close button that hides it for that release; a newer release shows it again.
* On a 360 px phone the defend sheet's arrow no longer runs through the "+1 boost" caption.
* Duplicating a deck again numbers the copies ("Deck (copy)", "Deck (copy 2)") instead of stacking "(copy)" suffixes.
* Undo is no longer offered after a move that used a random pick (a card discarded at random) or showed anything hidden: it now allows only moves made of visible, deterministic events, so it can't re-roll a result
* Undo and Z no longer cancel a payment and also take back the move before it: while a payment, cost choice or aim is open Undo is greyed ("Finish or cancel what you're doing first") and Escape closes only the selection
* A greyed Undo now says why: on hover, keyboard focus, a press or tap, and when Z is pressed
* Undo takes back one move per press (a rapid double click no longer takes back two) and says what it undid, such as "Undid: played Backflip"
* The pay bar fits a phone: the X stepper has its own row and can be pressed, and a long Pay label is no longer cut
* Payment order tags show on small phones as a number with a marker, and the note on why the top card matters shows on the bar on touch screens
* Paying for an event no longer tags a payment card "on top": the event itself lands on top of the discard pile, and the last card paid is tagged as under it
* Mr. Hollywood's tile and its "only to overpay" note are readable on a phone, and a picked helper or overpay-only tile keeps its name beside its order tag
* Back out now refuses what Undo refuses: a random discard or any draw, reveal or look can no longer be re-rolled or peeked at by backing out of a choice
* A stunned minion no longer makes its Quickstrike attack: the stunned card is discarded instead, as for any other attack.
* A "choose one" no longer offers an option that has no valid target, such as removing threat when no scheme has any; an ability whose every option is like that cannot be used.
* Cards whose threat removal is written with a condition (Mark V Helmet, Crisis Averted, Impede, Natural Flight, Giant Help) can no longer be played or used with no threat on any scheme; Photon Speed can, for its change of form.
* "Remove threat from each scheme" with no threat on any scheme is refused the same way on every card (Hyper Thrusters now agrees with The Douglass).
* Looking for Trouble and Defender of the Nine Realms can no longer be played with no threat on the main scheme, which put a minion into play for nothing.
* Rapid Deployment can no longer be played with no threat on any scheme: its bonus is a second removal of threat, so nothing could come of it.
* Ten cards were missing a printed scheme icon and now count it: hazard on Venom, Symbiote Suit, Kree Command Ship, Vandarian Power Stone, Guild Business and The One with the Breakup; acceleration on Public Outcry, Build the Case and Jormungand; crisis on Team Leader.
* Kang's four obligations (Weakened, Stolen Memories, Depowered, Time-Travel Hijinks) and Master of Time's Fear of Kang are now shuffled into the encounter deck; they were never in it.
* Core's Standard and Expert sets are offered again as alternatives where a scenario prints Standard II or Expert II.
* Guardians of the Galaxy enforces its limit of 1 Team card per player, and a flipped Solid or Phoenix Force gives its printed resource.
* A "Random and blind" Civil War game no longer names the hidden stage 2 in its game log after Save & quit and Continue.
* Shift+Tab steps back one control at a time on every screen (it moved three), and the Civil War builder's Preconstructed button is a Tab stop only on the review step, where it is drawn.
* On a phone, a swipe across the Pause menu that lifts over a Quick reference row no longer opens it, and the Pause menu's Game log row (was "Jump into the log", greyed out) now shows the game log.
### Engine
* A game mode on setup (cooperative or competitive): competitive is refused with a 'coming soon' message until it is built, and card scripts can already write their 'In competitive mode' clauses
* Team membership groundwork for competitive mode: a cooperative game is one team of every seat, and a per-team player count is ready for two teams
* Per player values (villain hit points, scheme threat, hinder, uses, minion hit points, printed cost and per player amounts in card text) are read through the team of the card they are printed on, ready for competitive mode; a cooperative game gives the same numbers as before.
* Team words for card scripts: the enemy leader, your leader, your team and the enemy team, read through the team queries (cooperatively the villain, no card, and the players)
* A choice a card gives to "the enemy team" is asked of the first player, marked as the enemy team's on the prompt and in the log (competitive plan task 7)
* A game can be set up with chosen stages of its main scheme card, played in the chosen order (GameSetupConfig.mainSchemeStageIndexes), for Civil War's pick of one stage 1 and one stage 2
* A Civil War leader takes three or four modular sets, refuses a set or a main scheme stage of the other side, and turns a chosen stage 1 and stage 2 into the main scheme deck (spec 3.1a)
* wave10Scenario builds any legal Civil War scenario (leader, stages, modular sets, difficulty, game mode), playableScenario routes to it, and the scenario offer lists sides, leaders, stage options, set legality and game modes (spec 3.1b)
* A defeat effect can ignore the defeated card's When Defeated abilities (Coup de Grâce), and a defeat made by an attack-labeled ability counts as that attack
* An encounter attachment can carry a per-host limit and a host chosen by the enemy team: a character at the limit is not a legal host at the reveal, and the host prompt is the first player's, marked as the enemy team's
* A defeated minion can be given to an enemy as a facedown boost card: setDefeatDestination(boostCardOf(enemy)), from an interrupt on the defeat or from the minion's own When Defeated ability (Teamwork minions, Dangerous Recruits)
* A response can hear a card revealed that gives heroes a trait: on.cardRevealedGranting(trait) with grantedTo(trait) for the cards given it (Hunting Rebel Heroes)
* A main scheme stage with hinder, printed or gained under a condition, has its hinder placed as it turns to its B side (wave 10 spec 3.13)
* An ability limit counted for each player the triggering event belongs to: once per phase per player on a forced trigger (wave 10 spec 3.14)
* An effect resolves the Boost ability of each minion in play (Freedom Fighters), with a named option for what the icons a borrowed ability gains do (spec 3.11)
* The unique rule turns away a revealed encounter attachment that matches a card in play before it attaches (spec 3.8)
* An attack in progress records that it uses the attacker's ATK (usedStat), carried onto its damage and its overkill damage; Predicate attackUsedAtk and the usesAtk mark on attack and dealDamage (wave 10 spec 3.17).
* RuleSpec attacksLoseKeywords: attacks do not have the named attack keywords against a matching character, whatever gave them (wave 10 spec 3.18).
* A permanent encounter attachment whose host leaves play, or whose player is eliminated, resolves its printed attach instruction again and the attaching is announced (wave 10 spec 3.19).
* Random Civil War scenarios: randomCivilWarScenario(seed, options) draws side, leader, both main scheme stages and the modular sets from a seed, with pins that a re-roll keeps
* Random Civil War scenarios: randomCivilWarScenario(seed, options) draws side, leader, both main scheme stages and the modular sets from a seed, with pins a re-roll keeps
* A constant can give each matching card the printed keywords of each other matching card (Earth's Mightiest Heroes).
* A card can replace a player character's attack or thwart before it happens, whether a basic power or an attack or thwart ability (Tangled Up).
* A rule that keeps cards out of play can be limited to one player, and the refused play names the card it is printed on (Teddy Altman).
* An encounter card can answer cards discarded from any player's deck and take them while the ability that discarded them still counts them (Skrull Business).
* Engine: an additional cost to play a card, and a cost that searches the encounter deck and its discard pile for a minion and puts it into play; Hunted (Tigra) is scripted (box heroes B3.1)
* Engine: what a resource generates can read the card being paid for; Alien Physiology (Hulkling) is scripted (box heroes B7.2)
* Engine: a resource whose cost takes a chosen amount of damage as it is spent, named in the payment and offered per amount in the payment prompt; Energy Siphon (Wonder Man) is scripted (hero packs H3.14)
* An identity's separate deck of encounter cards is built at setup and owned by its player (Hercules's Labor deck), so Hercules can be seated; such a card is an encounter card no player controls, in the deck and in play
* A script can reveal the top card of a separate deck (revealTopOfSeparateDeck) and count the cards left in it (separateDeckCountOf); an obligation enters its revealer's play area and an attachment with no attach line waits out of play for its own When Revealed
* A Labor card that leaves play goes to the bottom of the Labor deck unless it goes to the victory display; one revealed with no valid target is discarded, logged as revealedCardDiscarded, and ends on the bottom of the deck (RRG 1.8 FAQ p. 65)
* A maximum on the cards tucked under a card (`tuckLimit`, Ionic Physiology's "Max 3 cards tucked here"): a tuck takes only the cards there is room for, a full card refuses the tuck and skips the text after its "Then", and an ability that only tucks there is not offered
* Deck building: an identity may take off-aspect cards by printed resource (`OffAspectAllowance.printedResource`); Wonder Man's deck may hold events with a printed energy resource icon from any aspect, in `validateDeck` and in the deck builder's offer
* A leaves-play trigger can ask where the card went: `on.leavesPlay(who, { to: "victoryDisplay" })` hears a Victory card added to the victory display and not the same card discarded (Hercules' Atonement; hero packs HT7)
* A find can choose among every matching card, in play or out, each offered with the area it is in, and can reveal the card or put it into play: `findCard` gains `choose`, `outOfPlay` and the destinations `"reveal"` and `"play"`; every deck a match could be in is shuffled (Hercules' Labor cards; hero packs HT4)
* A reveal can treat each per player icon on the revealed card as a number, for that reveal only: `perPlayerAs` on `revealCard` and on a `findCard` that reveals (Embody Pathos; hero packs HT5, question 19 = A, provisional)
* Threat that only one character's thwarts can remove: `threatCannotBeRemoved` gains `exceptThwartBy` (`onlyThwartsRemoveThreat`), which stops every other removal, encounter cards' and supports' included (Embody Pathos; hero packs HT6)
* A cost can be reduced to 0 for a player's next matching card (`reduceNextCardCost(player, "toZero", …)`), and a reduction can be bound to the cards a search found, following each card until its next play (`reduceCostOfCards`; hero packs HT8, HT9)
* A cost that heals damage from a character the payer picks (healCost), for Battlefield Benevolence: an enemy the effect could not confuse is no candidate
* An additional cost to attack one enemy (additionalAttackCost), for Brother vs. Brother: paid as a basic attack or an (attack) ability is made against it, and an enemy that cannot be paid for cannot be attacked
* A resource ability that can only be overpaid: it never goes toward the cost, is always counted as overpaid, and is offered only while a card is being played (hero packs HT11)
* A value that counts the cards in a player's hand, deck or discard pile matching a query, so an option that takes a card from the discard pile is offered only while one is there
* A collection search for an identity-specific card of an identity not in the game, and Setup abilities of player cards in play resolving at setup step 16 (hero packs HT15)
* Any player may help pay the costs on a card: a rule that opens an attached card's ability costs to every player's resources, discards and cards in play, and lets one other player take over a part such as damage, while a part naming the user's own card stays with them (hero packs HT16)
### Content
* Civil War card data: 190 cards, four leaders, sixteen main scheme stages, sixteen modular sets, four ready-made scenarios and the Tigra and Hulkling starter decks (not playable yet)
* Hercules (59001a to 59045) emitted with the Labor and Gift separate decks, Wonder Man's Ionic Physiology erratum (RRG 1.8 p. 70), both packs in the wave 10 pool as data only; the data-only pool is now empty
* Civil War: Life Model Decoy carries its Limit 1 per character, and Life Model Decoy and Resistance Fighter the enemy team's choice of host
* Civil War scripted so far: the registration and resistance main schemes, the Iron Man, Captain Marvel and Spider-Woman leaders, and fourteen modular sets
* Hercules' events (The Gift of Battle, Son of Zeus, Wisdom of Athena), Amadeus Cho, Olympus and his five upgrades are scripted
* Hercules' pack: Ancient Rivalry and Evaluate Threat are scripted (Gilgamesh waits on a conditional additional cost)
* Hercules' Leadership cards are scripted: Deathcry, Namora, Thor, Recruitment Drive and "Avenge Me!"
* Gilgamesh (Hercules pack) is scripted: playing him confuses your identity unless you have the Eternal trait, and he cannot be played while your identity cannot be given a confused status card.
* Wonder Man's Wonder Fans, Jet Belt, Signature Sunglasses and "What Are You?" are scripted (Mr. Hollywood waits on the engine)
* Wonder Man's Basic cards are scripted: Swordsman, Jarvis and Avengers Compound (also in Hercules' pack)
* Wonder Man's pack: Stronger Together, Unified Strike, Caught in the Crossfire and Disarming Defense are scripted
* Wonder Man's obligation (Pacifism) and his nemesis set with Grim Reaper are scripted
* Wonder Man's Justice cards are scripted: Firebird, Hawkeye, Scarlet Witch, Sentry, Battlefield Benevolence, Everywhere All at Once and Heroic Conditioning
* Wonder Man: Mr. Hollywood overpays for a card, and the tuck options of Wonder Fans and Signature Sunglasses are offered only with a matching card in the discard pile
* Cameo (Wonder Man's pack): its Setup finds an ally of an identity nobody is playing and shuffles it into the deck
* Coordinated Effort (Wonder Man's pack) is scripted: while it is attached to an encounter card, any player may help pay the costs on that card
* Hercules gets his printed starter deck (Hercules / Leadership, 40 cards, from the decklist card in the pack); his pack stays out of the playable pool until his Team-Up art exists
* Wonder Man gets a starter deck (Wonder Man / Justice) from the owner's stand-in database listing, unverified against the printed card; the no-precon exemption is gone
* The Civil War Standard PvP double-sided card has art on all four faces: 56128b and 56206a use stand-in scans from the other side's copy until real scans arrive
### Docs
* PLAN.md records wave 10 (Civil War, Wonder Man, Hercules) as done

## [v0.20.0] - 2026-10-13
### Added
* Undo beside End turn (or the Z key) takes back the hero's own last move this turn; it stops at the start of the turn, at another player's move, and at anything that drew, revealed or searched cards
* Paying for a card numbers each picked card in the order it will be discarded and tags the last one "on top", with a hover note on why the top card matters (Pepper Potts)
* Desktop and Android apps check GitHub for a newer release on launch and show an update banner on the Title screen, with the changelog and a direct download of their own installer, when it has one for their platform
### Changed
* Update Capacitor, Tauri, Playwright, Vite, Vitest, oxlint, oxfmt and lint-staged to their latest releases, and @types/node to the Node 24 line
### Fixed
* Decks & Collection: "+ New deck" sits on the Your decks header, always in view, instead of at the bottom of the deck list
* Backing out of "Play this deck" (Take your seats, then Scenarios, then Title) lands on Title instead of looping back to Take your seats
* Decks & Collection: the Duplicate button and "+ New deck" use the display face at a readable size instead of the 9px label
* A crowded play area keeps its cards at a readable size and scrolls sideways (mouse wheel, drag, or the arrows at its edges) instead of shrinking them under their own action labels (#119)
* On the defend prompt, an option's damage and hit point lines stay inside its card at every window size (#125)
* Desktop: links out of the game (release notes, MarvelCDB, rulebook PDFs) open in the default browser

## [v0.19.1] - 2026-10-12
### Docs
* GitHub issue forms for bug reports (platform, heroes, scenario, steps, expected vs actual, screenshots), feature requests and TESTED community deck requests

* The Board shows damage on Encased in Ice and lets it be attacked, lists a card's attachments in Inspect, and shows the combined THW for Tech-Pac's cost
* Two public MarvelCDB decks per new hero are saved and tested (import, legality, a full seeded game)
* Community decks: the tested public MarvelCDB decklists are recommended on the Decks screen, credited to their authors, with the author's notes, a last-updated date and the MarvelCDB link; preview one and add it to your decks as an editable copy
* Enchantress (Trickster Takeover) is playable: Hypnotic Gaze reverse sides stay hidden until they flip, owed Forced Actions are marked with End Turn waiting on them, and the Crown's each-player cost reads in words
* God of Lies (Trickster Takeover) is playable: the board draws the neutral area with Loki's shared dial and flip point and Worlds Collide's threat and loss, Synergy counters against their limit, the rules cards in Inspect, and the Avatar shatter in the log
* Agents of S.H.I.E.L.D. campaign in the client: the story told through the box's own comic pages, a case-file Briefing (evidence, Executive Board secrets, carried numbers, expert hit points), flip warnings and one-card Zemo wording on the setup choices, and the campaign's music.
* Guided mode: wave 9 Try-it lessons for Nick Fury (Stealth and Assault), Maria Hill (Reassignment), Silk (tucked cards), Falcon (faceup top card and Redwing), Black Panther (Inventor and Specials) and the Baron Zemo evidence and accusation
* Take your seats: the tested MarvelCDB community decks can be picked for a seat, behind a new TESTED chip and under BUILT
### Changed
* Game log lines for wave 9's events: a surge deals a facedown card, the accusation and the mole, hidden piles, the encounter deck's faceup top card, extra costs to use an ally
* Campaign Briefing: a pick shows its cards as pictures (NeXt Evolution's side schemes show the scheme, the environment it earns and the encounter card it brings), and tapping a picture opens the card in Inspect
* The Rise of Red Skull campaign finale music, The Skull Shattered, replaced with a new recording
* A held minion's chip stays on its own tile and shows Guard off; a hero in play as a minion reads as one; a collection search shows pages of card pictures
* Importing a MarvelCDB decklist that lists a linked card leaves that card out with a note instead of refusing the deck
* Enchantress: Trance of Greed and Alluring Call now require the play when the card can be legally played and paid for (a new playFromHand mustPay flag makes the payment non-declinable); the damage or fallback applies only when no such play exists
* Expert campaigns (Age of Apocalypse, Agents of S.H.I.E.L.D.): a defeated player now answers rejoin or sit out before the scenario is set up, and a player who sits out is not counted as a player of that scenario, so its threat, hit points and other per-player values are those of the players actually playing
* A defeated hero who sits the next expert scenario out now reads as sitting out, not defeated: the rejoin question opens over a table not yet set up and says what each answer means, the log and setup checklist stay honest, and the debrief and Briefing do not count the seat.
* Line endings are LF on every machine (.gitattributes, .editorconfig, oxfmt endOfLine), so a Windows checkout matches a Mac one
### Fixed
* Across the Spider-Verse can no longer be played with no ready Web-Warrior card: the exhaust is its cost
* A facedown minion (a Drone, a Controlled minion) is no longer named by its hidden card in prompts
* Firepower is one attack with up to three hits, so an attack-and-defeat response answers it once
* Discarding an ally over the ally limit (or a Restricted card over its limit) no longer repeats the prompt while that card's leaving is being interrupted
* Overkill's excess damage lands before the defeated minion's When Defeated; a villain or identity that stays in play cannot hold damage past its hit points; a spend-a-resource option must be paid in full
* Heart of the Panther resolves at least one Special when one can resolve
* A Maria Hill deck no longer reports her deckbuilding rule as an unscripted card
* Alert Level hears a defeated hero; Attacrobatics in expert mode deals back all the damage it prevented
* Citizen V's Sword and Innocent Bystanders answer only your identity's attack; A.I.M. Interference asks the player who revealed it
* The Ends Justify the Means offers only options that have a target; Baron Zemo's Sword cannot be paid for with no secret counter to remove
* A MarvelCDB decklist with a long write-up is no longer refused as too large
* God of Lies on a tablet: the neutral strip no longer covers the second row of Synergy environments; the game-over screen describes Loki, God of Lies and Worlds Collide instead of the Avatar; an Avatar shattering gets a banner, and the log names the Avatar that held the counters
* Baron Zemo's Sword can no longer be used without paying 3 physical or 3 energy, Widow's Bite stuns before it damages, and winning the Agents of S.H.I.E.L.D. campaign completes scenario 5
* Campaign screens play the box's interlude music; Extras tabs, scenario lock captions, deck rows and phone setup sheets wrap instead of cutting; the Briefing drops carried numbers already used; expert hit points read as left; MojoMania's unlock hint names MojoMania
* Wave 9 board polish: ability labels no longer show a raw [star] token, the Maria Hill chip wraps whole, the Team-Up ring clears the stats and chips, held minions keep their tags clear of the environment and their titles, God of Lies setup says the Avatar is random, Thunderbolts shows no false set-aside footer, one title on Board Member tiles, and the God of Lies swap fixture reaches a real swap
* Thunderbolt Backup's held minions seat beside the environment at a readable size, Loki, God of Lies reads as a random Avatar on the setup and scenario screens, a very long deck name steps its type down to fit, and the Unlocks sheet wraps long headings and names at phone width.
* A payment the rules make you pay (Trance of Greed, Alluring Call) says which card you must play and has no way to skip it; the log says when no card could be played, and a flipped Trance is named for the side now showing
* A hero defeated on their own turn no longer freezes the game: the turn ends and passes to the next player, a defeated identity's hit points read 0 instead of a negative number, and your own defeated seat is shown as DEFEATED instead of a live "Your turn"
### Engine
* Vulnerable: a character that becomes stunned or confused is discarded without being defeated, ahead of simultaneous damage (RRG 1.8 p. 48)
* All-purpose counters: a counter lands as the type its card defines, removal and counts can take any type, moveCounters moves a chosen amount (MC50 p. 3)
* Threat on cards that are not schemes follows no scheme rules, and removing threat can be an ability cost with a chosen amount
* Enemy scheme threat can be diverted to another card, and card picks can be capped by total printed cost
* Preparation abilities (Black Widow) as their own trigger kind, and look-at over dealt encounter cards with rearranging
* Play from the deck at a reduced cost, an in-play cost that counts the card paying it, and damage reductions that except an attack by card or keyword (wave 9)
* A rule can give a labeled ability (a Preparation) to every encounter card that prints none (wave 9 engine task 10)
* A Preparation can prevent all damage from a player's attack and report how much; an attack moved onto a guard minion now deals its damage (wave 9 engine task 11)
* An interrupt on a card face turned up by a forced interrupt in the same window is offered (Nick Fury's Assault after Break Cover); a resource ability can generate one resource per threat removed as its cost (Fury's Watch)
* An effect can repeat a counted number of times (All-Points Bulletin); a defeated card is matched by what it was when defeated, so a facedown Controlled minion's defeat is recognized
* An effect can resolve once per card of a set with that card bound (Security Cameras); a cost can discard cards tucked under a card (Cindy Moon)
* A reveal in progress can be replaced by the reveal of another card (Eidetic Memory)
* A card leaving play records whether an encounter card or a player card effect caused it (Front Organization); an event after encounter cards are dealt (Intelligence)
* A resource card's own Interrupt can add resources to the payment that spends it (Organizational Support)
* A tuck can be heard and redirected to another host, and an ability can answer its own card being discarded from under a card (wave 9 engine tasks 23 and 24)
* A card leaving play can be tucked under another card instead (wave 9 engine task 12); a card attached from outside play now enters play, with its Uses counters
* A surge deals its card (Intelligence can answer it); an encounter card effect's discard from a hand or deck can be replaced (Front Organization)
* A scenario deck whose top card is in play: the next card enters play when the top leaves, and a card put under an empty deck enters play (wave 9 engine task 13)
* A minion can be held by an environment, engaged with nobody; engagement can rotate between players; a minion already in play that engages a player triggers quickstrike (wave 9 engine tasks 14 and 15)
* A status card about to be given can be heard and replaced; counters can be removed from among several cards, and a card picked by most or fewest counters (wave 9 engine tasks 16 and 17)
* A reset can set hit points to a printed number and still announce the reset; an ally can be attached under no player's control (a hostage), and no player may use a captive ally's Action or optional Response
* Hidden piles of cards that no view, prompt or log shows until gained or revealed (wave 9 engine task 19); a random pick from the victory display
* The encounter deck's top card can be kept faceup; a cost can discard a chosen number of cards from the top of the encounter deck (wave 9 engine tasks 25 and 26)
* A discard from the encounter deck can be heard; an ability can read what a just-resolved ability bound; a boost card being given can be answered by looking at it and the deck's top card and swapping them (wave 9 engine tasks 27 and 28)
* An encounter card about to be dealt can be heard and replaced; a resource ability can be limited per card paid for; a card's constant can grant it another ability (wave 9 engine tasks 29 and 30; Flight Squadron)
* A play knows which cards paid for it; a tucked resource card can be spent as if from hand; a character can defend without exhausting (wave 9 engine tasks 31 to 33)
* A surge deals a facedown encounter card that waits for step four (22B); a revealed minion can enter play held (26B); Down but Not Out is removed from the game either way (29B)
* A flip can keep named counters through a change of card type (Board Members, owner 1A); a card swapped into a boost position is facedown there, so it flips (Up, Up, and Away)
* Accusing the mole: accuse and identifyMole over an evidence grid, with the accused, the mole and the wrong guesses readable by later abilities
* An additional cost to attack, thwart or defend with a character (Divided Loyalties); a second Max 1 per player copy cannot be put into play (37A); the unique rule's replacement card waits facedown (38A)
* An enemy made to activate by a card offers its activation interrupts; an attack that defeats a hero records the defeat; effects can answer a scheme activation that is about to place threat (Battle of Wits)
* A hero's identity card can be in play as a minion no player controls, found in the collection by its hero title (Reluctant Foe)
* Removing fewer counters than named leaves Then unresolved; a shared title goes to the card on the ability's own side; a retargeted attack ability has the new target as its only target
* Reluctant Foe counts only faceup titles (44B); Divided Loyalties also taxes an ally's labeled ability and an effect-declared defender (48B); a named card dealt to a player can be answered (Aerial Recon with Serpent Solutions)
* A count of distinct traits; an attachment about to be attached can be redirected to another host (Fixer); a combined-stat threshold on an exhaust cost (Tech-Pac); a card that can be attacked as if it were a minion and is never defeated (Encased in Ice)
* Divided Loyalties no longer charges for an ally's labeled ability (53B); Talon Line and Aerial Recon's action work; spend X is one choice and one payment (A.I.M. Interference, Battle of Wits)
* Forced Actions must be used before the turn or phase ends; a card can be attached with its reverse side hidden; a counter about to be placed can be replaced; a character with two copies of Encased in Ice can attack either
* A cost every player pays; cannot make basic attacks; linked cards set aside for a named encounter card (Captain America's Shield is now set aside); swapping the villain by trait; a cap on counters
* The pod for God of Lies (a shared villain and scheme in a neutral area, per-group values, group choices); encounter cards ready at the end of the player phase; a linked ally can enter play while the minion that names it is being defeated
* Campaign groundwork for Agents of S.H.I.E.L.D.: a card's state recorded in the log and read back, a sealed random draw for the A.I.M. envelope, evidence seeded into the final scenario, a fresh draw after a lost attempt, and searches that leave out cards you already own
* A hidden reverse side's real card id no longer survives in the sealed state's state-check keys or anywhere else a view can read
* The game state the client holds is sealed for hidden piles and the campaign log's sealed values as well as hidden reverses (the Agents of S.H.I.E.L.D. evidence envelopes no longer reach the client); engine queries and previews run on a sealed state count the piles right and stop at hidden information
### Content
* Black Panther (Shuri), Silk, Falcon and Winter Soldier: printed starter decks and regenerated card data under the Agents of S.H.I.E.L.D. cycle
* Agents of S.H.I.E.L.D. box card data: 196 cards, five scenarios, Maria Hill and Nick Fury starter decks (not yet playable)
* Trickster Takeover card data: Enchantress and Loki, God of Lies scenarios, Trickster Magic, per-group scaling in the schema (not yet playable)
* Wave 9 content exports: the Agents of S.H.I.E.L.D. cycle and Trickster Takeover join the card pool (unscripted, not yet seatable)
* Monica Rappaccini (50126) no longer carries a printed Villainous keyword; she gains it only while Scientist Supreme is in the victory display
* Wave 9 scripting: A.I.M. Science, Batroc's Brigade, Scientist Supreme, Extreme Risk and Silk's identity; Shuri's Inventor, The Elephant's Trunk and Aerial Dogfight are no longer skipped
* Agents of S.H.I.E.L.D. data corrections from the scans: Black Widow III has 20 hit points per player, Strong Inhuman's Forced Response, three text typos, the M.O.D.O.K. set name
* Disavowed (50180) has no trait: MarvelCDB had copied its set name into the trait line
* Hero pack text fixes from the scans (Infiltration, Metal Punch) and two RRG 1.8 errata as current text (Eidetic Memory, S.H.I.E.L.D. Deputy: Max 1 per character)
* Agents of S.H.I.E.L.D. campaign data: evidence icons and colors, the 27 evidence combinations, secret and lock counter types, the Thunderbolt minion map
* Trickster Takeover data corrections: the God of Lies scenario record (an Avatar chosen at setup, Loki and Worlds Collide as neutral cards), Spellbound's When Defeated, the Fading Figments' infinite hit points, charm / shatter / synergy counter types
* The M.O.D.O.K. attachments and Citizen V's Sword now name the card they attach to correctly, so they attach when revealed; Reverse Engineering carries no stray scheme modifier
* Trickster Takeover: the God of Lies scenario lists its two reference cards (Shatter the Illusion, Epic Multiplayer Reminder)
* S.H.I.E.L.D. Executive Board set scripted (Board Members, their attachment faces, A.I.M. Interference)
* Wave 9 scenario builder sets aside permanent cards and Trickster Magic's linked allies, keeps double-sided back faces out of the deck, and starts God of Lies with its villains set aside
* The S.H.I.E.L.D. Executive Board set is an extra, uncounted modular set
* Baron Zemo scenario frame scripted: the villain's four faces, the three main schemes with the accusation, the evidence as hidden cards
* Baron Zemo's encounter cards scripted: his Sword, the S.H.I.E.L.D. Agent, Undermine Support, Might Makes Right, The Ends Justify the Means
* Divided Loyalties' additional cost to attack, thwart or defend with an ally
* Battle of Wits scripted: spend mental resources to prevent Baron Zemo's scheme threat and remove secret counters
* Reluctant Foe scripted: a hero from your collection enters play as an Elite, Villainous minion; the Agents of S.H.I.E.L.D. box is fully scripted
* The Techno and Whiteout Thunderbolt sets and Strength in Diversity are scripted; the Thunderbolts scenario draws from all ten sets again
* Enchantress scenario frame scripted: the villain, both main schemes, Future of Despair, and the Hypnotic Gazes with their hidden Trances
* Enchantress scenario fully scripted; Trickster Magic and its four linked allies scripted (one ability held back); both stay gated until two engine fixes land
* Trickster Takeover data: Love Triangle attaches to an ally you control, Future of Despair starts set aside, linked cards name a title and a card type
* God of Lies scenario frame scripted (Loki and Worlds Collide in the neutral area, the Avatars and Shatter the Illusion, the Synergy environments); Enchantress scenario games
### Docs
* Wave 9 (Agents of S.H.I.E.L.D., four hero packs and Trickster Takeover) handoff page
* Epic Multiplayer plan for Loki, God of Lies (multiplayer phase), with the groundwork wave 9 builds now
* Music prompts and titles for every scenario and campaign slot with no track
* A plan for an optional on-device helper (move suggestions, deck rating), to follow the Fear No Evil wave

## [v0.18.0] - 2026-10-09
### Added
* Wave 8 QA: Nightcrawler's precon game (eleven seeded games against Rhino with the Crazy Gang and two against Unus, every log replayed)
* Wave 8 QA: Bishop's precon game (six seeded games against Unus and four against Rhino, every log replayed)
* Wave 8 QA: Iceman's precon game (fifteen seeded games against Rhino with Sauron and against Unus); it pins that Frostbite is not set aside after its host activates
* Wave 8 QA: Magik's precon game (fifteen seeded games against Unus and Rhino, 91 plays from the faceup top of her deck checked)
* Wave 8 QA: Jubilee's and Magneto's precon games; the test driver now declares wild types, pays for a form change and spends up to a chosen number of resources
* Client wording for wave 8's prompts and log events (Cell Phone's basic power prompts, wild-type declaration, the form-change payment, villain row, deck-top reveal); a form change with an extra cost opens the payment bar
* The board lays villains out in the order of the villain row when a game has one (the Four Horsemen)
* Wave 8 QA: a rulings pass over the cycle's cards (26 tests); it pins that a card flipping in place into an ally skips the unique check (Mutants at the Mall beside hero Jubilee, Enemy of My Enemy beside hero Magneto)
* The Age of Apocalypse campaign definition (five scenarios, missions and Overseers drawn per attempt, carried results, rewards counted toward deck size, expert hit points); not yet listed in the client
* QA: an attack bonus lands once on Repulsor Blast's single damage instance; a sweep of every attack card with an additional damage clause found none scripted as a separate instance (Q53)
* Wave 8 QA, custom decks: every new aspect and basic card played from a Core hero's deck, illegal-deck tests for the wave's deckbuilding rules, the deck builder's start state for six heroes, and one public MarvelCDB decklist per hero imported and played; it pins that Iceman decklists import without his six Frostbite
* Wave 8 QA: the five scenarios played as whole games (standard, expert, two players, every setup option) and the wave's QA page; it pins that the Four Horsemen's simultaneous defeat asks two meaningless next-active-villain prompts
* Wave 8 is playable in the cards package: six heroes, five scenarios and thirteen modular sets, with the Gene Pool threat option, a side per Horseman, Apocalypse's easier start and Standard III for any scenario on plain Standard
* Client view models for the mission area, playing an ally to the mission, pairing cards with characters, Inspect notes for the hit point floor and considered icons, and wording for the rest of the wave's prompts, log lines and rules
* Wave 8 in the client: the six heroes and five scenarios are offered on Title, with hero and villain art in place; an Age of Apocalypse row on the unlock path
* Table setup offers the standard set (I, II, III), the Gene Pool threat, a side per Horseman and Apocalypse's easier start; the Four Horsemen no longer offer an Extreme difficulty
* The board draws the mission area, asks where an ally goes when it can be played to the mission, and pairs discarded cards with characters for a mission attempt; Inspect shows where a mission card is and the hit point floor
* The Age of Apocalypse campaign is on the Saga shelf: five issues with a mission and an Overseer drawn for each, the mission area on the board, the Dossier's mission table, rewards and Desperate Measures between games, the comic reader
* Guided mode for Age of Apocalypse: 37 glossary entries, 19 tips, How this works notes on about 70 cards, a Try-it lesson for each of the six heroes, and the New in Age of Apocalypse page
* Magik's faceup top card shows on her deck and can be played from there, once per phase for 1 less; her lesson teaches it
* The campaign deck editor lists rewards with a switch to leave one out or put it back; clearer titles for where an effect-played ally goes and for rejoining an expert campaign; log lines for a canceled attack and counters returning as a scheme advances
* `pnpm test:affected` runs only the unit tests your unpushed changes can affect, by the same rule the pull request jobs use.
* The choose-a-target sheet shows each scheme's threat bar and each enemy's hit point bar on its tile, with the effect's removal previewed as a hatched part on the pick
### Changed
* The e2e pre-push hook is back on the wave 8 branch for the final client pushes
* Age of Apocalypse rewards: a reward is not one of the 40 cards and is one of the 50; picking one is required, putting it in the deck is a choice; every eligible upgrade and support is offered; Desperate Measures counts toward neither limit; a defeated hero may sit a scenario out and not pay to rejoin
* Set the Table: the Standard and Expert set chips sit in the difficulty row, the Horsemen side chips are full size, and the Apocalypse easier start is a Briefing toggle in the campaign (off by default)
* Prompts and log lines for the new payment rules: which resources paid, overpaying a chosen-size cost, dividing an instructed basic power, and nemesis sets held back in The Wrecking Crew
* Set the Table scrolls as one page on tablets and short windows instead of squeezing its panels; the Dossier scrolls and stacks on phones and tablets; campaign and Decks controls are full touch size
* `pnpm test` runs every package's tests in one Vitest run with a shared worker pool, instead of one package after another (about 16% faster).
* The pull request and main unit tests run as parallel jobs beside a typecheck job, one per wave of the cards package plus up to three for everything else; jobs the changed files cannot affect are skipped, and the Netlify job only builds and deploys once they all pass.
* The release and desktop-build workflows run the same parallel tests stage as pull requests (one shared tests workflow), so their build and release jobs no longer run the suite themselves.
* Each test job heads its report on the Actions summary page with what it ran ("Wave 1 card tests", "Core engine and card data tests") instead of "Vitest Test Report".
### Fixed
* The basic Colossus ally prints the subtitle "Piotr Rasputin", so it can no longer be played beside the Colossus hero, with or without the same-name table option
* Same-name prompt: the browser test follows the Colossus ally's printed subtitle
* Defy Danger discards the top card of the encounter deck, as printed, not of the player's deck
* Frostbite is set aside after the attached enemy activates, so Iceman's six copies come back to be used again
* A card that would flip into a unique ally beside a same-named character in play no longer does (Mutants at the Mall beside hero Jubilee, Enemy of My Enemy beside hero Magneto)
* A character whose hit point bonus ends with damage at or above its hit points is defeated (RRG p. 22)
* A MarvelCDB decklist that omits identity-set cards MarvelCDB lists separately (Iceman's six Frostbite) imports as a legal deck, with a note saying what was added
* Villains defeated together are defeated in one step: the Four Horsemen's last blow wins the game with no next-active-villain prompt, and a survivor takes the active counter only after all the defeats resolve
* The wave 4 and wave 5 scenario builders honor a chosen standard or expert set from any box
* You Got This! and Sisterly Bond add the stat in use; Freeze! and other basic-defense responses answer a defender declared by an effect; a Setting environment picked at Absorbing Man stays in play
* A campaign game that is won but loses the campaign goes to the campaign-lost screen; views that follow a villain's stage also hear a stage revealed by an effect
* Two e2e specs follow this wave's table setup and cost-pick wording (Mojo's Longshot tile is scrolled into view; Rock, Paper, Scissors names its cost pick)
* Title and cover art is fetched once per picture even when its screen closes mid-load (no more 'Texture key already in use'); the campaign smoke spec confirms the chosen volume before opening it
* An attack-labeled ability that only deals damage begins its attack as it starts resolving (RRG p. 26); Mikhail Rasputin's Mission Response deals its damage to one chosen ally as one instance (RRG p. 20); an effect that plays an ally offers the mission area (MC45 p. 5)
* An attack ability whose only attack target cannot be attacked cannot be played (Attacrobatics, Swift Retribution, By Any Means, Return the Favor, Browbeat, Fighting Fit, Pivotal Moment, Payback under guard); a cancelled attack ability deals no further damage; a main scheme's counters are returned when it advances, which fixes Magneto's magnet counters
* The Wrecking Crew: each hero's nemesis set is set aside, so Face the Past can find the minion (FFG ruling, January 17, 2026)
* Board: Apocalypse's main scheme shows its real threat target, the four Horsemen tiles show their side, statuses and attachments, and counters on a scheme (the Pyramid) are drawn
* Campaign: a lost run reads as lost on the Saga shelf, Cover and Dossier; the Briefing says which cards start in play and which are in the deck, and lists penalties carried over from missions
* Set the Table totals the Horsemen's hit points for the chosen sides and Expert, scenario select shows the real acceleration, and the Decks screen shows its paste field and scrolls to an imported deck
* An ally in hand offers its destination (your area or the mission) instead of its in-play ability's cost; cards offered from set-aside areas and events being played show their faces; hand chips give the real reason a card cannot be played
* Minions that print hit points per player (the Overseers and Prelates, The Sleeper, Garm, Skurge, Nidhogg, Black Swan, Surprise Contender, Dragon, The Kraken, Ronan) now scale with the number of players (RRG 1.8 p. 32)
* A character told to use a basic power can pay that power's own cost (Wonder Man's discard); the engine decides which set-aside and resolving cards are visible; the ability cost check no longer reads a bookkeeping-only change as payable
* Expert campaigns: a player defeated in a won scenario must rejoin (and pay) at the next one, in Age of Apocalypse, NeXt Evolution, Mutant Genesis and Mojo Mania; Scrying's chosen card is drawn
* An attack redirected in its 'when you attack' window now lands on the new target for every attack card shape (Psionic Illusion against Repulsor Blast and similar)
* Magik's deck-top pile no longer offers a play off turn, hand chips fall back to a neutral reason, the pair sheet wraps and scrolls on a phone, and the Briefing's right column scrolls when it overflows
* Guided mode: glossary, tips, hints and Try-its match the current rulings (attack timing, overpaying, Grounded, Settings, Magneto's discard count); tips that fire during the villain phase now show afterward
* The pair sheet scrolls again, Horsemen tiles show their status under the stats, a minion shows which hero it is engaged with in a multi-hero game, and choice sheets no longer let taps through to the board
* When a main scheme stage advances, each card attached to it is discarded (RRG 1.8 p. 27): Under Surveillance, Overwatch, It Ain't Over
* "After [enemy] attacks you" answers for the player whose character defended when another player's hero or ally defends (RRG 1.8 p. 16)
* A boost or constant ability that says "you" during a defended attack means the defending player (RRG 1.8 p. 16)
* A response window stays open after you use one response: the others are offered again until you decline (Colossus's Steel Skin no longer forfeits Perseverance).
* The last campaign issue's aftermath beats now play before the Finale (NeXt Evolution, Sinister Motives, Mutant Genesis)
* Expert campaign hit-point restore in The Rise of Red Skull and The Mad Titan's Shadow now runs before scenario setup, so damage dealt during setup is no longer overwritten
* Game over lists its rounds in order and closes with why the game ended
* Indirect damage shows only as many tiles per card as there are points to assign
* The board marks encounter cards dealt facedown with a Facedown pile and its count
### Engine
* A script can raise a named moment that other cards answer (raiseMoment, on.moment)
* Finding and revealing a card that is already in play: a minion engages the finder and keeps what is on it; a side scheme or attachment stays where it is
* A card in play can be dealt to a player as a facedown encounter card, and a dealt card can be passed to the next player; the reveal step repeats until no dealt card remains
* An ability can ask whether a card of a given type was among the cards that paid for it (paidWithCard)
* A rule can keep the top card of a player's deck faceup for everyone, with a log line each time the showing card changes
* A constant can make the top card of a player's deck playable as if it were in hand, at a reduced cost, once per phase
* A payment records how many resources and which resource types paid; the player declares a wild resource's type when a card reads it
* An ability can cost a chosen number of resources within a range, and its payment's wild types are declared the same way a played card's are
* An attack-labeled ability is one attack: every instance of damage it deals to enemies is damage from that attack, retaliate answers once per surviving enemy after the last instruction, and 'after this attack' waits for the whole ability (Q47)
* An additional cost to change form (formChangeCost): the turn's change carries a payment, a player card's change-form effect asks for it, encounter-card changes are free; Jubilee's Grounded now charges 2 resources of the same type to change to hero form
* Every ability labeled (attack) is one attack even when it only deals damage (Q48); guard is checked for each enemy an attack ability targets as it would be attacked (Q49); attacked targets are tracked apart from damage recipients, so overkill victims are not attacked and each targeted enemy is (Q50)
* discardDeckUntil can bind every card it discarded (bindAll), and a raised moment can carry named slots to the abilities that answer it (raiseMoment carry, read as moment.<slot>)
* A card can have a chosen player make a basic attack or thwart with a bonus (basicPowerBy, canUseBasicPower); Jubilee's Cell Phone is registered
* Engine pieces for five held cards: a target chosen by the ability label it prints (printsAbility), attach hosts by classification, a cost that readies a card, upgrades that can attach to an ally, and a deck-discard cost of a chosen size
* Villains in a random row with the active counter passing to the next in the row (Four Horsemen, Q5); setup options applied after step 11 (Gene Pool's extra threat); a flipped card's event names the player who flipped it; setup step 11 resolves before step 12
* A character can be considered to have at least 1 hit point (defeat held off until the floor ends); a card's Forced Response can be resolved as a cost or effect with no attack made; lasting effects can run until the next villain phase begins
* A villain's next stage can be revealed by an effect with no defeat; the printed hit point numeral is readable; named abilities can be ignored by a rule
* A Special can be resolved as a cost with the player choosing among several Setting environments; a villain can change form with no reveal step; an attachment's Forced Interrupt can be resolved as if its condition were met
* A scenario play area: cards in play that no player controls, closed to other cards' abilities unless an ability reaches it; allies can be played into it uncontrolled and blank
* Mission attempts: discarded cards paired one each with characters by resource icon, a damage pool dealt one enemy at a time, a card that cannot be discarded, a cost reduction for the next ally played to an area, a resource icon a character is considered to have; a side scheme held at no threat is defeated when its protection ends
* A title can be barred from entering play for a game; a card found at campaign setup counts toward the starting hand; a card's other face can be named by a query; a campaign's random draw can differ on each attempt at a node; a granted card's deck-size rule is data
* An attack's damage bonus applies to each qualifying instance of the attack ability and never twice to damage marked additional (Q53); a window before an identity changes form; hand-discard costs in response windows; last-known target state for consequential damage; surge granted to another card; an interrupt completed while its window is open is still offered
* Small engine pieces for the last held wave 8 cards: a card-attached trigger, cards drawn bound for an effect, a cost pick's host and stats, a chosen damage cost, a remaining hit points query, an enemy attack's total ATK, a mid-reveal card given as a boost card, a thwart-in-progress predicate, several return-to-hand picks, a permanent upgrade answering its former host leaving play
* A basic power reports the stat that powers it (a thwart made with ATK is ATK) and bonuses to that power follow it (Q54); a character made the defender by an ability is making a basic defense (Q55); a main scheme's B side has no live abilities until the scheme turns to it (Q56)
* Tests for the Doctor Strange 'Pool community deck (MarvelCDB 34506): legal import, its four lines of play, and proof it cannot loop
* An attack ability on an older card begins its attack as the ability starts resolving, so 'when you attack' answers before its other instructions (RRG 1.8 p. 26)
* Owner rulings: Generation X on a divided thwart, Panicked Refugees revealed and replaced on draw, overpaying a chosen-size cost, a prompt for tied payments only when it matters, Grounded charges for an encounter card's Action, Cell Phone lets a divider divide
* Removed the unused returnedToScenarioDeck log event and its client log line.
* Card effects that count boost icons (Hex Bolt, Taskmaster, Crossbones' Machine Gun and the rest) now open the boost-icon counting window, so Chaos Control and Scarlet Witch's Crest can change those counts
* An obligation's "you" is the player holding it for a state-check condition and its resolver, for "only you may trigger", and for who dealt its damage or removed its threat (the defeating player); a crisis icon still does not stop an obligation's own ability
* A divided threat removal ("remove N threat from among schemes") adds the card's threat bonus once to each scheme given a share (RRG 1.8 p. 19; FAQ Shrink #11, p. 59)
* A card played into a scenario area (playCard.into) may now be an upgrade or a player side scheme, as well as an ally or a support; an event is still refused, with its reason.
