## [v0.17.0] - 2026-10-07
### Added
* Extras: an Artwork entry for loose pictures in art/extras, always open (first one: Psylocke and Angel)
* Villain artwork for the five NeXt Evolution scenarios (held in the pending folder until the scenarios join the pool)
* NeXt Evolution campaign cover art
* Settings are remembered between sessions and are part of data export and import
* The NeXt Evolution heroes (Cable, Domino, Psylocke, Angel, X-23 and Deadpool), scenarios and modular sets are playable
* The NeXt Evolution campaign: five scenarios in order, a player side scheme chosen for each and the environment it earns, the Morlocks you save, Hope Summers's damage, and the expert campaign.
* NeXt Evolution campaign story: five issues told through the rulebook's comic pages, and an intro page for each of the box's five scenarios
* Guided mode: ten NeXt Evolution glossary entries (player side schemes, per-player costs, the 'Pool aspect, Specialists, Angel's three faces, Psi-Knife, Hope Summers, Actions on another player's turn, Routed, Setup attachments) and a tip for each as it comes up
* Break Time can be answered on the board: a minutes stepper with quick picks for the "How many minutes were you away?" prompt, by pointer, touch, keyboard or gamepad
* Guided mode: a "How this works" note in Inspect for twelve NeXt Evolution cards with easy-to-misread wording, and a 'Pool page in the aspect guide
* In a game with two or more seats, a waiting player can use their Action abilities and Action events during another player's turn: an Act button on their row opens their hand, and Back returns to the turn in progress
* NeXt Evolution campaign is playable: pick each issue's player side scheme in the Briefing (scheme, environment and encounter card open in Inspect), see what carries in and any fixed set, and follow the six schemes and tallies in the Dossier
* Three Try-it lessons in How to play: Psylocke's Psi-Knife and Psi-Katana, Angel's three faces, and Cable's player side schemes with their limit
* Inspect shows a per-player cost as its scaled price with the printed rate under it ("3 per player × 2 players"), and Play carries the scaled price
* Cards under an environment are drawn: defeated villains under Routed show as a small faceup fan with a count, each opens in Inspect, and they no longer sit in the villain row
* Try-it lessons for the 'Pool aspect (the Dreadpool set it brings) and X-23's Specialists
* A card another player controls that any player may use (Plot Convenience) shows as a chip on that player's row; tap it to use it
* Morlock Siege battle music: Beneath the City
* Morlock Siege villain-wins music: Teeth in the Tunnel
* Morlock Siege heroes-win music: The Morlocks Stand
* On the Run battle music: The Hunt for Hope
* On the Run villain-wins music: Iron Monolith
* On the Run heroes-win music (Iron Ascent) and Juggernaut battle music (Head of Steam)
* Juggernaut villain-wins music: Crushed Beneath Momentum
* Juggernaut heroes-win music: The Unstoppable Falls
* Mister Sinister battle music: The Essex Experiments
* Mister Sinister villain-wins music: The Curator's Waltz
* Mister Sinister heroes-win music: Shattered Genome
* Stryfe battle music: Tomorrow's Tyrant
* Stryfe heroes-win music: Break the Timeline
* Stryfe villain-wins music: A Future Stolen
* NeXt Evolution campaign finale music: Hope for Tomorrow
* NeXt Evolution campaign interlude music: Regroup at Graymalkin
### Changed
* Campaign: a card struck from the campaign changes what a deck holds, not its minimum size; a deck left at 39 (an identity-set ally held under Find the Prisoners included) needs one legal card added to reach 40 before the next issue opens
* Pull requests run a lint and format check and the Netlify workflow, which now typechecks and runs the unit tests before it builds and deploys; the e2e suite no longer runs in CI and is required locally by a pre-push hook (pnpm e2e:verify runs the same gate)
* The Playwright suite runs with eight workers locally
* Card art is stored and shipped as WebP, which takes the bundled scans from 665 MB to 207 MB and every installer down by about 450 MB; `uv run scripts/fetch_card_art.py compress` converts any scan that arrives as PNG or JPEG.
* Break Time now runs a real wall-clock break timer: End break reports the whole minutes away (no cap), with Enter minutes instead as the manual path
### Fixed
* Board: every environment beside a lone villain is drawn at any window size; tiles wrap into rows and shrink instead of being dropped
* Board at short heights: readable pile labels and counts, other-hero rows that no longer overlap, and compact environment tiles; a multi-villain board never drops an environment
* Board labels wrap instead of ending in an ellipsis (attachment chips and strips, long ability names, villain stage line, environment names), and the villain's ATK/SCH badges stay apart with the +1 chip clear of the number
* Busy board at 1280x560: four encounter piles as legible rows, hero and villain stat badges and HP on one line, status tags on one row, hand tags wrapped to their own card, minion HP plate no longer cut by its strip
* A press behind the villain-phase recap (and Pause, and the tutorial round debrief) no longer reaches the table: it could open Inspect, switch the phone tab or play a card
* Playing a hand card with several legal hosts (Energy Transfer) names the card and the question on the target sheet, and every play path asks through one step, with tests
* Campaign: an identity-set ally struck from the campaign (Find the Prisoners) is no longer demanded back as a missing identity-set card
* Game Over names the card behind a card-caused loss (Robert Kelly leaving play, Operation Zero Tolerance, The Champion's ratings): the card in the Final blow box, a short cause line, and its full text one tap away in Inspect
* Rogue's Skin Contact no longer offers a host that cannot have Touched attached (Robert Kelly); new query field canHaveAttached
* Campaign briefing: a deck the engine would refuse is marked on its row and blocks Open issue with the reason beside it, before the press
* Campaign briefing: every seat's deck opens from the briefing (deck rows, or a seat chooser), and a deck edit keeps the calls already answered
* Wolverine's Healing Factor is not offered when he has no damage to heal
* Mutant Genesis dossier: the Log no longer lists hidden bookkeeping, names roles and removed Future Past cards in plain words, The World stops repeating labels, and Heroes shows each hero's role with a wrapped caption
* Mutant Genesis briefing and board: setup-card and captive/struck-ally rows say what is true, the deck footnote and run-screen result fit, the main scheme shows its printed stage, the heal option names its acceleration token, and the aftermath tags what was logged
* Campaign briefing: deck problems read with their own true label (a size problem names the card count, a removed or identity card never reads as size)
* MojoMania comics: issue #1's beats show only their own panel, long speech-bubble tails in issues #2 and #3 are capped, and the Longshot caption clears his hair on a phone
* Pause lists keep status words, no developer text, wrapped status line; Inspect follows the face showing, facedown piles show their size, landscape art is not letterboxed
* Game log: unique-card discards say which card and why, consequential damage is named, card flips, counters, swaps and a Blizzard blank get lines
* Game over: no points or unlock banner under unlock-all, the banner wraps and clears the villain card, no dead replay button, final blow shows the face in play, Overkill excess counts once in the damage stat
* Team-Up circles and rings show both characters' heads: each pair can carry a crop spec (center, zoom, closeup or full picture), set for Iron Man and War Machine, Ant-Man and Wasp, Cyclops and Phoenix and a few more; the Team-Up splash waits for the round banner instead of showing it through its scrim
* Playing a card that goes under any player's control (Uncanny X-Men) now asks Whose play area? with a tile per hero, the asking seat first, on the desktop and the phone, instead of a clipped bar on the hand strip
* A card whose cost exhausts or discards cards in play with a choice (Mutant Peacekeepers, Strength in Numbers, Team Strike) now asks which cards, with a running count and a live X, and sends exactly those instead of the engine's first pick
* Table setup counts the shared encounter deck of Tower Defense and Sinister Six; Settings rows toggle on a press anywhere and ON wears a visible frame
* Prompts and sheets: response windows say response, a payment sheet separates Pay from Don't use it, the end-of-turn discard preselects nothing, divide sheets name each scheme, the offered ability reads first, and a covered sheet is inert
* The villain-phase response window with two or more optional triggers makes its buttons toggles with one confirm, so using one no longer forfeits the other
* The villain-phase response window reads the offered ability first (Steel Skin) and points at the card when its text is cut
* Phone board: counter and effect strips wrap, a granted keyword (Hurricane's Retaliate 1) shows on the character panel, and tab badges count distinct changed cards instead of climbing events
* Target and pick sheets: tiles size to their text, the phone heading and source wrap, the touch hint says Tap, and Why not the others sits under the row where it covers no board card
* Card art: a scan still downloading when its overlay closed no longer lands behind the next scene's own load of the same card, which logged 'Texture key already in use'
* MojoMania's aftermath no longer soft-locks when a hero controls no card to record: that hero is settled as "Nothing to record" and Confirm moves on
* MojoMania's Dossier lists no working fields, shows each recorded card once in plain words with the genre sets checked off, cites its own page on a rewind, and lists recorded cards on Heroes
* The Dossier Log's next-issue box grows with its wrapped promise, so a phone never cuts it
* A tap that straddles a redraw, or begins in the frame after one, still lands on touch: a scene tap router judges a press by where it went down and came up (buttons, tabs, rows, target and pick tiles), and a finger no longer triggers the target-tile hover redraw
* Team-Up splash closes itself 2.5 s after the picture shows on the wall clock, so a device under 5 fps no longer holds the whole table's input for a minute while Phaser's smoothed frame time crawls
* A quick tap on a card or button is judged by the browser's event times, not by when a slow frame got to it, and hand, table and choice cards finish a press the redraw ate through the same tap router as buttons
* The villain-phase recap, Pause and the round debrief take a press the instant their shield is drawn, so a press in the frame after one of them redraws no longer falls through to the table behind
* An ability that must choose a card cannot be started when there is none to choose (Domino's swap with an empty hand or deck no longer spends its use)
* A flipped card's resource icons are those of the face showing; a card that cannot flip is no longer offered as a flip choice; "you" in a trigger's source or target filter on an attachment means the attached player
* An event with two Action abilities triggers exactly one: Wasp Sting deals 4 as Giant-Woman and 5 as Wasp instead of 9 or being unplayable
* An unowned upgrade taken into play is attached to its taker's identity; a unique card already in play is no longer offered; an event's in-hand ability is heard (This Card is Fire); placing 0 threat opens no window
* A facedown card attached to a card in play is out of play (RRG p. 23): it is not counted or targeted as a card you control, and Armed to the Teeth can swap its hidden weapon
* The Merc with the Mouth keeps your allies exhausted and stops other players' player-card abilities during your turn; an attachment's Action belongs to the attached card's controller; a response reads its target as it took the damage; a borrowed card returns to its owner's discard pile
* Mulligan now discards your whole hand before you draw a new one, even when it holds more cards than your hand size
* Regenerative Longevity is a plain Action, so Laura Kinney can play it in alter-ego form
* Telekinetic Wave returns the upgrade or support to its owner's hand, not the revealing player's
* Dreadful Deeds counts a player whose only 'Pool card in play is a player side scheme such as Live Dangerously
* Attack cards and abilities no longer let you pick an enemy that cannot take their damage, and cannot be played when no enemy can be damaged (Back to the Future, Ultron's drones, Out of Reach and similar)
* On the Run: a modular set's card that starts the game attached to the villain (Flight, Super Strength, Telepathy, the Power Stone, the Infinity Gauntlet) now begins in play on the villain the scenario picks, instead of being discarded before the game starts
* Table setup lists the Dreadpool set a 'Pool deck adds, and Morlock Siege and On the Run name their villain as The Marauders, drawn at random, instead of a placeholder
* Git Gud reads whether your last finished game was a win, from your saved games; it used to play free every time
* Thwarting a scheme with assault previews ATK, not THW, including when a normal scheme is also a target
* Hidden in the Clutter stays attached when a stun replaces its attack
* Temporal Leap, Blackout, Beat Cop and Storm (Phoenix) can no longer be used when the threat they would move cannot leave its scheme; before, the cost was paid and nothing moved
* Deck sharing: the app's text export writes a title shared by several cards with its code (1x Cable (44002)) and the importer reads that suffix, a pack, or the deck's own hero and aspects to pick the card, so every exported deck re-imports as itself; the MarvelCDB importer now knows the 'Pool aspect
* Deck builder: Cable's off-aspect player side schemes can be added (the list now asks the engine's per-card deck rule, with a few words saying why), Linked and other never-a-deck-card cards are not offered, Psylocke's precon reads 40 cards + 2 permanent, cards the deck holds stay removable after an aspect switch, per player costs read "3 per player", saved decks name their aspect, the aspect is spelled 'Pool everywhere, search matches traits and type, and the pack, wave and sort steppers join the keyboard route
* The macOS release now builds one installer each for Apple silicon and Intel instead of one universal installer, which carried the game's art and music twice and grew past GitHub's 2 GiB limit on a release file.
* Loki (Thor's nemesis) is healed instead of defeated when the card he discards is a treachery: the discarded card's type is now read in the discard pile
* Wave 7 QA fixes: a finished Try-it offers Back to lessons without conceding (and no lesson game counts as a result), the guide scrolls an off-screen hand card into view, NeXt Evolution rulebook and ruling cites read right, the stack panel and Game Over debrief use plain words, a defense lockout is named, restricted-discard and Break Time sheets say why, whole-page scenario intros, Angel's form button fits, Psylocke's lesson hand is stacked, and the aspect dev route accepts 'Pool
* A defeated card whose When Defeated is still resolving can no longer be defeated a second time or chosen as the target of a defeat, so Lady Deadpool as a 'Pool-ized minion no longer asks for her own target forever
* Merging to main no longer runs the Playwright suite while preparing the release pull request. The release job's own commit and push skip the repo's git hooks, whose pre-push gate ran the whole e2e suite and failed the release.
* Hidden in the Clutter makes one attack only (owner ruling 2026-10-07): it no longer triggers again while its own Forced Interrupt is resolving, so a defender's retaliate lands on the attached enemy instead of restarting the attack until the defender was out of hit points; a trigger can now be marked notWhileResolving
### Engine
* Player side schemes show their printed scheme icons, count for scheme-icon totals, and leave play with an eliminated player's other cards
* A player side scheme can be put into play by an effect, with the same placement, controller and starting threat as when it is played
* The player side scheme limit: one for one or two players, two for three or four; over the limit a scheme is discarded undefeated
* Assault: an ally thwarting an assault scheme takes its ATK consequential damage, and a divided basic thwart that includes an assault scheme uses ATK
* Per player printed costs on player cards (Team Investigation, Break Time): one reader for a card's printed cost, scaled by the players who started
* A When Defeated that names 'the player who defeated this scheme' still resolves when no player did, with no player as its source
* A villain's defeated last stage can be placed under another card and counted there; a villain still in play is never tucked
* A 'shares a title with' card query and a general 'not' for card queries, on the unique rule's title comparison
* A card can say card abilities cannot remove it from play: moves and outright defeats from abilities do nothing, damage and game rules still remove it
* The main scheme records what advanced it (a completed stage or a card's effect), and a stage can ask
* Tests for an attachment or obligation blanking an identity's text box on both faces (traits and printed stats kept)
* An enemy attack as the cost of a player's ability; the ability is not offered while that enemy cannot attack
* A scenario whose villain is put into play by the main scheme's Setup: every villain starts set aside and the one brought in resolves its own Setup and When Revealed once
* A double-sided encounter card can flip and reveal its new face: its When Revealed resolves and a side scheme face gets its starting threat on top of what the card held
* An encounter-set ally the first player controls passes to the next first player when its controller is eliminated, instead of being discarded
* An ally whose base THW and ATK come from its controller's hero, and the loss when a required card leaves play now also fires when its controller is eliminated
* A trigger for a status card being placed on a character, announced by every path that gives one (effects, costs, toughness, re-given statuses)
* Main scheme decks can remove random alternative stages and shuffle one stage number's group (wave 7 §3.28)
* Damage rules that depend on the attacker or the attack: cannot take damage except from an attacker or attack card with a trait or a ranged attack, damage reduced per attack except for an attacker with a trait, and attacks that ignore retaliate
* A value for the number of cards of the most common type in a player's hand, counting the six player card types
* A way to name the player an enemy's attack is against, whoever defends it
* An effect that has a player choose a card type, with later parts of the ability reading the chosen type; the choice mechanism is a general choose-from-a-list
* A villain held at zero hit points by a card is defeated at once, with no defeating player, when that protection ends; triggers for a scheme's last threat being removed and for a named villain stage's defeat
* An attachment's conditional attach instruction as an ability that resolves when the card enters play, not stopped by canceling its When Revealed
* A rule on one card that makes other cards enter play exhausted, applied at the one point every card enters play
* A per-phase record of which characters attacked and thwarted, and a condition over it
* The victory display as a place effects can move cards to and bring them back from; a side scheme returning from it enters with its starting threat, unrevealed
* A constant ability that applies while its card is in the victory display, for the card's owner
* A permanent side scheme at zero threat is not defeated; a side scheme returning from the victory display gets its hinder
* A scheme can say only one named character may remove threat from it; supports, non-character encounter cards and removals with no player behind them still can
* A trigger for a card discarded from the top of a player's deck, heard from the discard pile; a card its response takes away is not counted by the ability that discarded it
* A rule that counts a printed resource icon more than once on cards discarded from the top of a player's deck; a card discarded as a cost now carries its icons to the effect
* A trigger filter for an attack that has a keyword, and 'Max 1 per attack' for events played from hand: a copy at the maximum is no longer offered or paid for
* A player side scheme nobody controls: thwartable by anyone, kept through eliminations, flipped to an environment when defeated; a crisis icon now stops threat removal sourced from such a card
* Campaigns: a retried scenario reuses a recorded choice, and a query for the damage on a card at the end of a game
* A card query for a printed cost of N or more, read after per player scaling
* A minion whose own When Defeated attaches it to an ally: it stays a minion, keeps its damage, is never defeated again, never activates and leaves with its host
* A rule that stops a card being flipped, a flip as an ability's cost, and the restricted limit checked after a flip without ever discarding a permanent card
* A player's attack can be redirected onto a friendly character, keeping its damage and keywords; a query for a card printing a named resource type, with wild as its own type or as any type
* An identity with two hero faces can be told which one to change to; a plain form change from a hero face of a three-face identity asks which other face
* A card query for whether a character has room for a stunned, confused or tough status card, sharing the decision the engine makes when it gives one
* A scheme icon printed on one face of an identity counts only while that face is showing (Archangel's acceleration icon)
* A rule under which an attack's boost cards are ignored: turned up and discarded, adding nothing and resolving no Boost ability, without being canceled
* An effect can send a played event back to its owner's hand once it has resolved, instead of to the discard pile
* Linked cards are set aside at setup for each deck that names them, owned by nobody until a player takes control (RRG p. 27)
* An encounter set can be included by a setup condition: the Dreadpool set joins the game when a player chooses the 'Pool aspect
* Card scripts can read the acceleration tokens on a card and the encounter icons in play
* Resource cards can generate a computed yield, a cost can be a chosen amount of damage to yourself (0 allowed), and a cost can spend a computed number of resources
* A card can count as more than one restricted card (Laser Swords), and a computed resource cost is priced correctly when an event is played as a response
* A computed resource cost can read the attack that triggered it, and overkill excess from an attacked ally is dealt to its controller
* A card effect can search the collection for a card and add a new copy to the game (Armed to the Teeth)
* Facts from outside the game (previous game result, minutes away, talked this phase) enter as setup input or a recorded answer, never read by the engine itself
* A trigger can answer either of two events, and "you" on an attachment or obligation no player controls means the player it is attached to (RRG pp. 8, 30)
* A basic-power bonus now applies to a basic recovery (Probability Field)
* An upgrade put into play faceup is attached as a play would attach it; a standing condition can apply as its card enters play; a thwart records the schemes it thwarted
* E.V.A. is discarded immediately when Fantomex is not in play, before any response to her entering play (NeXt Evolution FAQ)
* A player may trigger an Action ability, or play an Action event, during another player's turn (RRG Player Turn); basic powers, form changes and playing other cards stay own-turn only
* Card scripts can tell indirect damage and which basic power is in use; a stat bonus equal to another stat no longer overflows; a status card can only go on a character in play
* Target validity now judges an attack effect's damage: an enemy that cannot take it is not offered, and the ability cannot be initiated when no enemy can (RRG 1.8 Target, p. 43); a stunned character may still use an attack ability with no valid target (p. 41)
* A campaign scenario can name the modular sets it requires (CampaignNode.requiredModularSetIds): the game start hands them to the builder, campaignModularSetIds adds them to the picked sets, and createGame refuses a campaign game without one; NeXt Evolution's Juggernaut requires Black Tom Cassidy (MC40 p. 14)
* Restricted is a state limit: a restricted card can be played or put into play over the limit, and its controller then chooses which restricted cards to discard down to the limit (RRG p. 38); Armed to the Teeth's swap and a change of control are checked the same way
* A move of threat needs a source its threat can leave (RRG p. 30, owner ruling 2026-10-06): an ability whose moveThreat has no such scheme (a crisis icon, a threat-cannot-be-removed rule, no threat) is not offered and is refused before any cost, with its own reason; a chosen source offers only such schemes; new canRemoveThreatFrom predicate
* A "would" interrupt resolves before the same event's other interrupts are gathered (RRG 1.8 "Would", p. 48; owner ruling 2026-10-06): no ordering prompt between Hope's Captor and a Marauder's "attacks you" interrupt, and a replaced attack or scheme never opens the later tier. Opted in: every scripted "would attack" and "would scheme" replacement
* 0 damage opens no damage window (owner ruling 2026-10-06, RRG p. 14 "any amount of damage"): damage whose amount is 0 as it would be dealt, or that an interrupt prevented in full, has no "would take damage" window, no "after ... takes / is dealt damage" responses and sets off no "each time" effect; a tough status card is kept; the attack itself still resolves ("after attacks", "after defends", retaliate)
* A "(defense)" ability used after the Declare Defender step was declined makes the hero the defender with no DEF subtracted and no second prompt, and the attack no longer reads as undefended afterward (owner ruling 2026-10-06, RRG pp. 14-15); 18 tests pin each ordering
* An attack's response reads its target's status cards as the attack damaged it (owner ruling 2026-10-06): Cypher draws on a killing blow against a confused enemy
* An effect that instructs the play of an event with an Action ability may play it outside a player's turn (playFromHand.ignoreActionTiming; owner ruling 2026-10-06): Warpath plays a Hero Action event in the villain phase
* "You" in a constant ability is read through one resolver by every rule reader: on an obligation, an attachment on a player card or an engaged minion it names that player in every rule's queries, stat modifiers, keyword and trait grants; a boost card's own text (I See You's +1 boost icon) reads the player the activation resolves against
* "Would be defeated" replacements resolve in the earlier "would" interrupt tier (owner ruling 2026-10-06, RRG 1.8 p. 48): they resolve before any "is defeated" interrupt with no ordering prompt, and a replaced defeat never reaches Spider-Tracer, Priority Target and the like; 16 abilities marked, and MaGog, Collector and Odin's Torment now declare their replacement so the later tier stays closed
* Defending is one player's per enemy attack (RRG 1.8 pp. 14-15): once a player resolves a (defense) ability or has a character defending, other players' (defense) cards are not offered and their characters cannot be declared, with a readable reason; a (defense) ability followed by a basic defense by the same hero is one defense, announced once
* Pinned with tests that a card being played is never offered as a payer for itself, for a play an effect makes in the villain phase (Warpath), one on the player's turn, and an ordinary play; a second copy of it in hand is a payer like any other card
* Setup step 11 searches the encounter set-aside area as RRG 1.8 p. 51 reads (Q20 = B): a set-aside setup-keyword card begins in play unless the scenario's own text keeps it aside (setAsideUntilCalled: Mister Sinister's Superpowers, the Milano at Escape the Museum, The Hood's set-aside sets) or it is a campaign's supply copy (the Norn Stone); the Milano now enters by its keyword at Brotherhood of Badoon, Nebula and Ronan, with the same board
* "After X deals damage" fires after a tough status card and after a prevention alike (owner ruling 2026-10-07, RRG pp. 35, 44): a damage event carries the amount dealt and the amount taken, responses read one or the other (on.damage's dealt and taken, enforced by the validator), and Power Stone, Challenge Accepted and Schadenfreude read damage dealt; every damage trigger is classified in docs/dealt-vs-taken-audit.md; 0 damage as dealt still opens no window

* American spellings throughout
* MojoMania: a declined Longshot is shuffled into the encounter deck, and any seat may be the player who reveals him
* A look-at in a hot-seat game with more than one seat waits behind a cover naming the looking hero until they tap to reveal
* The Team-Up panel shows the pair's picture in a circle
* Scenario select titles a tile with the scenario's name when it differs from its villain (Mansion Attack, Project Wideawake, the Museum pair), with the villain leading the line beneath
* Counters on a card read as '3 ratings counters' on the Board's character panels, and Inspect lists them
* The Team-Up ring moves from the top bar to the hero panels of the seats providing the pair, is larger, and shows the supplied closeup uncropped
* The Colossus Try-it rings Titanium Muscles' own tile in the payment row and explains it
* The campaign seat picker's hero rows show the hero's picture and the same colored aspect badges as Take your seats, with the deck source
* MojoMania's setup text and options read as plain language: Longshot's decline says he is shuffled in, the expert heal says Don't heal, and the internal "Not printed" notes are rewritten
* The Team-Up ring is a keyboard focus stop, the Team-Up panel scrolls when tall, and the Other heroes row keeps its HP line beside a ring
* Take your seats: Recommended is the first shelf of the hero list, drawn like the pack shelves (same tile, same header with Hide/Show), with its caption wrapped to two lines, a quieter Team-Up tag, and folded by default on a phone.
* Sign the roster asks about cards that can't be played only when you press Sign & open issue (no standing notice), and a hero's own card on that sheet has just Keep as a resource
* MojoMania's opener bubbles sit closer to their speakers, and a placeholder panel keeps its bubbles inside its frame
* MojoMania issue #2's third beat uses a stand-in picture until the hallway art is regenerated
* Team-Up ring and pair marker show whenever both characters are in play in any form (quiet and dashed until both show the hero side); the splash opens at the start of the first turn; the panel, hand tag and Inspect name the form still needed
* e2e: CI runs two Playwright workers per shard (three starve the software-WebGL runner)
* Team-Up: a yellow needs-hero-form blurb on the hero holding a present pair up replaces the long ring label and the second hand tag; the Team-Up keyword text says both characters must show their hero side
* Table setup offers every modular encounter set from the packs you have open, the scenario's recommended ones first and the rest grouped by cycle; the grid scrolls inside its panel and the phone folds each group
* Table setup: the Kree Fanatic print-and-play set is its own opt-in (official print-and-play content, off by default); Experimental Weapons is a normal modular pick except at Crossbones
### Fixed
* Campaign cards removed from the campaign (spent campaign cards, allies lost to Zola) now leave hero decks automatically after the game, win or lose, instead of waiting for the player to delete them
* Scenario and modular-set obligations (Warn the Others, Mojo's sitcom and crime sets) now belong to their encounter set instead of an empty one
* Wrapped in Metal's thwart restriction is scoped to the identity; M-Type Sentinel's boost scripted (giveBoostCard allowed in a Boost when it names its enemy)
* Shadowcat's Permanently Phased now emits a when-revealed ref for its unheaded mass form flip (mut_gen 32055)
* Bullets under a triggered lead-in ("if you are in:", "choose:") now stay part of that one ability instead of emitting phantom constant refs
* Card scripts re-keyed to the single ref the parser now emits for a header plus its bullets (no phantom per-bullet constants)
* off-aspect-allowance test reads Cyclops and Phoenix from WAVE6_CARDS now that they left the data-only pool
* Granted cards (role-building, Aspect Advantage) no longer count toward a hero's equal-cards-per-aspect deckbuilding requirement
* Mutant Genesis campaign: removed Future Past cards and held captive allies stay out of later scenarios, and a defeated expert seat skips Victory steps
* A card removed from a campaign no longer returns in the next scenario's composed encounter sets
* Board, log and text fixes: dashed target threat shows as a dash, icon tokens in card text, damage log names its source, truncated labels and villain titles
* Mutant Genesis briefing: long role-building lists wrap and page inside the panel, the narrator shows the box cover when a hero portrait is not filed, and a pending choice no longer reads as composing
* Briefing: recorded values read once in plain words, same-named role-building cards say which product or aspect, the setup summary labels villain HP as across all stages, and the Temporary keyword ability reads as its reminder text
* A quickstrike granted by another card (The Mojo Files, The Brotherhood, Symbiotic Berserker) now makes the minion attack as it engages a hero.
* An overkill granted to enemy attacks by a constant ability now spills, and the defend preview shows it; Wild Wild Mojo grants it to the attack, not to each enemy.
* A basic attack can no longer target an enemy that cannot take damage from it (ruling Mar 19, 2026): the target is not offered and the attack is refused instead of dealing 0.
* An obligation's "you" is the player whose play area holds it for a card leaving play (Mojo in the Middle draws for the player who discarded an obligation), a blanked text box (Family Matters) and the ally limit (The Odd Couple, which now forces a discard down to the reduced limit)
* The Search for Spiral reveals for the player who used its own Hero Action: a threat removal names the player who removed it (the thwarting player, or the player who used the ability, whatever card it is on), and a side scheme defeated that way names that player as the one who defeated it
* The Search for Spiral: when an encounter card's forced ability removes its last threat, the first player reveals the show deck's top card (owner decision Q64)
* A player-scoped "threat cannot be removed" rule now stops that player using an encounter card's own action; crisis icons still do not affect abilities on encounter cards (RRG p. 14, owner decision Q66)
* MojoMania: Set the table and Scenario select say what each scenario really does with its genre sets, and the setup log no longer says a set-aside reveal was dealt
* Wave 6 board polish: one-line Mojo encounter-sets cell, Mojo's genre sets shown as information, set-aside line no longer shrinks the encounter piles, legible threat count on the main scheme, hero identity history names the face it showed
* An upgrade attached to a card another player controls is controlled by that player, by every attach route, and back to its owner off that card (RRG p. 31)
* A take-damage cost is not paid when the damage is prevented (RRG p. 14)
* Berserker Barrage repeats only if Wolverine takes all 2 damage
* A played event goes to its owner's discard pile, not the discard pile of the player who played it (RRG p. 31)
* An encounter card drawn from a player's deck stays in hand when it has its own 'after this card enters your hand' ability (Misled in a Rogue game)
* Steel Fist is an attack (RRG p. 68 erratum)
* The Board's show deck panel (Spiral) shows the deck and its count and never draws a discard pile slot for it
* The phone Board's Enemies tab shows environments (MaGog's crowds with their ratings counters) in a strip under the villain
* The Shadowcat Try-it names Kitty Pryde's action button the way Inspect shows it
* The Try-it lessons for Shadowcat, Gambit and Colossus word their prompts the way the board shows them
* A piercing attack now discards tough status cards even when all of its damage is then prevented (Bulletproof Belle, Shadow and Steel), and does so before any "would take damage" interrupt.
* How to play: the New in each box rows line up with the two equal columns above them
* Mutant Protectors announces only the ally as the defender, and the hero takes over the defense (not a basic one) if that ally leaves play before damage (RRG 1.8 FAQ p. 63).
* The Injured Senator's "When Completed: Defeat Robert Kelly" now resolves before the final stage's loss, and Robert Kelly refuses every player card as an attachment, not upgrades alone.
* A guide step no longer appears while the villain-phase walkthrough is still auto-advancing; it waits until the board is waiting for the player
* Tests pin that a game saved during setup resumes with the deal and mulligan applied exactly once
* The campaign Aftermath offers each hero their own recorded-card list (MojoMania) instead of the first hero's, shows the card's cost, and uses wording for recording a card rather than the Hydra TECH note
* The Pause menu's rules tiles size to their text, so long entries like Permanent and Stalwart no longer run out of their tile
* Mutant Genesis' role choice is no longer drawn as a genre-set call: the box's roles decide whether a pick of ids that also name encounter sets is a role
* On a phone, Decks & Collection's card pool heading is no longer covered by its filter chips and the MarvelCDB link field's placeholder fits
* Mutant Genesis' composed briefing no longer lists its role picks as genre sets you chose
* Take your seats keeps showing the hero just seated in the detail panel while the next empty seat is being chosen
* Genre-set tiles fit their status label on a phone (Checked off, Checked off · Reusable)
* Throg's response is only offered when you are engaged with a minion
* Try-it and tutorial steps that point at a zone (the identity panel, the villain) now ring it where it is drawn, not under the guide rail
* Mutant Genesis's briefing no longer shows an internal "(Not printed: ...)" note: its setup steps read in plain wording
* Table setup for Mojo no longer says the first set-aside genre set joins at setup: one joins at random, as the card says
* Two flaky e2e specs: the Shadowcat overlay check reads the overlay and the step in one instant, and the concede test retries a Concede tap the sliding Pause sheet swallowed
* Inspect says what a resource ability gives in plain words (1 physical for each tough status card on Colossus) instead of 'follows the table'
* Phone Saga shelf: every volume row is a keyboard and gamepad stop that scrolls itself into view
* Mutant Genesis briefing: role-building reads as one sentence per hero (Colossus (Brawler) added Get Over Here! to the deck for this game)
* Campaign briefing on a phone scrolls under the pinned action bar, so the Decks panel is reachable and shows while a call is pending
* Longshot's choose-a-player sheet asks who reveals him, and a long sheet title no longer runs under the seat name
* The Board's set-aside panel wraps the genre set names onto as many lines as it needs instead of cutting them with an ellipsis.
* An identity's ability button wraps its name onto a second line (Skin Contact, Weather Control) instead of cutting it with an ellipsis.
* An imported deck keeps the decklist's own name (Stolen Thunder!), a bare card name in a pasted list counts as one copy, and a line that cannot be read is reported by line number instead of dropped; long deck names wrap in the deck list.
* The Mutant Genesis briefing reads role choices as sentences: "Colossus took the Brawler role." and "Colossus (Brawler) drew Brazen Defense as a role upgrade."
* The e2e suite waits on states instead of fixed delays and its Shadowcat check reads the overlay and step in one instant, so a slow CI runner no longer fails it
* The e2e workflow runs in four shards with a Chromium cache, and the e2e suite is documented with its repeat and throttle rule
* The campaign seat picker's headings are compact rows, a hero with four aspect badges wraps them to a second line, reasons wrap instead of ending in an ellipsis, and its Team-Up tag is outlined instead of solid red.
* Table setup on a phone wraps the modular sets summary ("...one joins at random at setup") onto a second line instead of running off the screen.
* The phone card sheet's Play button prints what the card costs right now (Colossus with a Mutant or X-Men hero shows Play 3, not the printed 4)
* Take your seats: the Team-Up tag on a hero tile reads in paper-white on its opaque ink plate over bright art
* Two scenes asking for the same picture at once load it once (the second waits), which ends the occasional "Texture key already in use" in the e2e runs
* A declined role-building pick reads "Colossus added nothing to the deck this game" in the briefing, and scheme and environment ability strips wrap to two lines instead of ending in an ellipsis
* e2e: the Storm deck-import test presses Play and Deal again until the next screen is up and walks intro, mulligan and setup choices to a calm first turn, so a slow runner no longer strands it
* Playing a card with several legal hosts or cost picks (Rogue's Energy Transfer attaches Touched to a character other than Rogue) now asks which one on the board instead of taking the first.
* Every choice that shows one player cards from a deck (Gambit's Thief Extraordinaire look-and-discard, deck searches) now waits behind the privacy cover in a multi-seat game, not only look-at prompts.
* A game lost to a card's own text (Robert Kelly leaving play) no longer reads as The scheme wins on the game-over screen
* A MojoMania loss by The Champion's ratings (and any other card-scripted loss) names the card that ended the game instead of reading as the scheme winning.
* Wolverine's Claws no longer reads "take [object Object] damage" on its Inspect button: a computed damage cost says "take damage"
* Run it back no longer shows the previous game's moves under "This card, this game" in Inspect
* Seat cards' clear button shows its cross instead of an ellipsis, a small stat badge's ribbon widens to its own name (THW is not cut to TH...), and a button's right-hand value sits flush right
* MojoMania issue 1 opener: every beat is framed to the reading area so no speech bubble is cut, the opening beat shows the building caption with Mojo's announcement, and a phone reads the same panels in halves.
* Phone Saga shelf: the volume list rests on whole rows (none half under the header) and shows a fade and a scroll-for-more cue while volumes continue below.
* Short labels wrap instead of ending in an ellipsis: ability strips step the font down before cutting a word, attachment chips take a second line, counter and damage lines on a character panel wrap, the aftermath effect line takes two lines, a name drops its parenthetical before being cut, and Take your seats keeps its title whole on a phone.
* A choice sheet whose cards would overlap into slivers on a phone (discard to hand size, the weather pick) lays them out as a grid of whole, tappable cards.
* The guide's collapsed completion note on a phone fits its whole sentence in two lines.
* The log names the attacker as the source when Robert Kelly takes an undefended attack's damage instead of the hero (it read 'Robert Kelly took 5 damage from Robert Kelly').
* Deck Check: card names wrap to two lines and the rules strip holds two whole lines instead of ending in an ellipsis, and the type filter chips wrap to rows wide enough for Upgrade and Resource.
* The guide rail's title wraps to two lines (dropping the kind when needed) instead of ending in an ellipsis, and card thumbnails' names (the glossary's Basic Attack and friends) wrap to two lines.
* The order-these-effects sheet keeps its cards where they are (a pick gets its number, nothing relays out) and an ability caption on a card wraps to two lines instead of being cut.
* Inspect says why Psychic Manipulation is not offered while a crisis icon is in play or a patrol minion is engaged (you cannot thwart the main scheme).
* The Phoenix Try-it no longer dead-ends when a Firebird pays for the other one: it says so and carries on to what Unleashed means.
* An interrupt that changes a player's form as the villain would activate (Colossus's Armor Up) now changes the activation: the villain attacks the hero instead of scheming.
* Piotr Rasputin's Aspiring Artist is no longer offered when his discard pile holds no Colossus card to shuffle.
* On a phone the hand row (and the payment strip's hand cards) shows a fade and a chevron on the edge where more cards continue.
* A tap that lands while a screen redraws (Keep all while the hand deals, Play on Take your seats) is no longer lost: a rebuilt button carries on the press its twin saw.
* A tap that lands while a screen redraws (card art arriving on How to win) no longer loses its release, so Start the fight and other buttons still fire
* The e2e suite no longer clicks once and hopes: clicks settle first and repeat when they changed nothing, waits are on states, and fixed sleeps are gone
* e2e: card faces on the look-at sheet and text-field clicks are waited on by state, not read after a fixed pause
* The round 2 player phase band no longer stays held forever after a slow villain phase walkthrough closes; e2e reads durable records of splashes and bands and caps its settling waits
* e2e: per-test time ceilings leave room for the slow CI runner (the Mutant Genesis campaign run takes 2.6 minutes there)
* e2e runs with motion off (prefers-reduced-motion), so no slide, fade or walkthrough auto-advance has to be caught mid-flight
* e2e: the remaining 60 second test ceilings leave room for two workers on the CI runner
* The same-name sheet no longer says Replace when every listed card is a hero's own
* An alter-ego interrupt in the villain phase (Jennifer Walters' I Object!) names and pictures the alter-ego side instead of the hero side
* Board: the schemes column never draws outside its panel on a short tablet: a compact main scheme row when that fits, otherwise the main scheme stays pinned and side schemes scroll (wheel, touch, focus), and minions get room beside a lone villain instead of vanishing
* Core, wave 1 and cycle 1 scenarios build with a modular set from any other box (one code path instead of the picker workaround)
* MaGog accepts a modular set from any box, as its card allows
* Ship Command's Milano starts in play in any scenario that uses the set as a modular set
* Ship Command's Exhaust the Milano options (and Cut the Power's boost) are not offered when no ready Milano can be exhausted
* Modular picks are checked in every scenario builder: own, Standard/Expert, nemesis, campaign, repeated, other scenarios' sets and zero-modular scenarios are refused, and the picker offers exactly what builds
* The Infinity Gauntlet set builds its Infinity Stone deck in any single-villain scenario, not only in Mad Titan's Shadow
* Ronan the Accuser: any card titled so counts as in play for Bring the Hammer Down, and a unique encounter card revealed beside its match is discarded (RRG Unique Icon)
* A player card that names the villain lets its player choose any villain in play when there are several (constant effects keep meaning the active villain)
* Tests: Mansion Attack stage walks use a villain no Brotherhood minion shares a title with
* Table setup lists the Infinity Gauntlet at Tower Defense as disabled (needs one villain) and no longer offers the Campaign Challenge set as a modular pick
* Modular matrix QA: the staged pool-gap workaround is gone (every pairing builds directly)
### Engine
* Identity off-aspect allowances may omit maxCards for no limit; Scott Summers admits X-Men allies from any aspect
* Wave 6 schema: Campaign.roles, Scenario.setAsideCardIds, and a withoutAttachmentNamed qualifier on the yourIdentity attach host (honoured by the engine's host check)
* Encounter cards with the staysInHand rule stay in the hand when drawn from a player deck (no deal, no replacement draw), their hand-active forced response fires, and a hand discard sends them to the encounter discard pile (wave 6 §3.10)
* New cardEntersHand trigger event: "After this card enters your hand" fires however a card enters a hand, not only on a draw (wave 6 §3.10)
* Wave 6 scaffold in @mc/cards: wave6 registry, coverage guard and wave6Scenario for the mut_gen and mojo scenarios
* Who may trigger an ability: trigger.triggerableBy on actions and optional interrupts/responses (X-Mansion, Protect the Senator), playersWhere in the DSL, and a Hero/Alter-Ego form gate on an encounter card's optional ability reads the player it is offered to
* Verification tests for wave 6 §3.21 (Mansion Attack villain sequence), §3.22 (Shadowcat mass form) and §3.24 (Future Past deck); §3.24 buildScenarioDeck over set-aside cards is an expected failure
* RuleSpec cannotBeHealed: a matching card heals nothing from a player card (or from anything, without bySource), logged healBlocked; moving damage off it and a basic recovery or heal cost on a locked identity are blocked too (wave 6 §3.12)
* modifyAttack noBoost: an interrupt withholds every boost card from the activation in progress (Master Mold, wave 6 §3.15)
* Main scheme stages shuffled at setup: shuffleMainSchemeStages stores a seeded MainSchemeState.stageOrder that the default advance walks (wave 6 §3.18)
* Main scheme stages go to the victory display (addMainSchemeStageToVictoryDisplay), counted as main schemes by victoryDisplayCount (wave 6 §3.19)
* Teamwork (trait) keyword: a minion entering play engaged activates against its player if another minion with the trait is in play, before its When Revealed (wave 6 §3.1)
* countersPlaced trigger event: 'After you place a [type] counter', one event per placement with its amount, and on.countersPlaced in the DSL (wave 6 §3.2)
* maxSustainedDamage constant: a cap on a character's sustained damage (Magneto's side schemes); capped damage is neither taken nor prevented, logged as damageCapped (wave 6 §3.3)
* Per-phase damage cap: maxDamageTakenPerAttack gains per: "phase" with a per-instance damageTakenThisPhase tally (Nimrod), capped damage logged as damageCapped; DSL maxDamageTaken (wave 6 §3.4)
* An ally attached to a card and controlled by no player (Robert Kelly, Odin) is an ally and character in play: lethal damage defeats it and player cards can target it, but it is not a friendly character (wave 6 §3.75)
* A card can lose a keyword (KeywordGrantSpec.loses, DSL losesKeyword): printed or granted, losing beats gaining, wave 6 §3.13
* Cannot recover: RuleSpec cannotRecover refuses a player's basic recovery (Wrapped in Metal), DSL cannotRecover, wave 6 §3.14
* Give a chosen card as a facedown boost card (giveBoostCard.card, Master of Magnetism): it resolves in the enemy's next activation before its normal boost card, even under noBoost (wave 6 §3.16)
* Resolve a card's When Defeated abilities on demand without defeating it (resolveSpecials trigger whenDefeated, DSL resolveWhenDefeatedOf); the player who defeated it is the resolving player (Zeal for the Cause, wave 6 §3.17)
* Permanent cards are set aside before setup step 1 (RRG 1.8 p. 32, wave 6 §3.74); step 11 sweeps them for Setup, and Vision's and Spectrum's Setups take their forms from set aside (DSL putIntoPlayFromSetAside)
* statusDiscarded response event: one per status card discarded (tough used, piercing, stun/confuse spent, effects, stalwart), sharing one window per step; DSL on.statusDiscarded (wave 6 §3.5)
* Discarding a status card as a cost (AbilityCost.discardStatus, announced as statusDiscarded with cause cost); removeStatus gains bind (how many were discarded) and count (wave 6 §3.6)
* A numeric tough status limit (statusLimit max a number; Colossus holds 2 tough), largest rule wins; DSL statusLimit(status, max, target)
* keywordIgnored response event per guard/patrol minion or crisis-icon card an attack or thwart got past, announced after it; DSL on.youIgnore (wave 6 §3.8, Q6)
* A status card a constant ability keeps giving (RuleSpec keepsGivingStatus, DSL youAre): White Queen keeps the engaged identity confused, re-given between frames after a thwart spends it (wave 6 §3.9)
* An ally with an encounter card back (BaseCard.cardBack, Longshot) changes control, never owner, when revealed: it leaves play to the encounter discard pile and can be revealed again; moveCards.assignOwnerTo makes a card a player's for the game (home and log); DSL revealSetAside (wave 6 §3.71, Q14, Q41, Q42)
* buildScenarioDeck takes an opt-in from: ["setAside"] so a campaign can build a scenario deck (MC32 Future Past) from set-aside cards; existing callers still read only the encounter deck (wave 6 §3.24)
* A timing window's candidates are fixed as it opens (forced and optional together), so an ability the forced tier turns on is not offered for that occurrence; EventPattern.subjectIs and DSL on.youAttackOrDefend() for 'after you attack or defend' (wave 6 §3.79)
* Identity-scoped cannot thwart/attack/defend: cannotThwart.thwarter, cannotDefend read with the speaker's context; Wrapped in Metal, Permanently Phased and Fear of Kang restrict the identity only, allies unaffected (wave 6 §3.77)
* encounterCards topmostOnly: "the topmost X in the encounter discard pile" takes only the first match; Sentinel Mark VIII (32114) scripted, Master of Magnetism (32151) uses it, and Zola's Experiments (04124) no longer attaches every Tech attachment in the pile (wave 6 §3.76)
* A divided damage now takes the played card's damage bonus (Aggressive Energy) once per enemy that takes a share (ruling June 25, 2026 (2); wave 6 §3.80)
* cannotChangeForm exceptSource "self": Permanently Phased's own When Revealed flips the mass form to Phased while its "cannot change mass form" still blocks every other source (wave 6 Q49 = A)
* modifyAttack.extraDamage: "this attack deals N additional damage" for a player attack in progress (basic, ability or event); Coup de Grâce 32176/32181 scripted (wave 6 §3.29)
* Rules can lower, raise or prevent an ally's consequential damage (from its attack, thwart or either, optionally on what the attack did); Group Assault, Rescue Operation and Cannonball scripted (wave 6 §3.31)
* modifyAttack preventDamage: prevent up to N of an attack's damage, set at initiation, applied after tough and announced as prevented; Brazen Defense 32178 scripted (wave 6 §3.81)
* Campaign query cardsInEncounterDeckAndDiscard, distinct cardList appends and CollectionFilter.notInOwnDeck; MC32 Future Past Victory record and role-building's "does not already include" authored
* Temporary keyword: each card in play with temporary (granted counts, lost exempts) is discarded to its owner's discard pile by an engine-owned forced interrupt as the round ends, logged as keywordResolved (wave 6 §3.26)
* takesFirstTurn rule: Field Commander's player takes the player phase's first turn (read as the phase begins), the first player token and every other player-order sequence unchanged, DSL takesFirstTurn(you), wave 6 §3.27
* PlayRestrictions.maxWithTrait: "Max 1 TRAINING upgrade per ally" / "Max 1 TEAM card per player" counted by trait, parsed by the pipeline (33015, 34016, 32013, 32043 re-emitted), enforced on play, effect plays, put-into-play hosts and legalActions (blocked host with its reason), wave 6 §3.28
* applyRuleUntil until endOfPaidFor: a resource ability's rule lasts while the ability or card it paid for resolves, so Ruby Quartz Visor's 'that attack gains piercing and ranged' touches only that use (wave 6 §3.30)
* modifyBasicPower useStat thw: a basic attack made with THW and its THW modifiers, no ATK modifier, still a basic attack with the ATK field's consequential damage; DSL useThwInsteadOfAtk for Befuddle 33033 (wave 6 §3.32, Q22)
* EventPattern sourceAbility: an attack event names the ability that made it (sourceAbilityId, carried into branches), so "When you use your 'Optic Blast' ability" hears only that ability's attack; DSL on.attackFromAbility (wave 6 §3.84)
* An optional interrupt or response whose cost can't be paid is no longer offered: an ability in play is checked against everything the player could spend, and an event played in the window against its printed cost plus its ability's cost (Full Blast with Cyclops exhausted, Nightcrawler with no energy; wave 6 §3.84)
* An ability's resource payment is for the ability's own card unless its cost picks one (useAbility, interrupt/response payment, the payment query), so a generatesFor resource can pay for it and binds paidFor; Ruby Quartz Visor 33003 scripted (wave 6 §3.30)
* EffectSpec modifyConsequentialDamage: a one-shot signed change to an ally's pending consequential damage from its current attack or thwart, logged as consequentialDamageModified; Dust 33012 scripted (wave 6 §3.31)
* A player attack reports the attacked character as slot attack.target (on the attack and to its consequential damage) whether or not it dealt damage; Coordinated Attack 33016 scripted, rules as written for a defeated host (FFG ruling Feb 8 2026) (wave 6 §3.31)
* ValueSpec stat.printed reads a character's printed stat with no modifiers (a dash or star reads 0), DSL printedStatOf; wave 6 §3.33
* RuleSpec cannotActivate: a matching enemy's attacks and schemes do not begin (villain phase, effects, quickstrike, teamwork), logged activationBlocked; DSL cannotActivate; wave 6 §3.34
* modifyAttack removesThreat: a scheme activation removes its total from the scheme instead of placing it, as the player card's removal (a crisis icon stops it); DSL modifyAttack({ removesThreat }), wave 6 §3.35 and §4.1 Q17
* modifyAttack damageTo: an enemy attack's damage (boost included) is dealt to a chosen other enemy as attack damage from the attacker, which is not attacked (tough absorbs it; no piercing, overkill, retaliate or prevent budget); DSL modifyAttack({ damageTo }), wave 6 §3.36 and §4.1 Q18
* RuleSpec schemeThreatDestination.scheme takes a TargetRef: a matching enemy's scheme activation places its threat (boost included) on the named scheme while it is in play, else on the main scheme (Dark Phoenix, "if able"); DSL schemeThreatOn, wave 6 §3.37
* RuleSpec gainsIcon.loses: a matching card shows none of an icon, printed or gained, while the rule is active (losing beats gaining, RRG 1.8 "Loses" p. 27); gainsIcon also takes amplify (CardIcon), counted by amplifyIconsInPlay and iconsOn; DSL losesIcon, wave 6 §3.38 (Consume the World)
* modifyStatUntil / grantKeywordUntil / grantTraitUntil until { kind: "nextBasicPower", powers }: the grant waits, doing nothing, for its target's next basic attack or thwart, applies to that use and ends with it (the first such power ends both of Psychic Kicker's bonuses; an (attack)/(thwart) ability or a defense neither gets nor consumes it; a still-waiting grant ends with the phase; logged lastingEffectRetimed); DSL modifyStat(..., { nextBasic }), wave 6 §3.39 and §4.1 Q23
* Counter costs can name another card (spendCounters.target takes a TargetRef, DSL removeCounter(..., { from })); a cost's counter removal is announced (response only) so 'after the last counter is removed' answers it before the paid-for card; Psionic Bond (34001a) scripted
* iconsOn returns 0 for a card whose icon is lost or blanked, as its doc says
* The basic recovery's healing is its own event (basicRecovery), with basicPowerUsing before it, so an 'instead of healing damage' interrupt (Death Factor) replaces only the healing
* Damage a player card says you take (takeDamage, dealDamage taken) gets no 'that event deals N additional damage' bonus such as Aggressive Energy
* Playing a card chosen in an ability's cost, remembering what played it: AbilityCost.chooseCard, damageSelf as a value, playFromHand.card/via/whileResolving, Predicate playedVia; Wolverine's Claws and Lunging Strike scripted (wave 6 §3.42)
* A lasting bonus for basic attacks against one enemy: attackInProgress.basic, and a lasting stat change's amount read per affected card (slot 'affected'); Jubilee scripted (wave 6 §3.43)
* A facedown identity separate deck with no discard pile that stays empty (Storm's Weather deck): built at setup, its cards return to it facedown instead of any discard pile, hand or deck (logged returnedToSeparateDeck), never reset, and shuffleDeck { separateDeck } shuffles it after a search (wave 6 §3.46, Q26)
* swapCards: swap an in-play card with an out-of-play card per RRG 1.8 p. 42 (different titles leave/enter play, same title transfers, out-of-play exchange; wave 6 §3.47)
* A consequential-damage reduction (Coordinated Attack) still applies when the attack defeats the card that grants it (wave 6 Q50, FFG ruling Feb 8, 2026 (1) intent)
* modifyConsequentialDamage creates consequential damage for an ally whose value is 0 (Havok), player attacks read modifyAttack's atkBonus, and TargetQuery.statCompare compares a card's stat with a value (Mirage)
* A constant rule's while on an uncontrolled card (an obligation) is read with the player whose play area holds it as you, so form predicates work there
* Predicate revealedFromEncounterDeck: a reveal records whether it began at an encounter deck or a facedown card dealt from one (wave 6, §3.64)
* A villain's flip or next stage reveals its new face in full (incite, When Revealed, reveal windows, peril, surge); a main scheme advance resolves its incite; granted peril counts (wave 6, §3.65)
* Damage by its source's printed resource ("can only take damage from", "+1 damage from") and doubled damage taken, logged as damageDoubled (wave 6 §3.68)
* Play a card searched from your deck, ignoring its cost, with the deck shuffled after; an Action event played by an effect only during a player's turn (wave 6 §3.70)
* A character set to its maximum hit points announces hitPointsReset ("After MaGog's hit points are reset"), with on.hitPointsReset and resetHitPoints DSL builders (wave 6, §3.67).
* Threat on cards that are not schemes: Hinder X on any card type, a character's threat is never thwartable, and a hero's change of form counts as a flip (wave 6, §3.59)
* chooseNumber effect and choice (any number of ...), and spendResources.distinctTypes for spending different resources as an effect (wave 6, §3.69)
* The show deck: a scenario deck with named cards and no discard pile, closed to player card effects, with a named-scenario-deck card destination (wave 6, §3.66)
* The encounter deck resets the moment it empties, and "After the encounter deck resets" can be heard (wave 6, §3.60)
* The start of villain phase step three is an interrupt timing point (villainStepStarting), announced before the step deals (wave 6, §3.61)
* A set-aside modular set can come in by its revealed SHOW environment, shuffled in or shuffled on top of the encounter deck (wave 6, §3.62)
* A cost can exhaust each matching card the payer controls (all ready or none), and Family Matters' Alter-Ego Action exhausts your identity and each support you control (wave 6)
* A 'Choose one' option can be gated on whether its player could pay a resource spend (canPayResources), priced as the spend itself prices it, distinct types included (wave 6)
* A play-time note: modifyCardEffect.note records a number on the card being played, read by Predicate playNote; playedVia reads the same record (wave 6, §3.52)
* Placing counters as a cost: AbilityCost.placeCounters, paid before the effects and announced as countersPlaced; DSL placeCountersCost (wave 6, §3.53)
* An interrupt or response with an up-to-N counter cost asks how many to remove inside a timing window (chooseCostCounters), from 1 to what it can (wave 6, §3.53)
* A cost can look at the top cards of the encounter deck and discard some of them, paid before the effects, with the discards bound for the effects (wave 6, §3.54)
* modifyThwart: "that thwart removes N additional threat" adds to the thwart in progress's one removal, so its target, crisis and patrol checks and its responses see the total (wave 6, §3.55)
* Find a card anywhere in the game: TargetRef find and EffectSpec findCard, shuffling only a deck it was found in (wave 6, §3.48)
* A player upgrade attaches to any character (another player's identity or ally, a minion, the villain) with its controller unchanged, and an ability can attach a card and deal damage as its cost (wave 6, §3.49)
* A lasting effect can grant another character's traits, read live and ended when a bound attachment leaves that host (wave 6, §3.50)
* A query matches cards of the same classification (identity-specific, aspect or basic) as another card (wave 6, §3.51)
* A change of form carries the traits the identity had on the face it left, so "after a MUTANT alter-ego changes into hero form" can trigger (wave 6, §3.56)
* Play a tucked ally as if from hand, paid for and entering play exhausted; a removed-from-game card is never retrieved by a ref (wave 6, §3.57)
* A TargetRef names the enemy whose activation (attack or scheme) is in progress; the Brotherhood boosts in Mansion Attack give the extra boost card only to the named villain's own activation
* A counter cost can remove each counter of a type with no choice (at least one); Bishop's interrupt is scripted
* A value counts the cards a ref names wherever they are, such as the cards tucked under a card
* A "(thwart)"-labeled ability is a real thwart however its threat removal is written: patrol stops it on the main scheme and "after you thwart" answers it (Psychic Manipulation, Heroic Intervention, Mentorship, Inconspicuous and 16 more)
* Determined Defense cannot be triggered while its player cannot thwart the main scheme (a crisis icon, an engaged patrol minion, a "cannot thwart" rule; RRG 1.8 "Target", p. 43), so the attack deals its damage
* The players lose when an encounter deck and its discard pile are both empty: checked when a card leaves the deck and has been placed, so the last card dealt, revealed or put into play with no discard pile ends the game (RRG 1.8 p. 17; owner decision Q57)
* A game that has ended stays at its game-over step with an empty stack and no pending choice, whatever was resolving when it ended (the step-three deal that takes the last encounter card)
* A response to the encounter deck resetting resolves right after the reset, in the middle of the deal, reveal or boost effect that emptied the deck: the rest is dealt from the new deck afterward (RRG 1.8 p. 17; owner decision Q58)
* A deck that was searched is always shuffled, found or not: a find that finds nothing shuffles the decks it looked through, and four searches that never shuffled now do (To Me, My X-Men!, Chooser of the Slain, Chaos Manipulation, Kang's Wrath 4B) (RRG 1.8 p. 39, Q77)
* A show-deck card discarded with no replacement applying goes to the encounter discard pile, not the bottom of the show deck; Across the Mojoverse 1B and Cornered! are unchanged (Q54)
* Curtain Call: a tie for the character with the most threat is broken by the first player, not the revealing player (RRG 1.8 p. 19, Q67)
* A "(thwart)" ability that names only a scheme its player cannot thwart (an engaged patrol minion, a crisis icon) can no longer be played: Impede, Looking for Trouble, Running Interference, Brainstorm and Psychic Manipulation; divided thwarts stop offering that scheme, and a blocked thwart is no longer heard by "after you thwart" (RRG 1.8 p. 43)
* A "(thwart)" ability is one thwart however many schemes it removes threat from: "when you thwart" and "after you thwart" are each heard once (Inconspicuous, Multitasking, Crisis Interdiction, Rapid Deployment, Heroic Intervention), Operative Skill is used once and adds to each instance, and Justice Served reads every thwarted scheme (RRG 1.8 p. 44)
* Emergency is a thwart: any "(thwart)" ability that reduces the threat a scheme places is a thwart that removes none, so an engaged patrol minion or a "cannot thwart" stops it, "after you thwart" hears it once, a crisis icon does not stop it and thwart bonuses add nothing (RRG 1.8 pp. 26, 32, FAQ p. 59)
* Table option "a hero and an ally with the same name can't both be in play" (GameSetupConfig.tableRules.sameNameHeroAllyConflict, off by default: FFG's unique rule is unchanged): with it on, a subtitle-less ally titled like a hero in play (Colossus, Valkyrie, Ironheart) cannot enter play
* A failing test pins Armor Up's activation timing: the villain should attack the hero Colossus flipped into, not scheme
* Simultaneous Temporary discards of cards with no leaves-play ability of their own no longer ask the first player to order them.

## [v0.15.0] - 2026-10-01
### Changed
* The deck builder's identity list shows each hero's art beside its name.
* The Card pool filter chips on Decks & Collection wear the aspect stamp colours (Basic grey, Hero ink).
* Deck builder pool rows show each card's picture and what it does; tap a row to inspect the card.
* The Decks list shows each deck's hero art beside its name.
### Fixed
* Decks list detail lines read at body size, and the Card pool chips are sized to their stamp labels instead of shrinking them.

## [v0.14.0] - 2026-10-01
### Added
* Export all your saved games, decks, campaigns and unlocks to a file from Settings → Save data, and import it on another device.
* The deck builder can filter the card pool by pack and by wave, and sort it by name, cost or pack
### Fixed
* Beat Cop, Mantis and Fruition (printed "Action:") can now be used in alter-ego form, and Cloak of Levitation (printed "Hero Action:") only in hero form.
* Moment of Triumph now offers its heal after your hero attacks and defeats an enemy; before, it was never offered.
* Locked and Loaded (Venom) can be played in alter-ego form, as its printed plain Action allows
* White Tiger (Spectrum pack) draws her cards when played in alter-ego form too, as her printed plain Response allows
* Preservation (Vision pack) heals 1 damage from your hero when spent; it healed nothing before
* Audacity (Valkyrie pack) no longer deals damage when spent in alter-ego form; it prints a Hero Response
* Pasting a decklist that names a card Sinister Motives reprints (such as Young Love) imports it instead of reporting an ambiguous name
* Importing a Spider-Woman or Adam Warlock deck from MarvelCDB reads all its chosen aspects instead of refusing the deck
* Anticipation (Valkyrie pack) can now interrupt a minion engaging you, including one revealed in the villain phase
* Interrupt and Response events attached to Jocasta, George Stacy or the Black Panther ally can be played in their timing window, as their text allows
* Shadow of the Past shuffles only your nemesis set into the encounter deck; it took every set-aside card, including Ironheart's later identity versions, which then hung the game when revealed
* Deck builder on phones: the identity list scrolls by drag, the deck controls scroll in their own area so the card pool stays on screen, and a drag no longer leaves a button highlighted.
* Deck builder: an aspect's tip opens over the aspect buttons instead of under them, and the aspect buttons wear their aspect's colour, as on the hero selection screen.
### Engine
* A revealed minion's Quickstrike attack now resolves before its When Revealed ability, per FFG's Feb 28, 2026 ruling
### Content
* Venom (Eddie Brock) and Symbiote Suit, Sinister Motives' two basic cards outside a hero kit, now work in custom decks instead of doing nothing.
* Marvel Boy's card text names the physical resource icon instead of MarvelCDB's typo "[physics]"
### Docs
* Commit messages, PRs and GitHub comments carry no Claude attribution; a commit-msg hook strips it from commit messages
* Cross-hero tests prove the aspect cards of Core and waves 1-3 play from another hero's deck
* A real MarvelCDB decklist for each of the 29 Core–wave 4 heroes imports, is legal and plays a full game in the test suite

## [v0.13.0] - 2026-09-30
### Changed
* Aspect filter chips now carry a full-size split "i" segment for their tip, replacing the small corner badge that was clipped and hard to tap.
### Fixed
* Scrolling on a phone or tablet no longer opens the Scenario stages panel when the finger lifts over it; only a tap does.

## [v0.12.1] - 2026-09-30
### Fixed
* Hawkeye's Arrows on the Quiver must be played (paid for and discarded); they could be used as a free card ability that dealt damage and left the Arrow in place.
* Windows desktop build links again: the embedded game assets no longer push the Tauri lib past MSVC's 4 GB archive limit.
* Madame Hydra's 2 threat asks the first player which Legions of Hydra side scheme to use when both copies are in play, instead of always picking the same one.

* Guided mode: Pause gained "Stop tutorial"/"Turn guide off" while a tutorial is active, the guide panel's context label now names the run instead of duplicating the GUIDE stamp, and starting a fresh non-guided game clears a still-active guided-run flag

* Guided mode: the round debrief fires at the end of each round, and the Log tab/panel stays locked until lesson 5 is done
* How to play's hub lessons 2-5 now replay the tutorial script straight to that lesson's checkpoint instead of starting from the top

* Guided mode: aspect lesson pages' Try it starts a short guided Rhino game with that aspect's Core precon, with its own one-lesson signature-card walkthrough

* Add a replay-safe tips dev fixture (?screen=board&fixture=tips) for live G10e opportunistic-tip QA
