### Changed
* Desktop builds skip Tauri's brotli pass over the embedded assets and ship an uncompressed NSIS installer, cutting minutes off the Windows and macOS CI builds
* Cards that soak damage or break at a damage threshold (armor attachments, Avengers Tower, Ice Wall-style supports) show their damage against that threshold on the board, on phone as well, and in their Rules & State sheet.
* MC27's optional deck-freeze opt-in moves from localStorage into the campaign run record
* The game opens on Title; the first-run "New to the fight?" chooser now appears after you press New Game, and Hints only / No guide continue straight into scenario select
* Tutorial lesson 2 now teaches paying for cards as Peter Parker (Scientist ability + a discarded card) before the flip, which is now lesson 3
* Gate the dev-only ?screen= jumps and demo scenes behind import.meta.env.DEV so a production build always boots to Title
* The two Spider-Man heroes are shown with their alter ego everywhere a hero is named: Spider-Man (Peter Parker) and Spider-Man (Miles Morales).
* The two Black Panther heroes are shown with their alter ego: Black Panther (T'Challa) and Black Panther (Shuri).
* Tutorial lesson 3 (Hero & alter-ego) now teaches attacking: Spider-Man attacks Rhino, then Black Cat attacks too, before the villain phase
* Justice Try it teaches the attack-or-thwart balance across two rounds, with Daredevil thwarting and fighting at once, and TRY THIS now walks Daredevil's payment (Strength, then Genius, then Pay)
### Fixed
* MarvelCDB decklist import resolves reprint codes to the card they duplicate
* Pause's on-the-table glossary no longer counts basic concepts; the Rules reference glossary tab still lists them
* Fix guided-mode Settings/Pause scroll clipping, unify the Settings body into one scroll region, and fix the Guide-level segment's clipped border and low-contrast selection
* Guide warning toggles in Settings use short titles so they fit beside their switch on phone
* Requirement keyword with several space-separated resource icons (Spider-Man ally 27049/52022) now parses into card data instead of being dropped
* Spider-Man (Miles Morales): Web-Shot, Swing In, Power Within and Defense Mechanism resolve only the Special they name, not both Venom Blast and Spider Camouflage
* Wave 5 scenarios add Core's Expert encounter set in expert mode (RRG 1.8 Expert Mode); the new sm builder only added Standard
* Chaos Manipulation, Ancient Grudge, Crossbones' Assault, Proxima's Power, Corvus's Cunning and The Trickster now attack a hero and scheme against an alter-ego when their enemy "activates against" a player
* cards DSL: the ability validator didn't recognize addVillain's bind, rejecting the Sinister Six's Ambush! ability ("slot read before it is bound").
* Ability scripts for Absorbing Man (III), Luminous, Radioactive Man, The Magus, Ebony Maw, Brothers Grimm's set and Established Dominance now fire their 'activates against you' triggers on a scheme against an alter-ego player too, not only an attack (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q67)
* A card that is only the other face of a villain or main scheme (Venom Goblin's Manhattan environments) is no longer shuffled into the encounter deck; it enters play only by that card flipping
* Fallen Warrior (mts 21153) no longer attaches to an ally already in play when revealed; it attaches to the ally its own When Revealed puts into play
* Fix Nova's "The War's Been Brought" to count printed wild resource icons, not cards
* Fix coreScenario building a modular set from a non-Core card pool
* Scheme Crisis flag follows gained/blanked icons, not just the printed card
* Discard-from-hand ability costs read naturally, no-ceiling and combined-cost shapes included
* Choice sheet titles now name the leftover-damage, status-card-divide, damage-cost and player-deck-bottom prompts instead of falling back to a bare 'Choose'
* An either/or cost branch showing a Value-sized deck discard (Shield Spell, Aunt May & Uncle Ben) no longer prints '[object Object]'
* Payment now shows several resource-ability options off one card (Sync Ratio's several Interfaces) separately instead of collapsing them to one spendable/spent entry
* sm: fix four main-scheme stage art refs from .jpg to the bundled .png
* Scenario select names a scenario with three or more villains after the scenario (The Sinister Six, Breakout), not its lead villain
* Ironheart "Go for Champions!" (29025): printed text now matches the pre-errata scan, current keeps the RRG 1.5/1.8 errata
* Norn Stone (mts 21187a/b) now actually grants its +THW/+ATK/+DEF to your hero, not to the attachment itself
* Snitches Get Stitches sends Venom to the victory display via the redirected attack, not an orphaned keyword grant
* Crossbones' Armor is discarded once 5 or more damage is on it, and an attachment's damage (with the point it breaks at, like 3/5) now shows on its chip.
* MC27 Aftermath's per-seat S.H.I.E.L.D. Tech deal: card art, no cross-seat guessing/taking
* SM Briefing rows: no raw op-dump text, no bogus hidden-evidence envelope for a plain hidden number
* Sinister Motives reputation node 9's setup search now makes each player take a minion it finds, and deals a facedown encounter card only to a player whose search finds none (it offered 0-1 before, and never dealt the card).
* Frozen-deck screen labels a market-less box's grants Campaign cards and hides The Market
* Frozen-deck screen names a flipped campaign grant's face by label, not by its own printed name
* Choice overlay shows a setup instruction's text/citation when a choice has no source card
* Tap a granted campaign card's line in Deck Edit's your-deck list to inspect it
* Aftermath's S.H.I.E.L.D. Tech deal shows real card art and a real one-line effect
* Aftermath's S.H.I.E.L.D. Tech decline reads Keep none, not MC10's No mark for me
* Aftermath's seat header no longer runs its status line into the next seat's column
* SM issue 1's aftermath panel shows Sandman's own art instead of a placeholder note
* Guide mode's lethal-damage hint now checks the best block against every queued attack at end of turn instead of the hero being exhausted with no ready ally
* Guide panel/callout polish: term-text paragraph breaks, panel section top padding, GUIDE stamp sizing, legend row inset, collapse control label and hit zone
* Guided mode: Skip this step no longer ends the whole tutorial, the rail stays bright over the spotlight dim, Stop tutorial is spelled out, each do-this step names its own action, paying for a card walks TRY THIS from the card to its source to Pay, and the GUIDE PICK stamp no longer overlaps a defend option's title
* Guided mode: keep the round/phase band clear of the guide rail, and stop the phone defend sheet's option rows from overlapping
* Guided mode: recompose the How to win screen's wide layout so the WIN/LOSE cards fill the space instead of leaving a dead gap above the EVERY ROUND strip
* Guided mode: Hold on! overlay matches the tiles — ink-tint safe button, tight card sizing, schemeFinish threat bar
* Guided mode: lesson 5's spotlight-scheme step gets a "How do I stop it?" shortcut into the thwart step, and the thwart step previews the threat THW would remove on the scheme's own meter; lesson 3's payment hint now says "Tap Pay".
* Finishing the tutorial drops guidance to Hints for that game only, without changing the saved guide level; the debrief's level picker now saves
* Fix guided mode's aspect lesson page (G10c): the narrow-layout gap before Signature cards, wide layouts filling the column with real card scans and a larger Try it portrait, and wire it into the How to play hub, the aspect chips' Aspects link, and Settings' Aspect lessons row.
* Guided mode's opportunistic tip toast no longer covers the hand or action bar, and the acceleration tip no longer fires on the main scheme's own printed rate
* Hold on! warnings no longer second-guess an active guide step, and Escape dismisses an on-board tip
* Guided mode: aspect try-it TRY THIS walks payment card-payer-Pay, header label no longer overlaps Collapse, complete copy points to How to play, and the lesson page has one red button
* An engaged minion no longer draws twice — once with the enemies and once in the engaged player's own play area
* The action bar's flip button keeps the word Flip on tabbed (phone/tablet-portrait) layouts instead of truncating to a bare destination
* Villain phase: an inline interrupt (Spider-Sense, Great Responsibility, etc.) now draws and answers on the first frame instead of lagging the reveal-pacing cursor
* Guided mode: lesson 4 (the villain phase) no longer desyncs when the player never presses Got it — the villain-phase-order step auto-advances and declare-defender resolves from live state, and the compact guide strip gets its own Got it
* Guided mode: the nudge now redraws, the phone waiting callout clears End Turn, the Stop × confirms first, and the rail no longer crashes on expand
* Villain phase walkthrough now closes itself once a defended attack resolves the rest of the phase, instead of hanging until Skip
* Escape on the Hold on! warning dismisses it instead of also opening Pause
* Guided mode QA nits: the aspect lesson's Try it/Got it debug rects track scroll, the Hold on!/Inspect/aspect-lesson debug hooks clear on close, and the aspect tip panel no longer overlaps its own chip
* Guide rail shows a lesson's text when it's long enough to scroll (it hid under the board dim); tighter paying-for-cards copy and a one-line resource legend
* Rules reference glossary card thumbnails scroll with their entries instead of staying pinned in place
* The guide's "Press G" hint disappears as soon as the guide is focused, and reads more plainly
* Fix three guided-mode design-polish bugs: How to win's cropped scheme art, the aspect lesson page's signature-card gap on phone, and How to play's header-hugging section labels
* Round debrief: shorter Up next subline, Bangers primary button, Keep playing on the final debrief; guide rail/callout show a dotted-word hint when a step's body has a glossary term
* Rules reference: villain/main-scheme/hero art now loads without tapping (the real bug was front-face art resolving to nothing for those types, not a stale redraw); loading vs. confirmed-missing scans are now distinguished
* Rules reference: Quickstrike's glossary card no longer overlaps its cite line, and ruling-conflict/unverified/source flags are now worded for players instead of leaking implementer notes
* Rules reference: Villain phase tab shows the encounter card back instead of grey 'no scan' boxes when there's no game in progress
* A scrolling list's rows no longer stay clickable once scrolled outside its own viewport, and a scroll drag can no longer activate whatever row it ends on (McVariableList/McVirtualList/McScrollRegion)
* Seats aspect info badges now scroll with their chips and have a 44px touch target; the overpay hint no longer ping-pongs between two payment choices
* Guide tooltip anchors to the tapped/hovered term's real screen position instead of opening near the top of the screen
* Tutorial lesson 2 explains that Scientist doesn't exhaust Peter Parker
* The tutorial's waiting/complete note is a compact strip above the hand on phone/tablet, and dismissing it no longer stops the tutorial
* The guide's Press G hint is hidden on touch devices until a keyboard or gamepad input is actually seen
* Tutorial phone/tablet-portrait waiting strip now shows its own text (the villain-phase/complete note), not just the GUIDE stamp and ×
* Guided-mode e2e runs one test at a time in CI and gives the full tutorial runs a longer budget, so the slower software-rendered runner no longer times out
* Tutorial: Attack Rhino now suggests Black Cat first, then Spider-Man, and the guide highlights the suggested character in the Attack with picker while explaining the order is the player's choice
* Glossary tooltips on dotted words now open when the mouse comes in from above or below, not only from the side; the guide rail's step text and the tooltip text are larger on desktop
* An aspect lesson's Try it button starts a game again after you concede the last Try it game and come back
* The ROUND N · PLAYER PHASE band now plays after the villain phase walkthrough closes, every round, instead of behind it; a villain phase with no decisions in it no longer announces VILLAIN PHASE on the way into the next round
* The player phase band is also held when the villain phase walkthrough is still opening, or when a slow machine first draws the table well after the band started
### Engine
* Wave 5 schema: lettered main scheme stages with environment faces, negative Victory, scheme icons on any card, progressing identities (refused until built), Sinister Six set-aside villains and card-ability win
* Villains that start set aside, come back and are set aside again; the active counter moves by activation order (The Sinister Six)
* An enemy activation can be interrupted and canceled, and the villain's activation is announced with no villain in play (Web Binding, Ambush!)
* Several main schemes with one marked by a counter (Venom Goblin's glider), moving counters, and a main scheme stage flipping to its environment face
* Acceleration tokens on any card count toward step one, move between cards, and announce their placement (Tracking Prey, Hapless Pedestrians)
* Encounter cards in a player's deck, hand and discard pile: moveCards into a player's zones, an interrupt when one is drawn or discarded from a deck, and a response after a boost card resolves (Mysterio)
* Boost cards held on a card that never activates (an identity) and moved to an enemy by card text (Venom, Leave Us Alone!)
* A resolveSpecials with bind reports what the resolved Specials bound, such as the cards Surging Sands discarded (Sandslide)
* A constant that increases the damage a character takes, once per damage event (Bell Tower)
* A constant that makes a card count as another card type with a trait for query matching (Festering Mass)
* Scheme icons printed on cards that are not schemes count toward hazard, crisis and acceleration (Venom ally, Public Outcry, Team Leader)
* A mode-of-play predicate, and an encounter card's grants to itself apply while it is revealed (Surprise!, Frequent Flyers)
* Every attack of a player's turn is recorded, and a predicate reads whether this is the first attack this turn (Venom III)
* When/After a card leaves play: an event with interrupt and response windows, answered by the card that left and matched on its traits as it left (Spider-Man (Hobie Brown), Web of Life and Destiny)
* Max 1 per [instance]: one use per triggering event shared by every copy of the card's title (Web-Bracelet, Ghost Kick, Phantom Flip)
* A card played from facedown is played faceup, and a player's own facedown attachment is visible to them (George Stacy)
* A card's payment records how many resources each resource ability generated for it (VEN#m, Rapid Deployment)
* A hand resource card can permit spending it toward another player's payment while a condition holds (Everyday Hero)
* A card in hand can be excluded from the hand-size count: discard down, draw up and the mulligan draw ignore it (Connection to the Worldmind)
* A character may hold any number of tough status cards (Armadillo)
* Printed resources of the cards in a player's hand can be treated as another type, for payment and every printed-resource count (Haywire)
* An additional cost to thwart a scheme, resources or indirect damage, asked before each thwart of it (Giant Monster Attack, Cat in a Tree)
* A basic thwart limited to the schemes a rule names, and ignores that apply to basic thwarts only (Retinal Display)
* Progressing identities: the first version is seated with the others set aside, and swapping to the next version keeps the dial, counters and statuses (Ironheart)
* Setup: a player can be given additional mulligans (PlayerSetup.extraMulligans), each a full second mulligan decided right after the first, for Sinister Motives reputation node 5 (docs/phase7-wave5.md §3.26)
* Optional encounter-card abilities triggered by damage are offered to the player dealing it (Bell Tower's Quiet side, docs/phase7-wave5.md §3.29 and §4 Q8); replacing an attack's damage with counters is tested to deal no damage and no excess (MC27 p. 21 FAQ)
* Generating resources is an event abilities can respond to (M.O.R.B.I.U.S.), and a resource ability can spend counters once per use, several times in one payment (Spider-Ham's toon counters)
* Campaign queries for MC27's reputation conditions and Waking Nightmare (accelerationTokensInPlay, defeatedIdentities, cardsInPlayerDecks, playersInScenario) and a grantAdditionalMulligans effect for reputation node 5 (docs/phase7-wave5.md §3.27)
* A permanent attachment whose host leaves play stays in play, unattached, in its controller's play area, and on.attachedCardDefeated() for a minion or side scheme host (Wrist Navigator)
* Looking at the top card of the encounter deck at any time (Sector Scan): a lasting mayLookAtTopOfEncounterDeck rule, read by faceVisible for the named viewer only; Inspect shows the face to that seat (docs/phase7-wave5.md §3.28)
* Sinister Motives' additional mulligan is a second pass in player order after every player's normal mulligan (p1, p2, then p1, p2); a player who kept their hand is not offered it (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q19, Q20)
* A player who cannot pay a scheme's additional thwart cost can no longer choose it as a thwart's target (basic thwarts and thwart effects; hand cards and resource abilities both count toward paying).
* A printed text box that cannot be blanked: RuleSpec textBoxCannotBeBlanked for SP//dr Suit and SP//dr (docs/phase7-wave5.md §3.31)
* "When X leaves play" interrupts resolve before the card moves, with it still in play with its attachments, counters and controller; responses see it gone, and a replacement can redirect the move (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q17)
* A permanent encounter attachment with no player owner or controller is discarded to its encounter discard pile when its host leaves play; player cards still return unattached (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.2 Q26)
* A counter spent as if it were a resource (Spider-Ham's toon counters) still pays but is not generated: it adds nothing to the resourcesGenerated event, and a payment of only counters raises none (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q5).
* A separated identity (SP//dr): two physical cards sharing one hit point dial, flipped together on a form change (docs/phase7-wave5.md §3.24).
* An additional thwart cost is paid together with a basic thwart's own cost (declining it, or a take-damage cost partly prevented, leaves the hero unexhausted and nothing thwarted); a divided basic thwart must afford the total; a thwart event's payability is judged after its own cost (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q27–Q30).
* A payment's "after you spend this card" and "after … generates resources" responses share one response window, so forced responses to either resolve before optional ones (RRG 1.8 p. 45, p. 5); new pushEventsSharingResponses primitive (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q25).
* An encounter card drawn or discarded from a player's deck that nothing replaces is dealt to that player facedown and they draw 1 card, as Mysterio's main scheme prints; setup draws and the mulligan included (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q4)
* SP//dr: counters and cards attached to the other physical card (the INACTIVE support in alter-ego form, the SP//dr upgrade in hero form) move to the identity on a form change instead of being discarded (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q38).
* takeIntoHand changes a card's owner with the move, after its 'when it leaves play' interrupts (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q35)
* The Permanent keyword now keeps blanks from cards outside its own hero, scenario or modular set off a card's text box, for both lasting and constant blanks (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q31).
* A confused hero or ally still pays a scheme's additional thwart cost on a basic thwart before the confused card replaces the thwart (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q40).
* Cards leaving play from one step share one interrupt window and one response window, attachments leaving with their host get their leave interrupts in the host's window still in play, and a replacement's move is announced after the replaced leaving's cancelled line (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q32–Q34)
* A hero's nemesis set counts as that hero's set for the Permanent keyword's blank protection (FFG ruling June 25, 2026; docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q43).
* A confused ally or identity thwarting through a card ability or effect (not just its basic thwart) discards the confused card instead: no threat removed, no after-thwarts responses (RRG 1.8 p. 13).
* A Permanent keyword granted by another card's effect or rule protects a text box from other sets' blanks, as a printed one does (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q45).
* test: leave-play host steps for main-scheme flips, type flips, stage/villain removal and cancelled carrier attachments (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q50)
* Permanent stops only effects from cards outside the permanent card's own set from defeating it or moving it out of play; its own hero, scenario or modular set's abilities get through, and game-rule moves are stopped as before (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q46)
* A host-step attachment whose own leave interrupt cancels its leaving stays in play: attached if its host only flips, unattached in play if its host leaves (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q53)
* A take-into-hand that Permanent or "cannot leave play" refuses no longer changes the card's owner or controller (Q46 follow-up)
* A leave or defeat that the Permanent keyword stops is logged as leavePlayBlocked with the new "permanent" reason (Q46 follow-up)
* A cost that discards or returns a card in play no longer offers a permanent card its move would be stopped for, and is unpayable when nothing else is left (Q46 follow-up)
* A permanent attachment on a host that leaves play through a host step (a villain removed, set aside or defeated while others remain, a main scheme stage removed) is unattached into its controller's play area, or discarded if it is an unowned encounter card, as when a host leaves play normally; on a host that flips and stays in play it stays attached (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q50)
* A card attached to an attachment is in play, and leaves play with its host's host in the same shared interrupt and response window, listed right after the attachment it is on (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q50)
* Joining another game area waits for the "when this leaves play" interrupts of the attachments on the joining area's own stage before the stage is removed, as the other host steps do (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q50)
* An unlabeled ability whose thwart effect names a confused ally or identity as thwarting can be used with no valid target; the attempt discards the confused card instead (RRG 1.8 "Confuse, Confused", p. 13; docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q50)
* Setup can stack named cards on top of player and encounter decks after the seeded shuffle, for tutorials (GameSetupConfig.stack)
* A separated identity's form change waits for the "when this leaves play" interrupts of the other card's attachments the identity cannot take, resolved before either card flips; a permanent one stays attached to the other card (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q50)
* The boostIcons value sums the boost icons of every card its ref names ("the number of boost icons discarded this way"), not only the first (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q56)
* Allies and minions defeated by one effect (one damage sweep, indirect damage, "defeat each …") share one defeat interrupt window, resolve every When Defeated (first player orders them across cards) while all are still in play, then leave from one step with one shared leave window and one response window (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q49)
* applyRuleUntil can last until the end of an attack: the one in progress, or the one the same ability's next enemyAttack initiates; cannotPlay rules now also keep events out of timing windows
* Wave 5 custom-deck legality tests: identity-specific kits never mix, Ironheart/SP//dr gates lifted, per-identity aspect rules, and deck builder start state
* reorderCards can put cards looked at from a player deck back on the top and/or bottom of that deck in any order (placeOnTopOrBottomOfPlayerDeck)
* resolveSpecials can name which of a card's Special abilities to resolve (abilities filter); without it every Special resolves as before
* A damage trigger can require (or exclude) an ally's consequential damage: EventPattern.consequential, when.damage(…, { consequential: true }).
* An attack records the damage each character took from it (indirect shares and overkill spill included), readable as eventDamageTakenAtLeast (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q65)
* enemyAttack and enemyScheme carry extraBoostCards and boostIconsEach scoped to the activation they start; modifyAttack gains boostIconsEach (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q66)
* "X activates against you" effects and triggers: an enemy attacks a player in hero form and schemes against one in alter-ego form (enemyActivation, docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q67)
* A character's defeat records the excess damage that defeated it (any damage source; exactly lethal, prevented or non-damage defeats have none), read by ValueSpec defeatExcessDamage (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q68).
* handCount can count only the hand cards matching a filter, read live (Q69)
* Constant rule cannotResolveTriggeredAbilities: triggered abilities (actions, resources, interrupts, responses, forced or not) printed on matching cards, optionally one identity face, are neither offered nor resolved; a random hand-discard cost can be narrowed with a filter (wave 5 Q70)
* Encounter attachments can declare a cannotAttach ability that resolves instead of the no-legal-host discard ("If you cannot, …, then attach this card to …")
* The cannotThwart rule takes an optional scheme scope (schemes), enforced for basic thwarts (refused before costs), legal actions and thwart-effect targets; Life-Size Decoy (sm 27142) now stops the engaged player thwarting side schemes
* With several main schemes in play, villain phase step one places threat on every one of them before any completion resolves, then their "after threat is placed" responses share one window (docs/phase7-wave5.md §4.1 Q71)
* Card searches can read the removed-from-game area (`CardSelector removedFromGame`), and find "a copy of your obligation" and "your nemesis side scheme" (`TargetQuery.obligationOf` / `nemesisSideSchemeOf`, RRG 1.8 p. 30)
* An encounter attachment with no "attach to" text (`AttachmentCard.attachesTo` now optional) is not attached by the reveal step: its own When Revealed attaches it, then it enters play, or it is discarded if left unattached (RRG 1.8 "Reveal" p. 38; ruling, Feb 20, 2026 (4)); `attach` no longer attaches to a card out of play
* Attachment hosts can require "you control" (controlledBy: "you"), so "the ally you control with the lowest cost" only ranks the revealing player's allies; tests for an ally treated as a minion activating, taking no consequential damage and keeping its exhausted state
* Discard costs can require a combined printed cost (any number of matching hand cards totalling N or more); Advanced Glider's Hero Action (sm 27136) now works
* A card can double the [wild] resources generated while paying for it (resourceMultiplier.forThisCard); Lightspeed Flight and Pot Shot's constants now work
* paidWith / paidWithOnly take an optional card: another card's interrupt can read how the card being played was paid for
* Cost shapes for a highest-cost in-play discard, taking indirect damage, and giving the villain a status or boost card; Osborn Tech's Arm Cannon, Kinetic Armor and Neocarbon Scales now pay them as real costs
* TargetQuery gains withKeyword/withoutKeyword (printed or granted keywords; Permanent read as isPermanent) and anyOf (a union of differently filtered queries)
* Resource abilities take a `while` condition (Brawn's "While Brawn is exhausted, he gains: 'Resource: …'"), honored wherever a payment source is offered or accepted; Brawn (ironheart 29004) is scripted
* A card whose text box is blanked shows no crisis, hazard, acceleration or amplify icons, printed or gained, while it is blank (FFG ruling on Vivian, confirmed 2026-09-28); new iconsOn helper for one card's icons.
* A basic thwart's event now carries its amount: the thwarter's THW before it resolves and the threat actually removed after, as a "(thwart)" ability's does; Lady Spider's Response now moves the threat her basic thwart removed
* An attachment whose own cost discards it ("discard this card →") still reads "attached" as the card it was attached to; Overwatch (30019) no longer offers its own scheme, and moves at most the threat on it
* A character's defeat records whether an ally's consequential damage defeated it, and defeatExcessDamage can count only that damage's excess
* Extend removeCounters to remove every counter type from a target ('discard all counters from X')
* An enemy's activation boost card now reads Villainous like every other keyword: a gained Villainous counts (Solus's Inheritor minions get one) and a blanked printed one doesn't.
* A resource ability whose cost picks a card can generate from that pick (one payment source per pick); SP//dr Suit's Sync Ratio is scripted
* Engine: a cost that deals damage to a chosen character (damageCards), and divide of status cards among up to N targets
* A printedHp value reads a character's printed hit points (RRG 1.8 "Printed", p. 35), with a printedHpOf DSL builder.
* Add an encounterCardResolved trigger (after you resolve a treachery, RRG 1.8 Resolve p. 37) and script Spider-Man Noir's attaching Response (spdr 31015)
* A "discard the top N cards of your deck" cost can bind the discarded cards for its effects (discardFromDeckSlot); scripts Aunt May & Uncle Ben (spdr 31007)
* Enemy attacks can divide their damage evenly among the attacked player's characters (Bombshell, Iron Spider's Sinister Syndicate); the first player places any leftover points.
* Campaign choice sources can choose among an earlier random deal (values) and exclude titles a log field records (excludingTitles); Sinister Motives now authors reputation node 1's S.H.I.E.L.D. Tech deal and Community Service's no-repeat draw
* Campaign setup can bring a named card in from outside the game (setAsideCards); an ownerless player card put into play is owned by its controller. Sinister Motives now puts Venom (190) into play in scenarios 3 and 4, and offers each player a Helicarrier / Symbiote Suit for reputation nodes 21 and 25.
* Scenario-specific card query (RRG 1.8 p. 39); Sinister Motives reputation node 17's penalty now searches for and reveals a scenario-specific side scheme
* Damage effects bind the characters they actually damaged (`<bind>.damaged`), and Bombshell's Boost now exhausts each character it damaged.
* Campaign-log card selector can match by printed name (byName), plus an inEncounterSet query clause; Venom Goblin's setup now shuffles in the Sinister Assault minions named like the recorded Last Ones Standing villains (MC27 p. 17)
* An effects frame from a campaign or scenario setup instruction now names that instruction, so a choice it raises (MC27 node 9's minion search) shows "Campaign setup" instead of an anonymous prompt.

## [v0.11.1] - 2026-09-27
### Fixed
* The opening "Round 1 · Player Phase" band shows again at the start of a game; it had already run out before the table appeared

## [v0.11.0] - 2026-09-27
### Added
* Campaign Rewind: "Rewind, new shuffle" deals a fresh game, and "Rewind, same hands" replays the lost attempt's exact deal, like a one-shot's Game over
### Changed
* Web deploys build in GitHub Actions and upload to Netlify with the CLI (production from main, previews per PR), so they no longer use Netlify build minutes
* Gamora, Falcon (both printings) and Astral Projection now show the card they look at before acting on it
### Fixed
* Inspect: long "This card, this game" log lines wrap under a round-tag column instead of overlapping the next line
* Jessica Drew's "Look at the top card of any deck" now asks which deck (encounter or a player's) and shows you the card
* Campaign: rewinding an issue after a loss deals a fresh shuffle instead of the same hands and encounter deck as the lost attempt
### Engine
* New lookAt effect: shows cards to one player through an acknowledge-only choice (Done), nothing moves
### Content
* Jessica Drew (04031b): fix the "Action::" typo in her card text
### Docs
* README shows the Netlify deploy status badge

## [v0.10.0] - 2026-09-27
### Added
* New screens for guided mode

## [v0.9.0] - 2026-09-26
### Changed
* Seats and Scenario select scroll smoothly: roster card faces are painted in a web worker (art fetched, decoded and downscaled off the main thread), and scrolling moves the shelves instead of redrawing them
### Fixed
* A roster card's SELECTED / LOCKED / SEAT tag no longer runs past its red plate on desktop
### Engine
* "Then" now also waits on searches and player-deck "discard until" that find nothing, reveals with no card or a cancelled one, cancels with nothing to cancel, and attacks or schemes that don't happen (preThenUnresolved on the log).
* "Each X attacks/schemes. Then, …" with no X in play counts as resolved, so its "then" still runs.
* "Discard cards from the encounter deck until …" now stops when the encounter deck empties instead of carrying on into the reshuffled deck (RRG Encounter Deck).
### Content
* Then sweep: Shield Toss (N/A) and Captain America's Helmet use andThen
* Then sweep: core pack uses andThen for Webbed Up, Crisis Interdiction, Split Personality, Ritual Combat, Biomechanical Upgrades
* Then sweep: drs pack, Master of the Mystic Arts uses andThen
* Then sweep: gmw pack uses andThen for Brainstorm, Calculate the Odds, Take the Fight to Them, Biogram Image, View the Cosmos, Nebula I/II/III, The Poison
* Call for Aid's "then add that ally" is skipped when the discard finds no Avenger ally.
* Then sweep: nebu pack uses andThen for Nebula, Gamora, Combat Ready; Cosmo N/A
* Gamora (19020)'s "then add that card" is skipped when her discard finds no event.
* Preemptive Strike is no longer offered for a boost card with no boost icons (FAQ Attacrobatics); its damage and Teen Spirit's "then add that card" now wait on the text before "then".
* Then sweep: vnm pack, Flash Thompson's Armed and Ready uses andThen
* Fetch the 28 card-art scans the pool referenced but lacked (main scheme B-sides, villain stages, Ironheart/Quicksilver alter-ego overrides) from Hall of Heroes, and fix two card records that pointed at an external image URL
* Then sweep: gob pack, Hostile Takeover uses andThen
* Black Widow's "reveal another card" and the Armored Rhino Suit's threshold now wait on the text before "then"; Legions of Hydra, The Doomsday Chair and Masters of Mayhem keep their shuffle (RRG Search).
* Then sweep: hood pack, Hyde Formula uses andThen; Hood/Making Connections/Field Recruitment N/A
* Spycraft's "then reveal another card" is skipped when there is nothing left to cancel.
* Counterspell's "then, discard this card" now waits on its cancel.
* Order and Chaos (Quicksilver)'s 2 damage is skipped when the treachery's When Revealed was already cancelled.
* Order and Chaos (Scarlet Witch)'s 2 damage is skipped when the treachery's When Revealed was already cancelled.
* Cosmic Ward stays in play when the treachery was already cancelled by the time it resolves.
* Then sweep: mts pack, Under Siege and Avengers Tower use andThen
* Then sweep: toafk pack, Kang's Arrival (Master of Time) and Time-Travel Hijinks use andThen
* Then sweep: trors pack, Photographic Reflexes and Master Strategist use andThen
* Held Hostage stays attached, and Crowbar Toss leaves the active villain counter alone, when the villain's attack or scheme is cancelled by a status (or can't happen).
* The Island of Dr. Zola and The Mad Doctor's threshold now sits behind "then" (no behavior change).
* Honor Among Thieves no longer gives the villain a boost card when its discard finds no Criminal minion; Planetary Invasion, Combat Ready and Badoon Ship now wait on the text before "then".
* Replace all 177 FFG-watermarked Mad Titan's Shadow scans with unwatermarked Hall of Heroes copies
### Docs
* docs/then-sweep.md records the open questions and e2e seed changes from the then-sweep primitives.

* Tower Defense battle music: "The Tower Besieged"
* Tower Defense villain-wins music: "Under Their Heel"
* Tower Defense villain-loses music: "The Last Banner"
* Table setup: a toggle for Tower Defense's suggested setup damage on Avengers Tower (wave 4 Q4)
* Thanos battle music: "The Unyielding Decree"
* Thanos villain-wins music: "The Universe Halved"
* Thanos villain-loses music: "A Realm Restored"
* Hela battle music: "Cathedral of Frozen Steel"
* Hela villain-wins music: "Crown of Cold"
* Hela villain-loses music: "The Sun Claims the Valley"
* Campaign dossier and briefing: a hidden-evidence envelope, count-only until revealed (campaign design Q4)
* Alliance cost: each helping player approves their own contribution before it's spent (wave 4 Q10)
* Loki battle music: "The False King's March"
* Loki villain-wins music: "Asgard Kneels"
* Loki villain-loses music: "The Trickster Unmasked"
* The Hood battle music: "The Syndicate Rises"
* The Hood villain-wins music: "A Cloak Over the City"
* The Hood villain-loses music: "The Syndicate Scatters"
* The Mad Titan's Shadow campaign finale music: "The Gauntlet Falls Silent"
* Dev-only ?screen=alliance and ?screen=hidden-evidence jumps: a two-seat War Machine + Star-Lord game with Cosmic Alliance in hand, and the hidden-evidence envelope sealed or revealed on the Dossier and Briefing
* Client campaign-launch regression coverage for start.encounterSets extended to all five GMW scenarios, not only Brotherhood of Badoon
* Per-scenario GMW Setup: instruction tests at 1 and 3 players, plus expert-vs-standard villain start checks, for all five scenarios
* Ronan III's Retaliate 1 villain keyword (the only untested villain keyword in the gmw box besides Toughness) is now driven with a real attack
* 3-player and 4-player, standard and expert, smoke games for all five GMW scenarios, seating all six wave 3 hero packs
* Desperate Defense (16013), a Core reprint in Groot's kit, is now driven end-to-end in a real gmw game (item 5 spot re-audit finding)
* Add exact 1p/3p setup-state tests for every mts scenario and The Hood (threat scaling, villains, set-aside cards, expert differences)
* Inspect shows what Band Together is worth right now and why: one [wild] per ally you control, up to 3, spendable on any cost
* Confirm before ending a turn with a basic attack, thwart or recover still open, with a Settings toggle to turn it off
* A digital-convenience Back out button on a pending choice opened by the player's own card, when nothing hidden has been revealed since
### Changed
* Larger text in the unlock confirm popup and on the game-over screen's side buttons, for desktop readability
* Larger labels on the title screen's menu buttons, sharing one menu-button type size with the game-over screen
* Small text (12px and under) draws two sizes up on desktop-width screens, across every screen; phone and tablet are unchanged
* Title footer counts live cards against every card in the game (from the MarvelCDB catalog), and shows the real client version: the release on native apps, the release plus build commit on the web
* Tower Defense shows as "Proxima Midnight / Corvus Glaive" on scenario select and table setup
* Soundtrack titles renamed to fit their villains: Rhino, Ultron, Brotherhood of Badoon, Nebula and Ronan tracks
* Campaign finale titles renamed to fit their campaigns: The Rise of Red Skull's is "The Skull Shattered", The Galaxy's Most Wanted's is "Saviors of the Galaxy"
### Fixed
* Decks & Collection and the deck builder keep their original text sizes on desktop; Deck check and the rules card list give card captions room for the larger text
* The standalone Rhino intro comic now starts Rhino's battle theme, carried on through the deal into the game
* On a phone, the card sheet for a card in play with an action (Aunt May) offers that action instead of a Play button that did nothing
* rules-qa pass 3 item 1: tighten weak loose-bound tests across wave4, document legitimately-loose ones
* hood: Disaster at the Docks used direct damage instead of RRG indirect damage
* Board: a facedown card with no printed traits (Spectrum's own energy forms) no longer shows a blank name; a flipped attachment (Vision's mass form) shows its current face's name, not the printed front
* MC21 campaign pool review fixes: real hero names/art, correct citations, authored pool copy, finale grid, real played-and-folded seed
* Magic Muscle (hood 24070) now searches for a Brute minion when every Brute in play already has a tough card; giveStatus reports how many it gave (bind)
* MC21 Briefing: grants row and Handled for you no longer repeat pool reads; box-authored automated-setup summary
* Board: a Spell environment in a player's play area (Ebony Maw) shows a real subtitle instead of falling through to a raw type name
* MTS campaign: its side schemes and carried-forward cards now exist in a real game (Find the Norn Stones at Hela, Odin at Loki); was-defeated reads defeat events so a never-played scheme earns nothing; each player gets one Norn Stone
* Client campaign games now carry a campaign's composed encounter sets, so campaign cards (MTS's Norn Stones/Odin, GMW's Campaign Challenge) actually reach play
* MTS comic reader pans a panel that overflows the reading area instead of cropping it, and Ebony Maw's close-up panel on p1 no longer loses Thanos's head
* Table setup's Standard II/Expert II toggle fills the difficulty row's own spare third slot instead of a whole extra row, so The Hood's own modular-set section doesn't starve the encounter-deck preview on a standard 900px-tall desktop
* Tower Defense's second main scheme no longer shows a second time as a side scheme
* MTS comic reader always shows a full-bleed cinematic camera (no dimmed spotlight box) that smoothly pans between panels, and Next never blocks on a moving camera
* Every comic reader (GMW, TRORS, MTS) draws through a zoom-capped cinematic camera — no dimmed box, no letterboxed strip, no cropped figures or balloons, and MTS issue #2 gets a wide-viewport-only pull-back closing beat
* Phones: the game fills the browser down to its address bar; Android no longer leaves a dark strip above a bottom toolbar
* Tighten weak loose-bound test assertions in the Vision pack (Ultron Unleashed/Relentless Android drone count, Mass Increase stun) to exact values
* Fix the cinematic comic reader camera so a wide panel's own default pan reaches its balloon on narrow reading areas, closing the last TRORS red-skull crop bug
* GMW's expert-campaign HP restore (hp-set, the optional heal) now runs before Collector II's own When Revealed damage, per ruling June 2, 2026 (3) #2, instead of after where it silently erased that damage
* An alliance card's payment strip shows the other players' hand cards, tagged with their hero, so a helper's card can be picked and the approval bar can be reached
* Verify no MTS campaign scenario has a Collector-II-style setup damage-ordering bug, and pin why hpSet/healToFull must stay at the default window for Tower Defense
* Add real-content deck-validation coverage for MC21's campaign-only cards (Shawarma, System Shock, Norn Stone)
* Add 3-player and 4-player standard+expert smoke games for all mts scenarios and The Hood
* Replace structural-only tests for As One! and Stand Together (warm 23032/23034) with driven games proving the real damage math
* Prove Wrecker's (hood 24065) undefended +2 ATK bonus actually applies mid-attack, not just its baseline
* The villain-phase threat warning no longer says the scenario is lost when completing the main scheme's stage only advances it (Attack on Knowhere stage 1)
* Adam Warlock's board panel names all four of his aspects instead of only Aggression
* The Mad Titan's Shadow Aftermath screens show a comic panel instead of a placeholder note
* Permanent cards stay in play facedown too: Caught Off Guard and the Reality Stone can no longer discard Spectrum's energy forms or Vision's mass form, and Caught Off Guard surges when nothing else can be discarded
* Band Together in hand shows the [wild] resources it generates (one per ally you control, up to 3) instead of none
* Backing out of a card's action also takes its line off the game log; the end-turn confirm no longer repeats its question
### Engine
* Wave 4 schema: form keyword, Adam Warlock's copy limit, villain-owned main schemes, shared encounter decks, other faces, mode-only faces, set classification and decks, Loki and Hood scenario fields
* Additional forms: the form keyword, changing energy and mass forms, and form-change triggers that tell the identity flip from an additional form
* Villains that protect each other ("cannot be defeated while X has any hit points") fall together when both reach zero
* A main scheme stage's completion can be replaced ("When this stage would be completed, … instead")
* Damage on a non-character card (Avengers Tower) is covered, and a constant can exempt a title from the unique rule
* Two main schemes in play at once, each belonging to a villain, with Focused Defense naming the active villain and a shared encounter deck (Tower Defense)
* A modular set's own deck (the Infinity Stone deck) built at setup, and timing points when a player or scenario deck runs out
* Loki: a random starting villain, swapping a villain with a set-aside one of its title, advancing to one on defeat, Victory X on villains, and a scenario victory count
* Alliance: any player may help pay an alliance card's costs; multi-slot in-play cost picks and cost picks inside timing windows (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.17)
* An encounter ally attached to the main scheme: detaching it under a player's control, cannotHaveAttachments, and "If X leaves play, the players lose"
* Counters removed as an interruptible event ("after the last counter is removed") and Spell environments that enter the revealer's play area
* Counting the different aspects among cards (Adam Warlock's Karmic Blast, Cosmic Awareness, Regeneration Cycle)
* Valkyrie's kit: player set-aside destination and play from set aside, extensionOf, defeat attachment snapshot, attackInProgress, declareDefender, defenseUsesAtk, resolveAttackAgainst (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.22)
* Abilities that work while their card is in hand, and "You cannot choose to discard this card" (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.13)
* Set-aside modular sets and their random shuffle-in, mode-only faces by difficulty, Steady verified against The Hood (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.18)
* Player events shuffled into the encounter deck (Cosmic Entities) and effects that cannot be canceled (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.14)
* Readying as a costed act (readyCost rule) and cannot be readied by player card effects; end-of-phase ready waits for its questions (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.19)
* Flipping a card into a separately emitted face of another type (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.10)
* An ally treated as a minion by an attachment (Beguiled family; docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.9)
* A trigger on damage a card prevented: preventAllDamage rule, damagePrevented event, preventDamage bind (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.20)
* A character that ignores guard, patrol and the crisis icon; Evasive Maneuvering scripted (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.24)
* An enemy attack against a chosen character: retargetAttack, and enemyAttack targetCharacter in the DSL (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.21)
* An ability cost that discards a card you control (discardCards)
* Discarding cards you control as a cost; Lethal Weapon scripted (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.25)
* A friendly character attacks its own player; Old Rivals scripted, Nebula fully scripted (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.26)
* A cancel whose only target cannot be canceled is not offered (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.27, §4 Q16 user decision)
* A blank text box except for keywords (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.28)
* A minion treated as an ally: Mind Control, Redemption, Karma (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.29)
* A resource ability's own effects resolve when it is used in a payment; Gauntlet Gun places its ammo counter (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.30)
* A character that cannot defend; Intangible scripted (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.31)
* One thwart that ignores patrol; Just Passing Through scripted (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.32)
* A card with a given timing word in its text; Phase Disruption scripted (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.33)
* The attacking enemy from any trigger; Flow Like Water scripted (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.34)
* Discard a boost card instead of applying it; Defiance scripted (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.35)
* Event patterns accept several values; an X-cost triggered ability asks for payment; Machine Man scripted (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.36)
* A side scheme's defeat has an interrupt window; Chance Encounter scripted, Twisted Reality fixed (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.37)
* Resources generated by reading the table; Energy Duplication and Band Together scripted (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.38)
* A keyword granted until a duration ends; Pulsar Shield scripted (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.39)
* Scenario rules with no card behind them; Ebony Maw's Spell rule seeded, five refs scripted (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.40)
* A stat totalled over several cards; Mass Attack scripted (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.41)
* A deck-discard cost sized by the triggering event; Shield Spell scripted (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.42)
* A chooseOne or if branch's bindings reach the effects after it (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.43)
* Players cannot discard protected cards; Powerful Enchantments scripted (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.44)
* A revealed treachery that moved itself is not discarded again (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.45)
* Per-player snapshots, and each player resolving a Special as themself; Promised Prosperity completed (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.46)
* Product of values; Hela's side-scheme scaling, Odin's King side, and the hela scenario's expert villain (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.47)
* An advance to a set-aside villain no longer pre-empts the defeated card's own When Defeated (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.48)
* Shuffle a scenario deck's discard pile back in on demand; Infinite Mischief scripted (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.49)
* Fix a typecheck error in the Infinite Mischief test (docs/phase7-wave4.md §3.49)
* Add EffectSpec moveCards.assignOwnerTo so a shared, unowned campaign card can be granted straight into a player's deck mid-game (Shawarma, MC21)
* Standard II / Expert II as an optional setup replacement for the Standard / Expert set (difficultySets; The Hood insert p. 2)
* A nemesis set's only minion is its nemesis minion (nemesisMinionOf without the parenthetical; RRG 1.8 p. 30)
* enemyAttack.keywords: an attack an effect initiates carries its own overkill/piercing (fixes Avatar of Death, Calvin Zabo)
* increaseDamage: an interrupt raises an in-flight damage amount; hasStatus.active reads stunned/confused through steady
* A granted keyword's number can be read live (retaliate X, KeywordGrantSpec.value)
* repeatWhile ('repeat this effect'), and a card's own dealDamage now reports the defeat it caused to its bind
* resolveSpecials can resolve a card's When Revealed abilities (incite and surge included) and report how many
* RuleSpec gainsIcon: acceleration/hazard/crisis icons granted to cards in play count everywhere icons are read
* putIntoPlay.bind reports the cards that actually entered play
* A player's attack whose attacker has left play ends with no damage (Speed Demon defeating the attacking ally; wave 4 §4 Q20, user decision), and damage for a card no longer in play is not dealt
* Citywide Crisis re-resolves only printed When Revealed abilities on side schemes, not incite or surge (wave 4 §4 Q23, user decision); resolveSpecials.includeKeywords opts in
* Target validity (RRG 1.8 "Target", pp. 42-43): a thwart can no longer target the main scheme while you are patrolled, nor can threat removal under a crisis icon; an ability with no valid target is refused and not offered
* The Invocation deck resets the moment it empties, and a last card resolving its Special is left out of the new deck (ruling, Apr 30, 2026 (3) answer 7; phase7-wave1 Q9)
* Excess damage dealt now counts the same value overkill spills (RRG 1.8 Overkill p. 31, superseding rulings Jan 26 (3) and Feb 8 (2)): damage taken beyond remaining hit points, so Into the Fray, Moment of Triumph and Murdered You! read the reduced amount, and Radioactive Buildup places no threat when Thunderball's damage hits a tough, prevented or cannot-take-damage target
* A defeated ally or minion stays in play while its own When Defeated abilities resolve and leaves play afterwards, then any overkill spill is dealt (RRG 1.8 When Defeated Abilities p. 48, as side schemes already did); the defeat sweep no longer re-defeats a card waiting to leave
* An identity's title now reads whichever face is showing (RRG 1.8 Identity, p. 23), so an alter-ego no longer matches its hero's name; Captain Marvel's Alpha Flight Station is fixed to match
* A player ability with nothing to choose is not offered and is refused (RRG 1.8 "Choose (Game Element)", p. 12), and a printed "Then" waits on the text before it (p. 44); Quinjet no longer discards itself for nothing
* A confused hero may attempt a basic thwart against the main scheme under patrol or crisis (RRG 1.8 "Confuse, Confused", p. 13): the attempt discards the confused card and removes no threat
* Campaign grants now count toward the copy limit (campaign design Q8): Aspect Advantage tops a title up to its deck limit, offers only cards legal for the hero, and granted copies are exempt from the aspect restriction
* Tower Defense's suggested setup damage (MC21 p. 11) is an opt-in setup option, off by default, carried to the engine as general scenario setup instructions (wave 4 Q4)
### Content
* Wave 4 precon starter decks for Nebula, War Machine, Valkyrie and Vision, an Eros errata fix, and a nebu quantity correction

* Script Tower Defense: Proxima Midnight, Corvus Glaive, two paired main schemes, Focused Defense, Avengers Tower, and the modular set
* Tighten Tower Defense's tests to exact, real-mechanic assertions; fix a Black Order Besieger setup bug it surfaced
* Script the Thanos scenario (mts wave 4 box): villain, main scheme, own encounter set, and the Infinity Gauntlet modular set
* Emit Tower Defense's two villains as multi-stage cards, matching Ebony Maw's shape
* Back-fill modeOnly onto GMW's split Campaign Challenge side schemes, drop their placeholder ability refs

* Script the Loki scenario (mts 21160-21176) for the Mad Titan's Shadow box

* Script The Hood's Streets of Mayhem modular set
* Register MC21 (The Mad Titan's Shadow)'s CampaignDefinition and content record
* Script The Hood's Ransacked Armory modular set
* Script The Hood's Sinister Syndicate modular set
* Script The Hood's Wrecking Crew modular set
* Script the MTS campaign's a/b card pairs (Cosmo, Black Swan, Defensive Protocols, Norn Stone, Retrieve Odin's Armor, Jormungand) and its Captive allies
* Script The Hood's Standard II and Expert II sets
* Register Cosmo/Black Swan flip-controller sentences as engine-covered, and drive Security Breach, Summoned Back, Jormungand, and Cosmo's leave-play through real MTS campaign games instead of shape checks
* Split System Shock (mts 21185) into its two printed abilities and script them

* Script The Hood's remaining refs (Beast Mode, Mandrill, Out for Blood, Controller, Flamethrower, Crime Pays, White Rabbit, Feisty Heist, Citywide Crisis, Secret Lair); fix Jetpack/Tech Gauntlets/Holoshield fallbacks and Corruptor
