## [v0.7.0] - 2026-09-25
### Added
* Attack and Thwart ask which character goes when more than one can (hero and allies), showing each one's stat, consequential damage, and a stun or confuse that would cancel it, so you choose the order; the button shows how many can go and keeps the stun hatch
* Rhino one-off intro: before the deal, a standalone Rhino game opens on a comic artboard that pans to Spider-Man, then Rhino, then the whole scene, with captions and Spider-Man quips (narration when he isn't at the table); SKIP goes straight to the deal
### Changed
* Rhino intro: on tablets and desktops each quip's bubble sits beside its speaker over a quiet part of the art, with a tail pointing at them; phones keep bubbles under the art
### Fixed
* Tapping a hero, ally or other card in play opens it in Inspect instead of using its ability, so a stray tap no longer exhausts it; abilities are used from the card's Inspect buttons
* An exhausted ally that also took damage in the same attack (e.g. consequential damage) no longer slides off the bottom of the play area on phones
### Engine
* Pepper Potts copies the top of the discard pile as it stood before the payment, so a card spent from hand in the same payment no longer replaces it (FAQ Pepper Potts #33, RRG 1.8 p. 58); paying with Pepper on an Energy plus another card now works
### Docs
* Licensed under the GNU AGPL v3.0 or later (LICENSE, package.json and Cargo.toml license fields, README License section); Marvel Champions IP and third-party art stay with their owners

## [v0.6.0] - 2026-09-24
### Added
* Crossbones music: "Concrete Engagement" in battle, "Calculated Finality" when Crossbones wins, and "When the Siege Breaks" when the heroes win
### Fixed
* Release: the macOS build names its signing certificate in the log, fails with the reason when it is the wrong one, and installs Apple's Developer ID intermediate certificate
* Release: the manual Desktop build runs the test suite once, before its three builds, instead of again on every OS

## [v0.5.1] - 2026-09-24
### Fixed
* Release: the macOS build signs and notarizes the app once the Apple Developer ID secrets are set, so Gatekeeper opens it without a warning
* Release: the Android build fails with a clear error when a keystore is supplied without its password, instead of silently trying a default one
* Release: the Linux .rpm bundles in about a second instead of 18+ minutes (its payload is left uncompressed; the card art and audio in it are already compressed)
* Tests: the two-hero Breakout soak test no longer times out on slower CI runners (Windows)

## [v0.5.0] - 2026-09-24
### Added
* `pnpm screenshots` captures every client screen at desktop, tablet and phone sizes (portrait and landscape) into artifacts/app-screenshots
* Campaign client foundation: a campaign service that drives the MC10 runner loop and stores each run, the five-issue story copy for The Rise of Red Skull, shared comic chrome widgets, and route contracts for every campaign screen.
* Developer fixture that plays a Rise of Red Skull campaign to a known point (after issue #2, a lost issue #3, or the finale) and exposes it on the dev console for building and checking campaign screens.
* Campaign issue opener: tap-through comic panels for an issue's opening beats
* Campaign Briefing: composes the next issue, shows the automatic setup and decks, opens the game
* Campaign: The Run and Issue detail screens read back a run's issues and one finished issue's attempts and log writes
* Between-issue deck editing: the campaign screens can edit a seat's deck through the in-app deck builder, with the identity locked and campaign-granted cards pinned
* Campaign: The Saga shelf (C00b) shows every campaign box, its unlock/win status and issue progress
* Campaign in-game beat: a villain-stage flip shown as a comic splash over the Board
* Campaign: the Cover page (C01) reads the box's own pitch, roster and next issue
* Campaign: the Dossier screen (Overview/Log/Heroes/Issues) reads the printed campaign log sheet back for the player
* Script Ronan the Accuser's last two wave 3 gaps: Single-Minded Fury (16114) and Kree Combat Armor's Hero Action (16131)
* Script Star-Lord's last three wave 3 gaps: Sliding Shot (17005), Target Practice (17017), Agile Flight (17029) — stld fully scripted
* Script Drax's last two wave 3 gaps: Martyr (19012), Regroup (19032) — drax fully scripted except Moondragon (§4 Q12)
* Campaign: Sign the roster (C02) seats a fresh run with any legal deck via its own picker overlay
* Campaign: Aftermath (C05/C06) folds a finished game, walking win choices one seat at a time, and hands a loss straight to Rewind
* Campaign: Rewind (C09) shows what a lost issue kept and struck, with Rewind/Edit decks/Shelve
* Campaign: Finale (C11) closes a won run with its stats and the expert-campaign unlock
* Wire wave 3 (cycle 2, The Galaxy's Most Wanted) into the playable card pool and the client's own pool
* Let the player choose an either/or cost branch, an 'up to N' counter count, and opt into a play-cost reduction while paying
* Render an infinite hit-point villain face (the Collector) as the infinity symbol, not the literal word
* Show a scenario's own out-of-play area (The Collection), inspectable like a discard pile
* Add log lines for wave 3's new events, and the Patrol reason for a blocked threat removal
* Campaign story panels can show art from art/campaigns/<id>/artboards/; Red Skull issue #1 opens on the mountain base
* Red Skull issue #1: the heroes' drop-in panel has its art
* Red Skull campaign: story art for issues #2, #3 and #5, and the Aftermath panels of issues #1 and #4
* Art folders for the five Galaxy's Most Wanted scenarios, waiting for villain pictures
* Villain art for Brotherhood of Badoon and both Museum scenarios, The Collector's defeat scene, and a Badoon squad artboard for the GMW campaign
* New screens for MTS, SM, and GMW for comic book display in Campaign mode. 
* Split images of the artboards for MTS since they were wide screen
* Villain art for Nebula and Ronan the Accuser
* Dossier World box: GMW's shared log fields (bounty ladder, Collection, Power Stone control, evasion counters) get short player-facing labels; bountyLadderRungs reads the Badoon Headhunter ladder's rungs off the campaign definition
* Register GMW in CAMPAIGN_RECORDS (generically, from @mc/content's CAMPAIGNS list) so The Galaxy's Most Wanted can actually be started from the client
* Wire MC16's expert-campaign deck freeze: frozenNonCampaignCardsOf derives the frozen snapshot from the stored run's own scenario-1 history, and the deck-edit scene passes it into campaignDeckContextOf
* dev-fixtures: seedGmwRun reaches GMW campaign stops (afterIssue1, afterIssue2, expertAfterIssue1) for screen work without playing full games
* Read a campaign's own cover.<ext> (art/campaigns/<id>/cover.*) on the Cover screen, falling back to the final scenario's villain art for a box that hasn't shipped one
* Test that The Saga opens Vol. 2 (gmw) once Vol. 1 is won on Standard, against the real @mc/cards campaign registry
* Infiltrate the Museum has its own battle track, Rows of Silent Glass
* Escape the Museum has its own battle track, The Keeper's Iron Maze
* Nebula has its own battle track, Hardened Perimeter
* Brotherhood of Badoon has its own battle track, Under the Colossus
* Ronan the Accuser has its own battle track, Crown of Black Marble
* One Game Over track can cover a whole pack: The Unending Reign plays whenever the heroes lose in The Galaxy's Most Wanted
* The Longest Watch Ends plays whenever the heroes win a Galaxy's Most Wanted scenario
* Campaigns can have a Finale track; The Longest Watch Ends plays when The Galaxy's Most Wanted is won
* client: add The Galaxy's Most Wanted's Market screen, keyed off the pending choice's own currency shape
* Anthem of the Returning Sun plays on the Finale when The Rise of Red Skull is won
* client: GMW Dossier Overview gets Wallets and Bounty Ladder panels, detected by shape from the definition
* GMW Expert campaign's frozen-deck screen (design tile 20): a read-only deck summary with campaign cards and Market status, in place of the builder
* GMW Cover gets a THE MARKET button with each seat's wallet, alongside DOSSIER
* seedGmw gains a finished stop, a won run through all five issues
* Take your seats: sort the hero roster by wave or as one A-Z list
* GMW's campaign story is told through its six comic pages
* Campaign art catalog reads comic pages under art/campaigns/<id>/pages/
* Comic reader view model resolves an issue's beats across pages and steps them
* GMW comic-reader screen and issue opener wiring
* PDF Image extractor - it will extract PDF pages as PNGs. there's an associated MD file for usage instructions too. It's a python script. Run it with uv.
* GMW's Run screen shows each issue as its own comic page, with a READ chip that rereads it and sealed pages pixelated
* GMW's in-game stage-flip splash shows a fitting page panel instead of a plain villain portrait
* Official rulebook comic pages for all nine campaign boxes, in art/campaigns/<id>/rulebook/, kept out of the client build
* GMW's Aftermath (C05) shows the issue's own comic page, tapped through, with the fold's real log writes stamped as tags and a Market-aware CTA
* GMW's Rewind screen tears its photo from the issue's last-read comic panel, with its own campaign-lost ending for Ronan's Expert-only loss
* GMW's Finale reads its 06-finale comic page full-bleed, with box-specific stat boxes (Units banked, Headhunters) declared generically in its story
* MC10's rulebook pages loaded via pages/ for the guided comic reader, with each of its 8 pages mapped to an issue and panel
* MC10's comic reader is a guided view: zoomed on one panel, panning/zooming between panels in reading order, no reader lettering over the box's own printed pages
* dev-only path to reach the Aftermath through a real won game in the live store (seedGmwWon/seedDesignWon), and fix the Aftermath's tag stack overlapping a beat's own caption
* Progression: the Core Set is always open; each later wave unlocks by play (beat Rhino for Wave 1 and The Rise of Red Skull, the Red Skull campaign for The Galaxy's Most Wanted), and heroes' precons join one villain at a time (imported and built decks always play). Wins earn champion points. Unlock a precon, scenario or campaign with earned points right where it's locked (Seats, Scenario select, the Saga shelf) or in Settings ▸ Unlocks, after a warning; Unlock everything is free and switches points off; Settings ▸ Unlocks ▸ How it works explains it all in the game, and docs/progression.md in the repo; ?unlock=all opens everything for one session
* Extras: a Title menu screen of campaign comics, hero and villain files, artwork and a soundtrack jukebox, each opened by playing (a hero by finishing a game as them, a villain by facing it, an issue by reaching it in a campaign)
* Extras: a Rulebooks tab that reads the Rules Reference, FFG rulings since RRG 1.7 and every campaign rulebook as searchable plain text, with links to the original PDFs
### Changed
* Campaign: Saga rebuilt to the current design (ALL VOLUMES 3x3 grid, complete/open header, archive-not-delete footnote); Cover's title, Dossier/Issues style and tag tilt now match the tile
* Escape the Museum has its own Collector picture, so the two Museum scenarios no longer share one
* Take your seats groups heroes into one shelf per release wave (Core, Wave 1, The Rise of Red Skull, The Galaxy's Most Wanted) instead of a catch-all Hero packs shelf
### Fixed
* A campaign game now sets up once the campaign has added cards to a deck: each seat's deck is checked under its campaign's rules (its granted cards, the campaign's removals), so The Rise of Red Skull's second scenario no longer refuses the TECH upgrades the first one handed out.
* Groot and Rocket Raccoon's hero art waits in art/heroes/_pending until their pack (gmw) is imported, so main's hero-art test passes
* All missing hero art currently in the repo, whether it has associated content with it or not, it's there now. 
* Seats screen's back button now reads "Scenarios" at full size instead of the scenario name squeezed into tiny text
* The full-game villain AI tests get a 30 s timeout, so they stop failing when several test runs share a machine
* Villain art waiting in art/scenarios/_pending/ is ignored like hero art in art/heroes/_pending/, so main's art test passes again
* Maximum Velocity and "Bring It!" are now limited to once per phase, as printed
* Flora and Fauna and X-Gene's printed play restrictions (Max 1 per deck / Max 1 per player) are now enforced
* Releases work with main's pull-request rule: a merge with changelog fragments opens a release PR, and merging that PR tags and builds the release
* Campaign deck editing: the card pool no longer offers campaign-specific cards a player can't legally add, and the deck-size line splits pinned grants from the counted total
* Main scheme stages that print 'If this stage is completed, the players lose the game' now end the game instead of advancing (Kang's Arrival, Escape the Museum, …)
* A player deck now reshuffles (and deals its facedown encounter card) the moment it empties, for every draw, discard from the deck, search and mill, per the Apr 30, 2026 ruling; an event that empties its own deck is no longer shuffled into the new one.
* pnpm card reports wave 3 abilities as scripted; it only read Core, wave 1 and wave 2 before
* Campaign Briefing: HANDLED FOR YOU also lists in-game campaign-log reads and values held for a later issue
* Campaign issue opener: fixed a resize crash, and panel art now fills the whole panel
* Campaign in-game beat: the splash now matches the tile's scale and tilt
* Campaign: the roster's deck picker scrolls the full pool (wheel/drag/keyboard) and no longer loses a hovered row's text
* Game over's Continue the campaign hands a finished campaign game off to Aftermath
* goToScreen no longer silently ignores a screen change that arrives while the screen's own entrance fade is still running
* Waylay stuns and confuses Gamora, not whoever revealed it; Gamora's nemesis cards find her by her identity, never Drax's Gamora ally
* Red Skull's step-one main schemes (None Shall Pass, Hunting Down Heroes, The Mad Doctor) fire once after the villain phase's first step, not on every threat placed
* Campaign Dossier/Run/Issue detail: deck count, After-issue header, mode-gated fields, Heroes result boxes and deck-edit CTA, and overlapping stat text
* An Expert Campaign run now plays as one: the Expert Campaign modifier is stored where the campaign rules read it, so its expert-only steps (remaining hit points carried over, obligations, Red Skull's campaign loss) actually apply.
* Campaign Aftermath keeps its committed choices on screen, and Rewind/Finale now match the tile's centring, buttons and comic-grid margins
* Board: a Hero/Alter-Ego Action or resource ability printed on a main scheme, side scheme or environment (The Grand Collection, Library Labyrinth, the Milano's threat-removal abilities) can now be triggered by tapping the card
* Board log: a card put directly into a scenario area (The Grand Collection's own Setup) no longer misreports itself as redirected away from a discard pile
* Campaign Briefing: the speaker's portrait is round, as the design draws it, instead of a square
* Scenario select: a one-stage villain reads 'Stage I', not 'Stages I–I', and the two Collector scenarios name themselves
* Fix the ability DSL's query([], ...) always matching nothing, which silently broke GMW's Campaign Challenge reveal and Badoon Headhunter ladder shuffles
* Ronan setup in The Galaxy's Most Wanted deals one You Stand Accused! and reveals one Pincer Maneuver, not every copy; Zola (II) reveals one Test Subjects and Peter Quill's setup fetches one Element Gun
* GMW: the "main scheme is on stage 1B" Victory unit (MC16 p. 8, p. 14) now fires, read through a new mainSchemeStageNumber campaign query instead of card ids that don't exist
* Un-skip GMW's Collection hand-choice QA test: the '4 cards, hand unchanged' finding was measuring against the wrong baseline (the main scheme's own setup also seeds The Collection), not a real bug
* Give the order-cards/choose-bottom-cards prompts their own titles, and read the determined (not written) cost for discard choices, ability labels and cost-branch prompts
* Export GMW_CAMPAIGN_DEFINITION from @mc/cards' top-level index (it was only reachable via campaigns/index.ts)
* client: pluralize GMW's Unspent Units log line correctly (1 unit, not 1 units)
* GMW Market screen: let a seat build a whole cart across price tiers before checkout, instead of only ever offering the runner's current tier
* The Saga shows each volume's own cover art (Galaxy's Most Wanted showed Ronan)
* client: Dossier Heroes tab shows a Market card's ability line instead of its Unit Cost price tag
* Re-ingesting The Galaxy's Most Wanted keeps its hand-authored campaign record
* GMW comic reader panel rectangles remeasured from the source pages, fixing missed/overhung/blank-panel beats
* GMW Finale: size the copy band from its own content so no button clips, and speech-bubble Groot's line as I am Groot
* GMW Rewind's torn photo aligns art and paper as one tilted object, crops to the panel's own art window, and names what the log actually keeps
* Aftermath's log-tag stack summed every cumulative 'add' write instead of reading only the last one against its own baseline, inflating GMW's units tag (e.g. 11 instead of MC16 p. 15's real max of 6)
* MC10 rulebook panel rects for pages 006/008/009/014/016 remeasured to hug real borders, fixing split/overlapping/cut-off panels
* The Run, Issue detail and Dossier Log now read a campaign log's add-mode writes as this fold's own delta, not the cumulative running total the log actually stores
* Issue detail/Dossier Log: pluralize and sign a field's own delta consistently, drop zero-delta rows, and size rows from measured text height
* Dossier Log: collapse cumulative card-list writes to their own delta, name the seat, and stop the headline/citation collision on phone
* Compute campaign log deltas per step, not per history entry, so a setup spend never gets net against a victory award
* Issue detail and Dossier Log's write lists scroll inside their own box instead of spilling past the frame
* GMW's Dossier Log tab: In force now shows the box's own fields, and a Market purchase's grant no longer duplicates its card-list write
* Release: a changelog fragment with unescaped apostrophes no longer stops the release job from batching the changelog
### Engine
* Villain defeat is an interruptible event ("when the villain would be defeated … instead"), RuleSpec cannotBeDefeated, ∞ hit points, and a flip to or from an ∞ face resets the dial (The Collector, Hela, MaGog)
* Round timing points as trigger events: phaseBeginning, phaseEnding (the villain phase's end is the round's end) and villainStepResolved ("after resolving step one of the villain phase"), pushed only when an ability listens
* Cycle 2 keywords enforced: Hinder X and Uses with per player values, Victory X and the victory display, Patrol, the amplify icon, and stalwart (plus RuleSpec cannotHaveStatus) shedding statuses as it applies
* RuleSpec firstRevealGainsSurge and a per-round reveal history: "the first [card] revealed each round/phase gains surge" (Nebula, Mister Knife), read as the card is revealed
* EffectSpec defeat (a character defeated outright), addCounters upTo/bind ("to a maximum of X", local), ValueSpec min/max/dealtEncounterCount, playedThisRound without a card type, and an event its own ability moves is no longer discarded
* A tough status resolves before "would take damage" interrupts (status-card timing priority), and constant limits on damage taken: RuleSpec reduceDamageTaken and maxDamageTakenPerAttack
* The first player's card and First Player abilities: RuleSpec controlledByFirstPlayer (the Milano follows the first player token), firstPlayerOnly on actions/interrupts/responses, and resource abilities usable by any player
* Lasting "each time …" effects (eachTimeUntil), attacks that deal indirect damage (attacksDealIndirectDamage), an excess damage bonus (excessDamageBonus), and a variable cost of any resource type with a maximum
* Player elimination resolves a permanent attachment's "attach to" text and removes other permanent cards it does not own (RRG step 3); RuleSpec cannotBeUnattached (the Power Stone)
* EffectSpec cancelConsequentialDamage ("does not take consequential damage for this use", Cosmo); consequential damage events are marked
* Star-Lord's cost reduction: AbilityDefinition.playCostReduction used through playCard.costReductionAbilities, and AbilityCost.dealEncounterCards ("deal yourself 1 facedown encounter card →")
* The Collection: a scenario out-of-play area (ZoneId scenarioArea, createScenarioArea, a destination, selector and count) and RuleSpec discardFromPlayDestination redirecting cards discarded from play
* Gamora and Venom: IdentityDeckbuilding.offAspectAllowance ("up to 6 attack and/or thwart events from other aspects"), RuleSpec restrictedLimit, and Predicate playedThisTurn
* AbilityCost.spendCounters can now spend counters off a target other than the ability's own card
* excessDamageBonus is now exported for card-side tests to read directly
* Support putIntoPlay for ownerless scenario-specific supports, currentActivationIs predicate, boost-count adjustment during a card's own Boost ability
* Ability DSL/engine primitives for Gamora (threatCannotBeRemoved/cannotAttack player scoping, zone.bottommostOnly, TargetQuery.unique)
* discardFromPlayDestination gains thenPlaceThreat so a single Forced Interrupt can redirect a discard and place follow-up threat
* Cost modifiers now apply to a card played only at its own trigger window (payForCard), matching normal play pricing
* A basic defense's 'after you use a basic power' responses now wait for the attack to end (RRG 1.8 p. 16); new DSL builders eachTimeUntil and on.youDealDamage close the Lashing Vines, Deft Focus, Schadenfreude and Salvage gaps (docs/phase7-wave3.md §3.28–§3.31)
* Team-Up names as vocabulary: TargetQuery.titled and identitySetTitled read a card's own Team-Up keyword (DSL teamUpCharacters, ofTeamUpSet); the Team-Up play and deck checks now accept 'Hero/Alter-ego' names such as Heart of the Panther's (docs/phase7-wave3.md §3.34)
* mainSchemeStageNumber value, encounterSetAside move destination (for Escape the Museum)
* PlayerRef superlative ('the player engaged with the fewest minions', Drang III) and choosePlayer.among for its ties, which go to the first player on encounter cards (RRG 1.8 p. 19); DSL superlativePlayer (docs/phase7-wave3.md §3.35)
* Three cost primitives: 'remove up to N counters' sized by the player (spendCounters.upTo, bind), 'discard the top card of your deck' (discardFromDeck, with the empty-deck rules), and either/or costs (AbilityCost.either); plus AbilityLimit.per 'player' and optional Command.costSelection / LegalAction.costBranches / costCounters (docs/phase7-wave3.md §3.32, §3.33, §3.36)
* MainSchemeStage.completionLoses: completing a non-final stage that prints 'If this stage is completed, the players lose the game' now loses instead of advancing (Escape the Museum 1B/2B; Kang's Arrival 1B once re-emitted); first tests for indirect damage 'among players' (docs/phase7-wave3.md §3.37, §3.38)
* engine: resolveSpecials inherits the calling ability's controller, so a Special ability's own 'you' (an encounter card with no controller of its own) isn't null
* PlayerRef controllerOf and TargetQuery.hasAttachment: the player who controls a card, and a character with a matching attachment (Single-Minded Fury, Target Practice)
* divide 'up to' (Agile Flight) and a play restriction on any condition, constant.playOnlyIf, with ValueSpec victoryDisplayCount (Sliding Shot and every other 'Play only if' wording)
* AbilityCost.sameResourceType: 'Spend N resources of the same type' (Kree Combat Armor); a timing window's ability payment now gets the same resource checks as an action's
* An ally's consequential damage carries the results of the basic attack or thwart it follows (attack.*/thwart.*), for Martyr's 'if that attack defeated an enemy'
* A defeat's destination: setDefeatDestination (Regroup's 'return it to its owner's hand instead of discarding it'), RuleSpec defeatDestination generalizing Time Portal's rule, and characterDefeated.fromAttack
* An identity's own defeat now carries the same fromAttack/sourceInstanceId hint an ally's already did; fixes Judge, Jury, Executioner's overbroad 'defeated by an enemy attack' trigger (ron)
* A turn beginning opens an interrupt window, and an uncontrolled attachment's or obligation's "your" names its host's controller or its play area's player; scripts The Poison's Forced Interrupt (gmw 16125).
* New EffectSpec dealAsEncounterCard deals an already-identified card to a player as a facedown encounter card; scripts You Dare Oppose Me? (ron 90005), completing the Kree Fanatic set.
* One effect dealing damage to several characters deals it simultaneously, and a villain defeated at the same time as the last identity is a loss (FFG rulings May 18, 2023 and June 2, 2026; wave 3 §4 Q1).
* An enemy can attack another enemy (EffectSpec enemyAttacksEnemy, docs/phase7-wave3.md §3.23): an attack, not an activation, per the user's §4 Q12 decision. Moondragon (19013) is scripted, and drax is fully scripted.
* An effect's "up to N" now chooses at least 1 whenever a legal target exists, per the user's §4 Q16 decision: divide.upTo (Agile Flight), the new chooseTarget.upTo, and the "up to" chooseCards scripts. chooseTarget.optional now means only a printed "may".
* Close campaign-foundation gaps GMW needs: composeEncounterSets' set-aside pool, a unit-price collection filter, cardsInScenarioArea, and seatCount/divide/valueAtLeast arithmetic
* TargetQuery.printedId, for two cards that print the same title (MC16's Campaign Challenge side-scheme faces)
* CampaignGameQuery keywordValueSum and capAt, for a summed printed keyword value with a printed ceiling
* CampaignGameQuery atMost, the complement of atLeast
* Engine: reorderCards can place each card on the top or bottom of the encounter deck (chooseBottomCards, orderCards to encounterDeckBottom), and AbilityCost.conditional lets the board pick a cost branch (costIf, costAsDetermined)
* Card selection can take at most N matches (CardSelector atMost, DSL atMost/oneCopyOf) for 'search for one copy' instructions
* Campaign elimination policy: a player defeated in a won expert game skips that scenario's Victory steps (MC16 p. 5); GMW rejoins them at printed HP (ruling June 2, 2026 (3))
* An obligation drawn from a player deck (The Rise of Red Skull's expert campaign obligations) goes into that player's play area instead of their hand; refills to hand size keep drawing past it, and only that player can use its abilities.
* An obligation drawn at setup (opening hand or mulligan) goes into play and is not replaced by that draw; the mulligan draws up to the (possibly reduced) hand size (maintainer decision, docs/campaign-mode-design.md Q20).

## [v0.4.0] - 2026-09-22
### Added
* Campaign mode step 10: CampaignStorage/IdbCampaignStorage (a third IndexedDB database, mc-campaigns) and SaveMeta.campaignId/campaignNodeId (SAVE_SCHEMA 4, migrating a pre-campaign save rather than retiring it)
* Campaign mode step 11: the five client view models over campaign state (browser list, log sheet, between-games step/launch/fold, deck-edit gate, scenario-choice board), and SessionConfig.campaign wiring a composed campaign game through the existing host path
### Fixed
* Client: the game no longer freezes when a hero is defeated (or any HP/threat counter is mid-count during a redraw): the counter's animation is tied to what it draws, and one failing frame can no longer stop the game loop
### Engine
* Campaign mode C1: a composable mode set (PlayModes) replaces the standard/expert difficulty enum; existing saves replay byte-for-byte
* Campaign mode C1: the campaign definition and campaign log type vocabulary, plain serializable data, with a synthetic fixture campaign
* Campaign mode C1: in-game campaign primitives — log reads in card effects, recordInCampaignLog and removeFromCampaign (by card face, per ruling April 30, 2026), campaign input frozen into GameState, five campaign setup windows and trace events
* Campaign runner: resolveBetweenGames, startGameFromLog, campaignResultOf and applyCampaignResult, with seeded campaign randomness and replayable pending-choice re-entry
* Deck rules in campaign context: validateDeck takes a DeckContext, so a campaign-specific card is legal inside its own campaign, removed and prohibited cards are refused, the identity is locked, a frozen deck cannot be edited, and campaign grants are exempt from deck size
* Campaign vocabulary: filtered campaign-set choices, a tucked-cards record query, optional random draws, a choiceMade predicate (and the flag polarity fix), and deck legality for campaign-granted scenario cards and obligations
* Add ValueSpec deckCount (deckCountOf in the DSL): a player's deck size, so "the top half of their deck" can feed a zone selector's top
### Content
* Campaign mode C1: the Campaign content record (id, box/pack code, scenarios, campaign-specific sets, log-sheet reference), validateCampaign, and The Rise of Red Skull's (MC10) record — the only campaign box with scenario data ingested so far
* Campaign mode C1 step 7: The Rise of Red Skull (MC10) CampaignDefinition — five scenarios as a linear graph, the campaign registry, and the coverage/smoke tests; four printed instructions left as flagged vocabulary-gap TODOs
* Script 28 of MC10's 30 Hydra Campaign ability refs (TECH upgrades, both faces of the four Condition upgrades, Zola's Algorithm, and Medical Emergency); Martial Law and Anti-Hero Propaganda stay skipped pending a content-data ref split
* Split Martial Law (04165) and Anti-Hero Propaganda (04166) into constant/action ability refs via a generalized obligation-parser rule, and script all 30 of MC10's Hydra Campaign refs
* The Rise of Red Skull (MC10) rules-fidelity QA suite: full standard/expert campaigns, a real driven villain-defeat win, retry/removal/legality/determinism scenarios, each test cited to MC10, RRG, or a post-1.7 ruling
* Campaign mode step 12: MC21 acceptance gate passes content-only (test-only definition + one DSL parameter); one deck-size primitive gap flagged for game-rules-architect
* Campaign gate (MC21): 'discard the top half of your deck' rounds down, matching the printed MC21 card rather than the RRG modifier rule; flagged for FFG
### Docs
* Campaign mode foundation design (docs/campaign-mode-design.md): mechanism inventory across all ten campaign rulebooks, types, engine primitives, plan and open questions

## [v0.3.0] - 2026-09-22
### Added
* Client: Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+D copies a diagnostic snapshot of the running scenes, overlays and pending decision, for bug reports
### Changed
* Client: on desktop the Inspect card panel and its art grow with the window, and a long table's villain and scheme rows grow into a tall band so the cards read larger
* Client: phone Scenario select and Take your seats reclaim the top of the screen — the search field is behind a toggle at the head of a scrollable chip rail, and the active seat's details collapse into a disclosure
* Client: aspect filter chips wear the aspect's card-frame colour, matching the stamps on the hero cards
* Engine: the 'can't afford' refusal reads as a sentence ("Needs 1 resource; the payment covers 0.") instead of printing the requirement as JSON
* Client: aspect filter chips are full-colour aspect stamps, matching the stamps on the hero cards, and every filter chip and aspect stamp uses the Bangers comic face (Take your seats, Scenario select, Decks)
### Fixed
* Client: the pending-choice sheet comes back when the engine answers one decision and raises the next in the same command (targets, triggers, the villain phase); it no longer sits faded out until the game is resumed
* Client: the setup screens' search fields are visible again (they were hidden under the always-running music scene)
* Client: cards you may play from outside your hand (an Arrow on Hawkeye's Quiver, a card playable from your discard) appear at the end of the hand strip, tagged with where they are
* Client: an overlay that is closing no longer takes clicks, the villain-phase walkthrough takes none while the choice sheet owns a decision, and an answered choice sheet left on an unchanged decision gives it back after a moment instead of ignoring clicks until a reload
### Engine
* A Boost that makes the same villain activate again (The Wrecking Crew's I've Been Waiting For This!) no longer re-flips its own boost card forever; the villain phase used to freeze on that command
* Resources that only pay for one kind of card (Expert Marksman: a resource for an Arrow event) now count when the game decides whether you can afford that card
* Arrow events attached to Hawkeye's Quiver are offered as legal plays (the legal-move search looked only one attachment level deep)
